        <section class="panel" id="taxonomy" aria-labelledby="taxonomy-title">
          <div class="page-head compact-head">
            <div>
              <div class="eyebrow">Gobernanza del conocimiento</div>
              <h1 id="taxonomy-title">Taxonomía y lenguaje de UX Intelligence.</h1>
              <p>Define cómo clasificamos el producto y qué vocabulario utilizamos para que el conocimiento sea consistente, comparable y recuperable.</p>
              <div class="taxonomy-view-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Vistas de taxonomía">
                <button class="taxonomy-view-tab active" type="button" role="tab" aria-selected="true" data-taxonomy-view="product">Estructura de producto</button>
                <button class="taxonomy-view-tab" type="button" role="tab" aria-selected="false" data-taxonomy-view="vocabularies">Vocabularios</button>
                <button class="taxonomy-view-tab" type="button" role="tab" aria-selected="false" data-taxonomy-view="glossary">Glosario UX Intel</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <section id="taxonomy-view-product" class="ux-taxonomy-view" data-taxonomy-panel="product">
            <div class="ux-taxonomy-canonical-path" aria-label="Jerarquía canónica">
              <span>Producto</span><i aria-hidden="true">→</i>
              <span>Funnel</span><i aria-hidden="true">→</i>
              <span>Minifunnel</span><i aria-hidden="true">→</i>
              <span>Componente</span>
            </div>
            <div class="taxonomy-layout">
              <nav class="taxonomy-tree" id="taxonomy-tree" aria-label="Árbol de taxonomía"></nav>
              <article class="taxonomy-detail" id="taxonomy-detail"></article>
            </div>
          </section>

          <section id="taxonomy-view-vocabularies" class="ux-taxonomy-view" data-taxonomy-panel="vocabularies" hidden>
            <div class="ux-taxonomy-view-intro">
              <div>
                <h2>Vocabularios controlados</h2>
                <p>Valores gobernados para clasificar el conocimiento con criterios consistentes y evitar variantes o duplicidades.</p>
              </div>
              <button class="secondary-button" id="taxonomy-vocabulary-create" type="button">+ Crear término</button>
            </div>
            <section class="taxonomy-controlled" id="taxonomy-controlled" aria-label="Vocabularios controlados"></section>

            <section class="ux-impact-criteria" aria-labelledby="ux-impact-criteria-title">
              <div class="ux-impact-criteria-head">
                <div>
                  <span class="eyebrow">Criterio de clasificación</span>
                  <h3 id="ux-impact-criteria-title">Cómo medimos Impacto UX</h3>
                  <p>Impacto UX mide la consecuencia sobre la experiencia del usuario en su contexto real. No equivale a volumen de tráfico, importancia estratégica ni potencial CRO.</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div class="ux-impact-criteria-grid">
                <article>
                  <span class="status-badge impact-high">Alto</span>
                  <p>Puede comprometer una tarea o decisión importante: abandono, error, coste inesperado, pérdida de un beneficio, asociación incorrecta de datos o dificultad seria para acceder a una tarea crítica. También aplica cuando se oculta información clave para valorar una compra o un beneficio personal relevante.</p>
                </article>
                <article>
                  <span class="status-badge impact-medium">Medio</span>
                  <p>Genera fricción, dudas o esfuerzo relevante, pero el usuario puede continuar o recuperarse. Incluye mejoras de claridad, jerarquía, encontrabilidad o control y oportunidades que mejoran una experiencia que ya funciona razonablemente bien.</p>
                </article>
                <article>
                  <span class="status-badge impact-low">Bajo</span>
                  <p>Aporta contexto, apoyo o un refinamiento menor. La ausencia de la mejora tiene poca consecuencia sobre la tarea y no cambia de forma relevante la comprensión, el control o la capacidad de completar el journey.</p>
                </article>
              </div>

              <div class="ux-impact-context-rules">
                <h4>Cómo lo aplicamos en Air Europa</h4>
                <ul>
                  <li><strong>Buscador:</strong> hoy funciona bien. Los refinamientos visuales o de UI son normalmente Medio o Bajo; suben a Alto solo cuando hay evidencia de abandono, error o dificultad para completar la búsqueda.</li>
                  <li><strong>Header:</strong> no medimos éxito por interacción. El impacto aumenta cuando afecta a la encontrabilidad de autogestión o de tareas críticas como Check-in o Gestionar reserva.</li>
                  <li><strong>SUMA / Fare Families:</strong> la ausencia de información sobre beneficios puede tener impacto Alto cuando dificulta entender el valor, comparar opciones o tomar una decisión de compra informada.</li>
                  <li><strong>CRO:</strong> alcance, tráfico y potencial de conversión se valoran aparte. Un hallazgo puede tener Impacto UX Medio y, aun así, ser una oportunidad CRO muy relevante por su volumen.</li>
                </ul>
              </div>
            </section>
          </section>

          <section class="taxonomy-glossary ux-taxonomy-view" id="taxonomy-view-glossary" data-taxonomy-panel="glossary" hidden>
            <div class="ux-taxonomy-view-intro">
              <div>
                <h2>Glosario UX Intel</h2>
                <p>Definiciones que delimitan cómo interpretamos hechos, evidencias, hipótesis, inferencias, oportunidades y learnings consolidados.</p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div id="taxonomy-glossary"></div>
          </section>
        </section>

    // Master product taxonomy: approved contexts remain selectable even with zero records.
    const taxonomy = {
      Home:{
        Home:{Header:["Acceso Check-in","Accesos de autogestión"],Buscador:["CTA Buscar vuelos","Calendario","Pestañas de producto","Última búsqueda"],"Banner promocional":[],Avisos:[]}
      },
      Booking:{
        Disponibilidad:{"Fare Families":[]},
        "Shopping Cart":{}, Pasajeros:{}, Extras:{}, Pago:{}
      },
      "Check-in":{},
      MMB:{}
    };
    // Current navigation follows the canonical taxonomy; the historic chart remains separate.
    const homeOverviewTaxonomy = {
      Home:{Home:{}},
      Booking:{Disponibilidad:{},"Shopping Cart":{},Pasajeros:{},Extras:{},Pago:{}},
      "Check-in":{},
      MMB:{}
    };
    const homeCoverageProducts=["Booking","Check-in","MMB"];
    function taxonomyTermIsGoverned(term){
      if(!term)return false;
      if(['screen','element'].includes(String(term.level||'').trim()))return false;
      const status=normalizeText(term.status||'canonical');
      if(!['canonical','canonico'].includes(status))return false;
      if(term.governed===true)return true;
      const origin=String(term.origin||'').trim();
      return !/^excel revisado\s*·/i.test(origin);
    }
    function governedTaxonomyTerms(level=''){
      try{
        const terms=Array.isArray(taxonomyProject?.state?.terms)?taxonomyProject.state.terms:[];
        return terms.filter(term=>taxonomyTermIsGoverned(term)&&(!level||term.level===level));
      }catch(_){return [];}
    }
    function governedTermName(term){return String(term?.canonicalName||term?.name||term?.label||'').trim();}
    function governedTermAliases(term){return Array.isArray(term?.aliases)?term.aliases.map(value=>String(value||'').trim()).filter(Boolean):[];}
    function governedTermMatches(term,value){
      const target=normalizeText(value||'');if(!target)return false;
      return [governedTermName(term),...governedTermAliases(term)].some(candidate=>normalizeText(candidate)===target);
    }
    function governedTermContext(term){
      if(!term)return {};
      const terms=governedTaxonomyTerms();
      const byId=new Map(terms.map(item=>[String(item.id||''),item]).filter(([id])=>id));
      const chain=[];const seen=new Set();let current=term;
      while(current){
        const id=String(current.id||'');
        if(id&&seen.has(id))break;
        if(id)seen.add(id);
        chain.unshift(current);
        const pid=String(current.parentId||'');
        current=pid?byId.get(pid):null;
      }
      const context={};
      chain.forEach(node=>{
        const level=String(node.level||'');
        const name=governedTermName(node);
        if(name&&['product','funnel','minifunnel','component'].includes(level))context[level]=name;
      });
      if(term.transversal===true){
        Object.keys(context).forEach(level=>{if(level!==term.level)delete context[level];});
      }
      return context;
    }
    function isLearningCatalogItem(item){
      const id=String(item?.id||'').trim(),status=normalizeText(item?.status||item?.type||'');
      return /^L(?:-|$)/.test(id)||status==='learning consolidado';
    }
    function governedTaxonomyRecord(item){
      const source=item||{};
      // Si la taxonomía gobernada aún no ha cargado, mostramos la clasificación
      // existente. El canon normaliza cuando está disponible, pero nunca vacía
      // metadata válida por una carrera de inicialización.
      if(!governedTaxonomyTerms().length){
        const raw={
          product:String(source.product||'').trim(),
          funnel:String(source.funnel||'').trim(),
          minifunnel:String(source.minifunnel||'').trim(),
          component:String(source.component||'').trim()
        };
        if(normalizeText(raw.product)==='home'){raw.funnel='';raw.minifunnel='';}
        if(normalizeText(raw.funnel)!=='extras')raw.minifunnel='';
        return {...source,...raw};
      }
      const resolved={product:'',funnel:'',minifunnel:'',component:''};

      const candidates=(level,value)=>governedTaxonomyTerms(level).filter(term=>governedTermMatches(term,value));
      const compatible=(term,expected={})=>{
        const context=governedTermContext(term);
        return Object.entries(expected).every(([level,value])=>
          !String(value||'').trim()||normalizeText(context[level]||'')===normalizeText(value)
        );
      };
      const match=(level,value,expected={})=>{
        if(!String(value||'').trim())return null;
        const all=candidates(level,value);
        if(!all.length)return null;
        const scoped=all.filter(term=>compatible(term,expected));
        if(scoped.length)return scoped[0];
        return all.length===1?all[0]:null;
      };
      const applyContext=term=>{
        const context=governedTermContext(term);
        Object.entries(context).forEach(([key,value])=>{
          if(['product','funnel','minifunnel','component'].includes(key)&&!resolved[key])resolved[key]=value;
        });
      };

      const productTerm=match('product',source.product);
      if(productTerm)applyContext(productTerm);

      const funnelTerm=match('funnel',source.funnel,{product:resolved.product||source.product});
      if(funnelTerm)applyContext(funnelTerm);

      if(normalizeText(resolved.funnel||source.funnel)==='extras'){
        const miniTerm=match('minifunnel',source.minifunnel,{
          product:resolved.product||source.product,
          funnel:resolved.funnel||source.funnel
        });
        if(miniTerm)applyContext(miniTerm);
      }

      const rawComponents=isLearningCatalogItem(source)
        ? [source.component].filter(Boolean)
        : [...(Array.isArray(source.components)?source.components:[]),source.component].filter(Boolean);

      for(const value of rawComponents){
        const componentTerm=match('component',value,{
          product:resolved.product||source.product,
          funnel:resolved.funnel||source.funnel,
          minifunnel:resolved.minifunnel||source.minifunnel
        });
        if(componentTerm){applyContext(componentTerm);break;}
      }

      if(!resolved.product&&source.component){
        const unique=candidates('component',source.component);
        if(unique.length===1)applyContext(unique[0]);
      }

      if(normalizeText(resolved.product)==='home'){
        resolved.funnel='';
        resolved.minifunnel='';
      }
      if(normalizeText(resolved.funnel)!=='extras')resolved.minifunnel='';

      const legacy={};
      for(const level of ['product','funnel','minifunnel','component']){
        const raw=String(source[level]||'').trim();
        const canonical=String(resolved[level]||'').trim();
        if(raw&&normalizeText(raw)!==normalizeText(canonical)){
          const key='legacy'+level.charAt(0).toUpperCase()+level.slice(1);
          legacy[key]=source[key]||raw;
        }
      }
      return {...source,...legacy,...resolved};
    }
    function governedComponentNames(item){
      const learning=isLearningCatalogItem(item);

      // Un learning consolidado representa un único componente vigente.
      // Si existe `component`, NO mezclamos `legacyComponent` ni antiguos `components[]`
      // en UI, filtros o rutas. Esos valores históricos pueden seguir existiendo
      // para trazabilidad, pero no forman parte de la clasificación actual.
      if(learning){
        const current=String(item?.component||'').trim();

        // "Beneficio de fidelización" es una etiqueta conceptual histórica,
        // no un componente canónico. En el learning transversal SUMA no debe
        // mostrarse ni participar en filtros.
        if(
          String(item?.id||'')==='L-SUMA-03' ||
          (
            normalizeText(item?.funnel||'')==='transversal' &&
            normalizeText(current)==='beneficio de fidelizacion'
          )
        ){
          return [];
        }

        if(current){
          const terms=governedTaxonomyTerms('component');
          const currentContext=governedTaxonomyRecord(item);
          const compatibleTerm=terms.find(candidate=>{
            if(!governedTermMatches(candidate,current))return false;
            const context=governedTermContext(candidate);
            const same=(a,b)=>!String(b||'').trim()||normalizeText(a||'')===normalizeText(b);
            return same(context.product,currentContext.product||item?.product)&&
              same(context.funnel,currentContext.funnel||item?.funnel)&&
              same(context.minifunnel,currentContext.minifunnel||item?.minifunnel);
          });

          // Regla estricta: cuando la taxonomía gobernada está disponible,
          // un learning solo puede mostrar un componente que exista en el canon
          // y sea compatible con su contexto. Nunca inventamos ni reutilizamos
          // una etiqueta histórica como clasificación actual.
          if(terms.length){
            return compatibleTerm?[governedTermName(compatibleTerm)||current]:[];
          }

          // Antes de que el canon termine de cargar, conservamos temporalmente
          // el valor actual para evitar parpadeos; se revalida al refrescar filtros/render.
          return [current];
        }

        // Solo si falta realmente el componente actual recuperamos un histórico,
        // priorizando un valor gobernado y sin devolver más de uno.
        const fallback=[item?.legacyComponent,...(Array.isArray(item?.components)?item.components:[])]
          .map(value=>String(value||'').trim()).filter(Boolean);
        const governed=fallback.find(value=>governedTaxonomyTerms('component').some(candidate=>governedTermMatches(candidate,value)));
        const chosen=governed||fallback[0]||'';
        if(!chosen)return [];
        const term=governedTaxonomyTerms('component').find(candidate=>governedTermMatches(candidate,chosen));
        return [governedTermName(term)||chosen].filter(Boolean);
      }

      const raw=[...(Array.isArray(item?.components)?item.components:[]),item?.component].filter(Boolean);
      const out=[];
      raw.forEach(value=>{
        const rawName=String(value||'').trim();
        if(!rawName)return;
        const term=governedTaxonomyTerms('component').find(candidate=>governedTermMatches(candidate,rawName));
        const name=governedTermName(term)||'';
        if(name&&!out.some(existing=>normalizeText(existing)===normalizeText(name)))out.push(name);
      });
      return out;
    }
    function governedTaxonomyDisplay(item,level){
      if(level==='component')return governedComponentNames(item).join(' · ');
      const governed=governedTaxonomyRecord(item)?.[level]||'';
      if(governed)return governed;
      // En learnings preservamos la metadata consolidada aunque la taxonomía aún
      // no haya resuelto ese término. No convierte el valor en canónico.
      if(isLearningCatalogItem(item))return String(item?.[level]||'').trim();
      return '';
    }
    globalThis.UXGovernedTaxonomy={
      normalizeRecord:governedTaxonomyRecord,
      values:level=>[...new Set(governedTaxonomyTerms(level).map(governedTermName).filter(Boolean))],
      componentNames:governedComponentNames,
      terms:governedTaxonomyTerms
    };
    function componentsOf(item){return governedComponentNames(item);}
    function canonicalComponentName(value){
      const raw=String(value||'').trim();
      if(!raw)return '';
      const term=governedTaxonomyTerms('component').find(candidate=>governedTermMatches(candidate,raw));
      return governedTermName(term)||'';
    }
    function learningComponentOptions(item){
      const product=String(item?.product||'').trim(),screen=String(item?.screen||'').trim();
      const isBroadScreen=/^(multipantalla|transversal|no especificad[oa])$/i.test(screen);
      const contexts=taxonomyContexts().filter(context=>String(context?.component||'').trim());
      const compatible=contexts.filter(context=>
        (!product||normalizeText(context.product||'')===normalizeText(product))&&
        (isBroadScreen||!screen||normalizeText(context.screen||'')===normalizeText(screen))
      );
      const source=compatible.length?compatible:contexts;
      const names=[...new Set(source.map(context=>String(context.component||'').trim()).filter(Boolean))]
        .filter(name=>canonicalComponentName(name))
        .sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,'es'));
      return names;
    }
    function normalizeLearningSingleComponentState(){
      let changed=false;
      learnings.forEach(item=>{
        const legacy=Array.isArray(item.components)?item.components.map(value=>String(value||'').trim()).filter(Boolean):[];
        const current=String(item.component||'').trim();
        let primary=current;
        if(!primary){
          primary=legacy.find(value=>canonicalComponentName(value))||legacy[0]||'';
          if(primary){item.component=primary;changed=true;}
        }
        const secondary=legacy.filter(value=>normalizeText(value)!==normalizeText(primary));
        if(secondary.length){
          mergeUnique(item,'applications',secondary);
          changed=true;
        }
        if(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(item,'components')){
          delete item.components;
          changed=true;
        }
      });
      return changed;
    }
    function repairKnownLearningMetadata(){
      const defaults=new Map([...defaultBuscadorLearnings,...defaultHeaderLearnings,...defaultSumaLearnings].map(item=>[String(item.id||''),item]));
      let changed=false;
      learnings.forEach(item=>{
        // 1) Recuperación genérica desde trazabilidad previa: nunca inferimos.
        const history=Array.isArray(item?.classificationHistory)?item.classificationHistory:[];
        const lastHistoryValue=field=>{
          for(let i=history.length-1;i>=0;i--){
            const entry=history[i];
            if(String(entry?.field||'')!==field)continue;
            const value=String(entry?.to||'').trim();
            if(value)return value;
          }
          return '';
        };
        const legacyByField={
          product:String(item?.legacyProduct||'').trim(),
          screen:String(item?.legacyScreen||'').trim(),
          component:String(item?.legacyComponent||'').trim(),
          device:String(item?.legacyDevice||'').trim(),
          market:String(item?.legacyMarket||'').trim(),
          impactUx:String(item?.legacyImpactUx||'').trim()
        };
        for(const field of ['product','screen','component','device','market','impactUx']){
          if(String(item?.[field]??'').trim())continue;
          const traced=lastHistoryValue(field)||legacyByField[field];
          if(traced){item[field]=traced;changed=true;}
        }

        // 2) Para los learnings base, la definición consolidada es una fuente de
        // recuperación segura. Solo cubre ausencias reales y nunca sobreescribe
        // una decisión posterior ni "No especificado".
        const seed=defaults.get(String(item?.id||''));
        if(!seed)return;
        for(const field of ['product','screen','component','device','market','impactUx']){
          const current=String(item?.[field]??'').trim();
          const fallback=String(seed?.[field]??'').trim();
          if(!current&&fallback){item[field]=fallback;changed=true;}
        }
      });
      return changed;
    }

    function taxonomyContexts(){
      const levels=['product','funnel','minifunnel','component'];
      const clean=value=>String(value??'').trim();
      const termId=term=>clean(term?.id||term?.termId||term?.key);
      const parentId=term=>clean(term?.parentId||term?.parentTermId||term?.parent?.id||term?.parent);
      const termName=term=>clean(term?.canonicalName||term?.name||term?.label||term?.value);
      const status=term=>normalizeText(term?.status||'canonical');
      const isCanonical=term=>['canonical','canonico'].includes(status(term));

      try{
        const terms=Array.isArray(taxonomyProject?.state?.terms)
          ? taxonomyProject.state.terms.filter(term=>isCanonical(term)&&taxonomyTermIsGoverned(term))
          : [];
        if(terms.length){
          const byId=new Map(terms.map(term=>[termId(term),term]).filter(([id])=>id));
          const contexts=new Map();
          terms.forEach(term=>{
            const chain=[];
            const seen=new Set();
            let current=term;
            while(current){
              const id=termId(current);
              if(id&&seen.has(id))break;
              if(id)seen.add(id);
              chain.unshift(current);
              const pid=parentId(current);
              current=pid?byId.get(pid):null;
            }
            const context={};
            chain.forEach(node=>{
              const level=clean(node?.level);
              const name=termName(node);
              if(levels.includes(level)&&name)context[level]=name;
            });
            if(!Object.keys(context).length)return;
            const key=levels.map(level=>normalizeText(context[level]||'')).join('|');
            contexts.set(key,context);
          });
          if(contexts.size)return [...contexts.values()];
        }
      }catch(_){}

      // Sin taxonomía gobernada cargada no inferimos estructura desde datos históricos.
      // El canon es fuente de verdad; los registros nunca crean términos por sí solos.
      return [];
    }

    const evidenceMeta = {

      "E-14":{exclude:true}
    };
    const evidenceItems = evidence.filter(item=>!evidenceMeta[item.id]?.exclude).map(item=>({...item,...evidenceMeta[item.id],evidenceDate:'',ingestedAt:''}));

    const experiments = [
      {id:"AB-01",title:"Nueva arquitectura del Header · mobile",product:"Booking",screen:"Home",component:"Header",element:"Accesos de autogestión",device:"Mobile",duration:"13 días",outcome:"Positivo",pro:"No implementado",hypothesis:"La nueva estructura facilitaría las tareas principales de navegación en mobile sin introducir fricción sobre Buscar vuelos.",result:"Mejora el acceso a tareas de autogestión y Buscar vuelos se mantiene estable (-0,03%).",metrics:[{label:"Check-in",value:"+15,28%"},{label:"Mi Cuenta",value:"+12,77%"},{label:"Gestionar reserva",value:"+198,80%"},{label:"Buscar vuelos",value:"−0,03%"}],interpretation:"La hipótesis se considera validada en mobile: las tareas operativas ganan visibilidad sin una pérdida relevante en la acción principal. Los tres accesos de autoservicio alcanzan un 100% de probabilidad de superar al control según el informe; Buscar obtiene un 48,97%.",recommendation:"Mantener la priorización directa de Check-in y las tareas principales, conservar una solución específica para mobile y validar de nuevo el conjunto antes de llevarlo a producción.",limits:["El incremento de Gestionar reserva es relativo frente al control y no informa del volumen absoluto.","La muestra y la instrumentación detallada no constan en la documentación disponible.","El resultado corresponde a mobile y no debe extenderse a desktop."],learningIds:["L-02","L-03"]},

    ];

    const DOCUMENT_ANALYSIS_TYPES=[
      'Auditoría UX','Test con usuarios','Análisis conductual','Análisis de datos',
      'Experimentación / CRO','Benchmark','Desk research','Voz del cliente / CSAT','Síntesis estratégica'
    ];
    const DOCUMENT_ANALYSIS_TYPE_MAP=new Map([
      ['auditoria ux','Auditoría UX'],['auditoría ux','Auditoría UX'],['analisis heuristico','Auditoría UX'],['análisis heurístico','Auditoría UX'],['auditoria heuristica','Auditoría UX'],['auditoría heurística','Auditoría UX'],
      ['ux research','Test con usuarios'],['test de usuarios','Test con usuarios'],['test con usuarios','Test con usuarios'],['research interno','Test con usuarios'],
      ['analisis conductual','Análisis conductual'],['análisis conductual','Análisis conductual'],['quantum metric','Análisis conductual'],['qm','Análisis conductual'],
      ['analisis de datos','Análisis de datos'],['análisis de datos','Análisis de datos'],['data','Análisis de datos'],['datos','Análisis de datos'],
      ['experimentacion / cro','Experimentación / CRO'],['experimentación / cro','Experimentación / CRO'],['cro','Experimentación / CRO'],['a/b test','Experimentación / CRO'],['test a/b','Experimentación / CRO'],['ab test','Experimentación / CRO'],
      ['benchmark','Benchmark'],['desk research','Desk research'],
      ['voz del cliente / csat','Voz del cliente / CSAT'],['csat','Voz del cliente / CSAT'],['voz del cliente','Voz del cliente / CSAT'],
      ['sintesis estrategica','Síntesis estratégica'],['síntesis estratégica','Síntesis estratégica'],['analisis estrategico','Síntesis estratégica'],['análisis estratégico','Síntesis estratégica']
    ]);
    function normalizeDocumentAnalysisType(value){
      const raw=String(value||'').trim();if(!raw)return '';
      const exact=DOCUMENT_ANALYSIS_TYPES.find(item=>normalizeText(item)===normalizeText(raw));
      if(exact)return exact;
      return DOCUMENT_ANALYSIS_TYPE_MAP.get(normalizeText(raw))||'';
    }
    const DOCUMENT_ANALYSIS_EXCEL_CLASSIFICATION_KEY='ae-ux-intel-document-analysis-excel-classification-v1';
    function documentAnalysisFilename(item){
      return String(item?.originalName||item?.insightRegister?.file?.name||item?.fileMeta?.name||item?.name||'').trim();
    }
    function isExcelAnalysisDocument(item){
      const filename=normalizeText(documentAnalysisFilename(item));
      return /\.(xlsx?|csv)$/.test(filename)||Boolean(item?.insightRegister?.rows?.length);
    }
    function inferAnalysisTypeFromDocumentName(item){
      const name=normalizeText([documentAnalysisFilename(item),item?.name,item?.initiative].filter(Boolean).join(' '));
      if(!name)return '';
      if(name.includes('test a/b')||name.includes('a/b')||name.includes('ab test')||name.includes(' cro')||name.includes('experiment'))return 'Experimentación / CRO';
      if(name.includes('test de usuarios')||name.includes('test con usuarios')||name.includes('ux research')||name.includes('research usuario'))return 'Test con usuarios';
      if(name.includes('benchmark'))return 'Benchmark';
      if(name.includes('desk research'))return 'Desk research';
      if(name.includes('quantum metric')||name.includes('datos qm')||name.includes('analisis qm')||name.includes(' qm ')||name.endsWith(' qm')||name.includes('conductual'))return 'Análisis conductual';
      if(name.includes('csat')||name.includes('voz del cliente')||name.includes('nps'))return 'Voz del cliente / CSAT';
      if(name.includes('auditoria')||name.includes('auditoría')||name.includes('heuristic')||name.includes('heuríst'))return 'Auditoría UX';
      if(name.includes('datos internos')||name.includes('inputs y datos')||name.includes('recogida de inputs')||name.includes('analisis de datos')||name.includes('análisis de datos'))return 'Análisis de datos';
      if(name.includes('sintesis estrateg')||name.includes('síntesis estratég')||name.includes('final insights')||name.includes('evolucion del above the fold')||name.includes('evolución del above the fold'))return 'Síntesis estratégica';
      return '';
    }
    function inferAnalysisTypeFromRegisterOrigins(item){
      const rows=Array.isArray(item?.insightRegister?.rows)?item.insightRegister.rows:[];
      if(!rows.length)return '';
      const scores=new Map(DOCUMENT_ANALYSIS_TYPES.map(value=>[value,0]));
      const add=(type,weight=1)=>{if(type)scores.set(type,(scores.get(type)||0)+weight);};
      rows.forEach(row=>{
        const origin=normalizeText(row?.origin||'');
        if(!origin)return;
        if(origin.includes('ux research')||origin.includes('test de usuarios')||origin.includes('test con usuarios')||origin.includes('research interno'))add('Test con usuarios');
        if(origin.includes('quantum metric')||/(^|[ ;|])qm($|[ ;|])/.test(origin))add('Análisis conductual');
        if(origin.includes('a/b test')||origin.includes('ab test')||origin.includes('test a/b')||/(^|[ ;|])cro($|[ ;|])/.test(origin))add('Experimentación / CRO');
        if(origin.includes('auditoria ux')||origin.includes('auditoría ux')||origin.includes('heuristica')||origin.includes('heurística'))add('Auditoría UX');
        if(origin.includes('benchmark'))add('Benchmark');
        if(origin.includes('desk research'))add('Desk research');
        if(origin==='data'||origin==='datos'||origin.includes('analisis de datos')||origin.includes('análisis de datos'))add('Análisis de datos');
        if(origin.includes('csat')||origin.includes('voz del cliente'))add('Voz del cliente / CSAT');
      });
      const ranked=[...scores.entries()].filter(([,score])=>score>0).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]);
      if(!ranked.length)return '';
      if(ranked.length===1)return ranked[0][0];
      const [first,second]=ranked;
      return first[1]>=second[1]*1.5?first[0]:'';
    }
    function inferExistingExcelAnalysisType(item){
      if(!isExcelAnalysisDocument(item))return '';
      return inferAnalysisTypeFromDocumentName(item)||inferAnalysisTypeFromRegisterOrigins(item)||'';
    }
    function documentAnalysisType(item){
      const explicit=normalizeDocumentAnalysisType(item?.analysisType);if(explicit)return explicit;
      const candidates=[item?.documentProfile?.analysisType,item?.analysis?.metadata?.detectedType,item?.type];
      for(const candidate of candidates){const mapped=normalizeDocumentAnalysisType(candidate);if(mapped)return mapped;}
      return inferAnalysisTypeFromDocumentName(item)||inferAnalysisTypeFromRegisterOrigins(item)||'';
    }
    function classifyExistingExcelAnalysisTypes(){
      if(localStorage.getItem(DOCUMENT_ANALYSIS_EXCEL_CLASSIFICATION_KEY)==='1')return 0;
      let changed=0;
      sourceItems.forEach(item=>{
        if(!isExcelAnalysisDocument(item))return;
        const inferred=inferExistingExcelAnalysisType(item);
        if(!inferred)return;
        if(normalizeDocumentAnalysisType(item.analysisType)!==inferred){item.analysisType=inferred;changed++;}
        if(item.documentProfile&&item.documentProfile.analysisType!==inferred)item.documentProfile.analysisType=inferred;
      });
      try{localStorage.setItem(DOCUMENT_ANALYSIS_EXCEL_CLASSIFICATION_KEY,'1');}catch(_){}
      return changed;
    }
    function documentAnalysisTypeOptions(item){
      const current=documentAnalysisType(item);
      return `<option value="" ${current?'':'selected'}>Sin clasificar</option>`+DOCUMENT_ANALYSIS_TYPES.map(value=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(value)}" ${value===current?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(value)}</option>`).join('');
    }
    function setDocumentAnalysisTypeFilter(selected='Todos'){
      optionSet(document.getElementById('source-type'),'Todos',DOCUMENT_ANALYSIS_TYPES,selected);
    }
    const sourceType = item => {
      const name=normalizeText(item.name);
      if(name.includes('quantum')||name.includes(' qm')) return 'Quantum Metric';
      if(name.includes('benchmark')) return 'Benchmark';
      if(name.includes('desk research')) return 'Desk research';
      if(name.includes('test de usuarios')) return 'Test de usuarios';
      if(name.includes('test a/b')) return 'Test A/B';
      if(name.includes('auditoria')) return 'Auditoría UX';
      if(name.includes('conductual')) return 'Análisis conductual';
      if(name.includes('plan')) return 'Plan de trabajo';
      if(name.includes('inputs')) return 'Data';
      if(name.includes('evolucion')) return 'Decisión de diseño';
      return 'Análisis UX';
    };
    const sourceItems = sources.map(item=>{const type=sourceType(item);return {...item,product:"Booking",screen:"Home",component:item.area,element:"",initiative:item.area,type,analysisType:documentAnalysisType({...item,type}),catalogDate:item.date||'',exactDate:item.date||"No indicada",documentDate:temporalValue(item.date,'catálogo'),ingestedAt:'',status:'Disponible'};});

    // Visible document titles use sentence case while preserving real acronyms.
    // Header research phases also have canonical UX-facing titles so PDF and Excel siblings stay aligned.
    const DOCUMENT_TITLE_OVERRIDES=new Map([
      ['final insights designs','Final insights designs'],
      ['resultados test a/b desktop','Resultados test A/B desktop'],
      ['resultados test a/b mobile','Resultados test A/B mobile'],
      ['benchmark','Benchmark'],
      ['test con usuarios','Test con usuarios'],
      ['analisis y recogida de inputs','Análisis y recogida de inputs'],
      ['auditoria heuristica','Auditoría heurística'],
      ['desk research','Desk research']
    ]);
    function canonicalDocumentTitle(value){
      const raw=String(value||'').trim();if(!raw)return raw;
      const normalized=normalizeText(raw);
      if(DOCUMENT_TITLE_OVERRIDES.has(normalized))return DOCUMENT_TITLE_OVERRIDES.get(normalized);
      const letters=(raw.match(/[A-Za-zÁÉÍÓÚÜÑ]/g)||[]).join('');
      if(!letters||letters!==letters.toLocaleUpperCase('es-ES'))return raw;
      let next=raw.toLocaleLowerCase('es-ES');
      next=next.charAt(0).toLocaleUpperCase('es-ES')+next.slice(1);
      const replacements=[
        [/\ba\/b\b/gi,'A/B'],[/\bux\b/gi,'UX'],[/\bsuma\b/gi,'SUMA'],[/\bcro\b/gi,'CRO'],
        [/\bqm\b/gi,'QM'],[/\bpdf\b/gi,'PDF'],[/\bseo\b/gi,'SEO'],[/\bcsat\b/gi,'CSAT'],
        [/\bmmb\b/gi,'MMB'],[/\bcta\b/gi,'CTA'],[/\bui\b/gi,'UI'],[/\bab\b/gi,'AB'],[/\bexcel\b/gi,'Excel']
      ];
      replacements.forEach(([pattern,label])=>{next=next.replace(pattern,label);});
      return next;
    }
    function normalizeVisibleDocumentNames(){
      const renamed=new Map();
      sourceItems.forEach(item=>{
        const before=String(item?.name||'').trim(),after=canonicalDocumentTitle(before);
        if(before&&after!==before){item.name=after;renamed.set(before,after);if(item.documentProfile?.title===before)item.documentProfile.title=after;}
      });
      if(renamed.size){
        learnings.forEach(item=>{if(Array.isArray(item.sourceNames))item.sourceNames=item.sourceNames.map(name=>renamed.get(name)||name);});
        safeStorageWrite({project:false});
      }
      return renamed.size;
    }

    function insightRegisterDocumentDateFromFileName(fileName){
      const raw=String(fileName||'').replace(/\.[^.]+$/,'').trim();
      if(!raw)return null;
      const monthMatch=raw.match(/(?:^|[_\-\s])(ene(?:ro)?|feb(?:rero)?|mar(?:zo)?|abr(?:il)?|may(?:o)?|jun(?:io)?|jul(?:io|y)?|ago(?:sto)?|sep(?:t(?:iembre)?)?|oct(?:ubre)?|nov(?:iembre)?|dic(?:iembre)?|jan(?:uary)?|feb(?:ruary)?|mar(?:ch)?|apr(?:il)?|jun(?:e)?|jul(?:y)?|aug(?:ust)?|sep(?:t(?:ember)?)?|oct(?:ober)?|nov(?:ember)?|dec(?:ember)?)[_\-\s]?(20\d{2}|\d{2})(?=$|[_\-\s])/i);
      if(monthMatch){
        const monthAliases={jan:'ene',january:'ene',feb:'feb',february:'feb',mar:'mar',march:'mar',apr:'abr',april:'abr',may:'may',jun:'jun',june:'jun',jul:'jul',july:'jul',aug:'ago',august:'ago',sep:'sep',sept:'sep',september:'sep',oct:'oct',october:'oct',nov:'nov',november:'nov',dec:'dic',december:'dic'};
        const token=normalizeText(monthMatch[1]);
        const base=Object.keys(monthAliases).find(key=>token.startsWith(key))||token.slice(0,3);
        const month=monthAliases[base]||base;
        const year=String(monthMatch[2]).length===2?`20${monthMatch[2]}`:String(monthMatch[2]);
        return temporalValue(`${month} ${year}`,'excel-filename');
      }
      const yearMonth=raw.match(/(?:^|[_\-\s])(20\d{2})[_\-](0?[1-9]|1[0-2])(?=$|[_\-\s])/);
      if(yearMonth)return temporalValue(`${yearMonth[1]}-${String(yearMonth[2]).padStart(2,'0')}`,'excel-filename');
      const monthYear=raw.match(/(?:^|[_\-\s])(0?[1-9]|1[0-2])[_\-](20\d{2})(?=$|[_\-\s])/);
      if(monthYear)return temporalValue(`${String(monthYear[1]).padStart(2,'0')}/${monthYear[2]}`,'excel-filename');
      return null;
    }

    function shouldApplyInsightRegisterFileDate(item,inferred){
      if(!inferred)return false;
      const current=temporalValue(item?.documentDate||item?.exactDate);
      if(!current)return true;
      if(current.value===inferred.value&&current.precision===inferred.precision)return false;
      if(item?.documentDate?.source!=='manual')return true;
      // A previous metadata edit could accidentally convert the ingestion month into a
      // "manual" document date even when the user only edited taxonomy/scope. If the
      // current manual date is exactly the ingestion month and the filename carries an
      // explicit document date, prefer the explicit filename date once.
      const ingestion=temporalValue(item?.ingestedAt);
      if(!ingestion)return false;
      return String(current.value||'').slice(0,7)===String(ingestion.value||'').slice(0,7);
    }

    function repairReviewedInsightRegisterMetadata(){
      let changed=false;
      sourceItems.forEach(item=>{
        const register=item?.insightRegister;if(!register)return;
        if(register.reviewedByUx!==true){register.reviewedByUx=true;changed=true;}
        if(item.documentProfile&&item.documentProfile.reviewedByUx!==true){item.documentProfile.reviewedByUx=true;changed=true;}
        const inferred=insightRegisterDocumentDateFromFileName(register.file?.name||item.originalName||item.fileMeta?.name||'');
        if(shouldApplyInsightRegisterFileDate(item,inferred)){
          item.documentDate=inferred;
          item.exactDate=formatDateEs(inferred);
          if(item.documentProfile)item.documentProfile.date=item.exactDate;
          changed=true;
        }
      });
      if(changed)safeStorageWrite({project:false});
      return changed;
    }

    // Clean Excel-first workspace: legacy demo/seed knowledge stays in code/archive only, never in the active base.
    learnings.splice(0);
    evidenceItems.splice(0);
    experiments.splice(0);
    sourceItems.splice(0);

    const PILOT_SOURCE_ID='S-11';
    // New empty workspace. The previous browser state remains untouched under
    // ae-ux-intel-pilot-v1 and can be recovered together with the archived files.
    const PILOT_STORAGE_KEY='ae-ux-intel-excel-v3-clean-20260918';

    const WORKSPACE_SCHEMA_VERSION=3;

    // Declared before any bootstrap-time safeStorageWrite call to avoid temporal-dead-zone errors.

    function safeStorageRead(){try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(PILOT_STORAGE_KEY)||'{}');}catch{return {};}}
    function learningDecisionRowsKey(rows){
      return [...(rows||[])].map(value=>String(value)).sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,'es')).join('|');
    }
    function learningDecisionLogRead(){
      try{
        const parsed=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(LEARNING_DECISIONS_STORAGE_KEY)||'[]');
        return Array.isArray(parsed)?parsed:[];
      }catch{return [];}
    }
    function learningDecisionLogWrite(entries){
      try{localStorage.setItem(LEARNING_DECISIONS_STORAGE_KEY,JSON.stringify(entries||[]));}catch(_){}
    }
    function rememberLearningSuggestionDecision(item,candidate,status,learningId=''){
      if(!item?.id||!candidate)return;
      const rowsKey=learningDecisionRowsKey(candidate.rowRefs);
      const log=learningDecisionLogRead();
      const next={
        documentId:String(item.id),
        candidateId:String(candidate.id||''),
        fingerprint:String(candidate.fingerprint||''),
        rowsKey,
        rowRefs:[...(candidate.rowRefs||[])],
        status:String(status||candidate.status||''),
        action:String(candidate.action||''),
        learningId:String(learningId||candidate.appliedLearningId||candidate.targetLearningId||''),
        title:String(candidate.title||''),
        know:String(candidate.know||''),
        text:String(candidate.text||''),
        edited:Boolean(candidate.edited),
        savedAt:new Date().toISOString()
      };
      const index=log.findIndex(entry=>
        String(entry.documentId||'')===next.documentId&&
        ((next.rowsKey&&entry.rowsKey===next.rowsKey)||(next.fingerprint&&entry.fingerprint===next.fingerprint))
      );
      if(index>=0)log[index]={...log[index],...next};
      else log.push(next);
      learningDecisionLogWrite(log.slice(-500));
    }
    function seedLearningDecisionLogFromWorkspace(state){
      const log=learningDecisionLogRead();
      let changed=false;
      (state?.documents||[]).forEach(documentItem=>{
        const suggestions=documentItem?.insightRegister?.learningSuggestions;
        if(!suggestions)return;
        (suggestions.candidates||[]).forEach(candidate=>{
          if(!candidate?.status||candidate.status==='pending')return;
          const rowsKey=learningDecisionRowsKey(candidate.rowRefs);
          if(log.some(entry=>String(entry.documentId||'')===String(documentItem.id||'')&&((rowsKey&&entry.rowsKey===rowsKey)||(candidate.fingerprint&&entry.fingerprint===candidate.fingerprint))))return;
          log.push({
            documentId:String(documentItem.id||''),
            candidateId:String(candidate.id||''),
            fingerprint:String(candidate.fingerprint||''),
            rowsKey,
            rowRefs:[...(candidate.rowRefs||[])],
            status:String(candidate.status||''),
            action:String(candidate.action||''),
            learningId:String(candidate.appliedLearningId||candidate.targetLearningId||''),
            title:String(candidate.title||''),
            know:String(candidate.know||''),
            text:String(candidate.text||''),
            edited:Boolean(candidate.edited),
            savedAt:String(candidate.resolvedAt||new Date().toISOString())
          });
          changed=true;
        });
      });
      if(changed)learningDecisionLogWrite(log.slice(-500));
    }
    let storageWriteFailureToastQueued=false;
    function updateStorageWriteStatus(hasFailure,error){
      const indicator=document.getElementById('storage-save-status');
      if(indicator){
        indicator.hidden=!hasFailure;
        if(hasFailure){
          const detail='El último cambio no se ha podido guardar. No recargues la página hasta que el guardado vuelva a funcionar.';
          indicator.title=detail;
          indicator.setAttribute('aria-label',`Cambios sin guardar. ${detail}`);
        }else{
          indicator.removeAttribute('title');
          indicator.setAttribute('aria-label','Todos los cambios están guardados');
        }
      }
      if(!hasFailure){storageWriteFailureToastQueued=false;return;}
      if(storageWriteFailureToastQueued)return;
      storageWriteFailureToastQueued=true;
      queueMicrotask(()=>{
        storageWriteFailureToastQueued=false;
        const current=document.getElementById('storage-save-status');
        if(!current||current.hidden)return;
        if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Cambios sin guardar','El último cambio no se ha podido guardar. No recargues la página hasta que vuelva a guardarse correctamente.');
      });
    }

    const DUPLICATE_REVIEW_STORAGE_KEY='ux-intel-duplicate-review-v1';
    function duplicateReviewWorkspaceRead(){
      try{
        const value=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(DUPLICATE_REVIEW_STORAGE_KEY)||'{}');
        return value&&typeof value==='object'?value:{};
      }catch(_){return {};}
    }
    function buildWorkspaceState(){
      let taxonomyGovernance=null;
      try{taxonomyGovernance=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(TAXONOMY_GOVERNANCE_STORAGE_KEY)||'null');}catch(_){}
      return {
        workspaceInitialized:true,
        workspaceSchemaVersion:WORKSPACE_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        duplicateReviewState:duplicateReviewWorkspaceRead(),
        taxonomyGovernance,
        documents:sourceItems.map(item=>({
          id:item.id,name:item.name,originalName:item.originalName||'',product:item.product,funnel:item.funnel||'',minifunnel:item.minifunnel||'',screen:item.screen,component:item.component,components:item.components||[],documentScopeMode:item.documentScopeMode||'',
          initiative:item.initiative,initiatives:item.initiatives||[],zone:item.zone||"",scope:item.scope||"",market:item.market||"",type:item.type,exactDate:item.exactDate,documentDate:item.documentDate||null,ingestedAt:item.ingestedAt||'',status:item.status,fileMeta:item.fileMeta||null,
          simpleKnowledgeActive:Boolean(item.simpleKnowledgeActive),supportingDocument:Boolean(item.supportingDocument),
          documentProfile:item.documentProfile||null,insightRegister:item.insightRegister||null,exportDimensions:item.exportDimensions||null,
          hasAnalysis:Boolean(item.analysis)||Boolean(item.hasAnalysis),analysisSummary:item.analysis?{pages:item.analysis.pages||0,findings:item.analysis.findings?.length||0,relations:item.analysis.relations?.length||0,pagesRead:item.analysis.extraction?.pagesRead||0}:item.analysisSummary||null,
          analysisError:item.analysisError||'',analysisStack:item.analysisStack||'',analysisFailure:item.analysisFailure||null,processing:item.processing||null,
          sourceHistory:item.sourceHistory||[],sourceUpdateFailure:item.sourceUpdateFailure||null
        })),
        knowledgeUnits,
        openQuestionItems,
        learningState:learnings,
        patternState:patternItems
      };
    }
    function safeStorageWrite({project=true}={}){
      try{
        const state=buildWorkspaceState();
        localStorage.setItem(PILOT_STORAGE_KEY,JSON.stringify(state));
        localStorage.setItem(WORKSPACE_INIT_KEY,'1');
        if(project){
          sourceItems.filter(item=>item.analysis&&['Pendiente de revisión','Procesado'].includes(item.status)).forEach(item=>saveDocumentAnalysis(item.id,item.analysis).catch(error=>console.error('No se pudo guardar el análisis en IndexedDB',error)));
          queueProjectHistory();
        }
        updateStorageWriteStatus(false);
        return true;
      }catch(error){
        console.error('No se pudo guardar el estado local',error);
        updateStorageWriteStatus(true,error);
        return false;
      }
    }
    const persistedPilot=safeStorageRead();
    if(persistedPilot?.duplicateReviewState&&!localStorage.getItem(DUPLICATE_REVIEW_STORAGE_KEY)){
      try{localStorage.setItem(DUPLICATE_REVIEW_STORAGE_KEY,JSON.stringify(persistedPilot.duplicateReviewState));}catch(_){}
    }

    seedLearningDecisionLogFromWorkspace(persistedPilot);
    if(persistedPilot?.taxonomyGovernance&&!localStorage.getItem(TAXONOMY_GOVERNANCE_STORAGE_KEY)){
      try{localStorage.setItem(TAXONOMY_GOVERNANCE_STORAGE_KEY,JSON.stringify(persistedPilot.taxonomyGovernance));}catch(_){}
    }
    (persistedPilot.documents||[]).forEach(saved=>{
      const existing=sourceItems.find(item=>item.id===saved.id);
      if(existing)Object.assign(existing,saved);else sourceItems.unshift(saved);
    });
    const classifiedExistingExcelDocuments=classifyExistingExcelAnalysisTypes();
    if(classifiedExistingExcelDocuments){
      safeStorageWrite({project:false});
      console.info(`[UX Intel] ${classifiedExistingExcelDocuments} Excel clasificado${classifiedExistingExcelDocuments===1?'':'s'} por tipo de análisis.`);
    }
    knowledgeUnits.push(...(persistedPilot.knowledgeUnits||[]));
    openQuestionItems.push(...(persistedPilot.openQuestionItems||[]));
    if(Array.isArray(persistedPilot.learningState))learnings.splice(0,learnings.length,...persistedPilot.learningState);

    const LEARNING_COPY_REVIEW_20261022_KEY='ae-ux-intel-learning-copy-review-20261022-v1';

    function applyLearningCopyReview20261022(){

      let changed=false;
      learnings.forEach(item=>{
        const patch=LEARNING_COPY_REVIEW_20261022[item?.id];
        if(!patch)return;
        ['title','text','know'].forEach(key=>{if(patch[key]&&item[key]!==patch[key]){item[key]=patch[key];changed=true;}});
        if(Array.isArray(patch.open)&&JSON.stringify(item.open||[])!==JSON.stringify(patch.open)){item.open=[...patch.open];changed=true;}
        if(item.id==='L-SUMA-03'){
          const fact='Evidencia interna: con el usuario logado podemos identificar si un servicio gratuito viene de la tarifa, de SUMA o de otra condición. El dato que puede faltar es la cuantificación exacta en Millas.';
          if(!Array.isArray(item.forEvidence))item.forEvidence=[];
          if(!item.forEvidence.includes(fact)){item.forEvidence.push(fact);changed=true;}
        }
        item.updatedAt='2026-10-22';
      });

      const oldQuestion='No queda confirmado que podamos identificar técnicamente el motivo exacto por el que un servicio es gratuito: tarifa, SUMA u otra condición.';
      const oldReading='No asumir que podemos etiquetar automáticamente el origen del beneficio hasta confirmar qué dato devuelve Amadeus.';
      const newInsight='Si el usuario está logado, podemos identificar por qué un servicio es gratuito.';
      const newReading='Podemos explicar si el beneficio viene de la tarifa, de SUMA o de otra condición. Lo que no siempre conocemos es el valor exacto en Millas asociado al beneficio.';
      const newQuestion='¿Podemos disponer del valor exacto en Millas asociado al beneficio cuando sea necesario comunicarlo?';
      const patchRecord=record=>{
        if(!record||typeof record!=='object')return;
        const values=Object.values(record).filter(value=>typeof value==='string');
        const matches=values.some(value=>value.trim()===oldQuestion||value.trim()===oldReading||value.includes('motivo exacto por el que un servicio es gratuito'));
        if(!matches)return;
        if('recordType' in record)record.recordType='Hecho';
        if('type' in record&&normalizeText(record.type||'')==='pregunta abierta')record.type='Hecho';
        if('insight' in record)record.insight=newInsight;
        if('title' in record&&String(record.title||'').trim()===oldQuestion)record.title=newInsight;
        if('implication' in record)record.implication=newReading;
        if('uxImplication' in record)record.uxImplication=newReading;
        if('text' in record&&String(record.text||'').trim()===oldReading)record.text=newReading;
        if('openQuestion' in record)record.openQuestion=newQuestion;
        changed=true;
      };
      sourceItems.forEach(documentItem=>{
        (documentItem?.insightRegister?.rows||[]).forEach(patchRecord);
      });
      knowledgeUnits.forEach(patchRecord);
      openQuestionItems.forEach(patchRecord);
      if(changed)safeStorageWrite({project:false});
      try{localStorage.setItem(LEARNING_COPY_REVIEW_20261022_KEY,'1');}catch(_){}
      return changed;
    }

    function applyLearningNaturalCopy20261022V4(){
      if(localStorage.getItem(LEARNING_NATURAL_COPY_20261022_V4_KEY)==='1')return false;
      let changed=false;
      learnings.forEach(item=>{
        const patch=LEARNING_NATURAL_COPY_20261022_V4[item?.id];
        if(patch){
          ['title','know','text'].forEach(key=>{
            if(item[key]!==patch[key]){item[key]=patch[key];changed=true;}
          });
          item.updatedAt='2026-10-22';
          return;
        }
        const title=String(item?.title||'').trim();
        const component=String(item?.component||'').trim();

        if(
          title==='La identificación SUMA aporta valor cuando reduce esfuerzo y aprovecha información ya conocida' ||
          title==='Estar identificado debe ahorrar pasos y aprovechar datos ya conocidos'
        ){
          item.title='Si el usuario ya está identificado, aprovechemos los datos que ya tenemos';
          item.know='Si el usuario ya está logado en SUMA y conocemos sus datos o beneficios, volver a pedir la misma información añade esfuerzo.';
          item.text='Podemos recuperar información y aplicar ventajas disponibles, dejando claro qué se ha recuperado o aplicado.';
          item.updatedAt='2026-10-22';changed=true;return;
        }

        if(
          title==='El mensaje inicial no deja claro si las Millas mostradas son saldo total o máximo aplicable a la reserva' ||
          title==='No queda claro si las Millas son el saldo total o el máximo que se puede usar' ||
          component==='Slider de Millas'
        ){
          item.title='No queda claro qué representa la cifra de Millas';
          item.know='La cifra puede parecer el saldo total cuando en realidad puede ser el máximo aplicable a esa reserva.';
          item.text='Antes de usar el slider debe quedar claro qué significa y cuántas Millas se pueden aplicar.';
          item.updatedAt='2026-10-22';changed=true;
        }
      });
      if(changed)safeStorageWrite({project:false});
      try{localStorage.setItem(LEARNING_NATURAL_COPY_20261022_V4_KEY,'1');}catch(_){
      }
      return changed;
    }
    applyLearningNaturalCopy20261022V4();

    function mergeUnique(target,key,values){
      const current=Array.isArray(target[key])?target[key]:[];
      const seen=new Set(current.map(value=>normalizeText(value)));
      values.forEach(value=>{if(!seen.has(normalizeText(value))){current.push(value);seen.add(normalizeText(value));}});
      target[key]=current;
    }
    function applyHeaderLearningReinforcements(){
      let changed=false;
      Object.entries(headerLearningReinforcements).forEach(([id,patch])=>{
        const item=learnings.find(value=>value.id===id);if(!item)return;
        const before=JSON.stringify(item);
        mergeUnique(item,'initiatives',patch.initiatives||[]);
        mergeUnique(item,'forEvidence',patch.evidence||[]);
        mergeUnique(item,'contrary',patch.contrary||[]);
        mergeUnique(item,'noProof',patch.noProof||[]);
        mergeUnique(item,'applications',patch.applications||[]);
        mergeUnique(item,'sourceNames',patch.sources||[]);
        if(patch.know)item.know=patch.know;
        item.updatedAt='2026-09-19';
        if(JSON.stringify(item)!==before)changed=true;
      });
      return changed;
    }

    function bootstrapInitialWorkspace(){
      const alreadyInitialized=workspaceWasInitialized||persistedPilot.workspaceInitialized===true;
      if(alreadyInitialized)return false;
      const initialLearnings=[
        ...defaultBuscadorLearnings,
        ...defaultHeaderLearnings,
        ...defaultSumaLearnings
      ].map(item=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(item)));
      learnings.splice(0,learnings.length,...initialLearnings);
      applyHeaderLearningReinforcements();
      learnings.forEach(item=>{
        if(!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(item,'createdAt'))item.createdAt='';
        if(!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(item,'updatedAt'))item.updatedAt='';
      });
      safeStorageWrite({project:false});
      return true;
    }

      publicDemoRows.forEach(row=>{
        let date='';

        if(row.initiative==='Buscador'){
          if(row.origin==='Auditoría UX' || row.origin==='Desk research') date='2026-05';
          else if(
            row.origin==='Análisis de datos' ||
            row.origin==='Análisis conductual' ||
            row.origin==='Benchmark'
          ) date='2026-06';
          else if(row.origin==='Auditoría UX') date='2026-05';
          // "Análisis QM" está normalizado como Auditoría UX en algunos registros:
          // los DEMO-144..153 corresponden al Excel BUSCADOR_04_Analisis_Heuristico_QM (jun 2026).
          if(/^DEMO-(14[4-9]|15[0-3])$/.test(row.id)) date='2026-06';
        }

        if(row.initiative==='Header'){
          // CRO desktop Excel: jun 2026 (4 registros: DEMO-164..167)
          if(/^DEMO-(164|165|166|167)$/.test(row.id)) date='2026-06';
          // CRO mobile Excel: jul 2026 (3 registros: DEMO-168..170)
          else if(/^DEMO-(168|169|170)$/.test(row.id)) date='2026-07';
          else if(row.origin==='Benchmark') date='2026-06';
          else if(row.origin==='Test con usuarios') date='2026-05';
          else if(row.origin==='Análisis de datos') date='2026-04';
          else if(row.origin==='Auditoría UX') date='2026-06';
          else if(row.origin==='Desk research') date='2026-04';
        }

        if(row.initiative==='SUMA Frequent Flyer'){
          // Conclusiones y limitaciones AMADEUS: oct 2026
          if(/^DEMO-(00[1-9]|010)$/.test(row.id)) date='2026-10';
          // QM Desktop + Mobile: oct 2026
          else if(/^DEMO-(01[1-9]|020|021|022|023|024|025|026|027)$/.test(row.id)) date='2026-10';
          // Auditoría heurística Mobile: oct 2026
          else if(/^DEMO-(028|029|030|031|032|033|034|035|036)$/.test(row.id)) date='2026-10';
          // Desk Research: jul 2026
          else if(/^DEMO-(03[7-9]|04[0-9]|05[0-7])$/.test(row.id)) date='2026-07';
          // Auditoría slider Millas: jul 2026
          else if(/^DEMO-(058|059|06[0-9])$/.test(row.id)) date='2026-07';

          // Benchmark: sept 2026
          else if(/^DEMO-(088|089|09[0-9]|100|101)$/.test(row.id)) date='2026-09';
        }

        row.incorporationDate=date;
      });

    function openDocumentStore(){
      return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
        const request=indexedDB.open('ae-ux-intel-files-v3-clean-20260918',2);
        request.onupgradeneeded=()=>{const db=request.result;if(!db.objectStoreNames.contains('files'))db.createObjectStore('files');if(!db.objectStoreNames.contains('analyses'))db.createObjectStore('analyses');};
        request.onsuccess=()=>resolve(request.result);
        request.onerror=()=>reject(request.error);
      });
    }
    async function saveDocumentBlob(id,file){const db=await openDocumentStore();return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const tx=db.transaction('files','readwrite');tx.objectStore('files').put(file,id);tx.oncomplete=()=>{db.close();resolve();};tx.onerror=()=>{db.close();reject(tx.error);};});}
    async function getDocumentBlob(id){const db=await openDocumentStore();return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const tx=db.transaction('files','readonly');const request=tx.objectStore('files').get(id);request.onsuccess=()=>{db.close();resolve(request.result||null);};request.onerror=()=>{db.close();reject(request.error);};});}
    async function deleteDocumentBlob(id){const db=await openDocumentStore();return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const tx=db.transaction('files','readwrite');tx.objectStore('files').delete(id);tx.oncomplete=()=>{db.close();resolve();};tx.onerror=()=>{db.close();reject(tx.error);};});}
    const insightRegisterBlobKey=id=>`insight-register:${id}`;
    async function saveInsightRegisterBlob(id,file){return saveDocumentBlob(insightRegisterBlobKey(id),file);}
    async function getInsightRegisterBlob(id){return getDocumentBlob(insightRegisterBlobKey(id));}
    async function deleteInsightRegisterBlob(id){return deleteDocumentBlob(insightRegisterBlobKey(id));}
    async function persistSourceFile(id,kind,file){
      const query=new URLSearchParams({id:String(id||''),kind:String(kind||''),name:String(file?.name||'source-file')});
      const response=await fetch(`/api/source-file?${query.toString()}`,{method:'POST',headers:{'X-UX-Intel':'source-file'},body:file});
      const payload=await response.json().catch(()=>({}));
      if(!response.ok)throw new Error(payload.error||'No se ha podido guardar el archivo fuente en el proyecto.');
      return payload;
    }
    async function getPersistentSourceBlob(id,kind){
      const query=new URLSearchParams({id:String(id||''),kind:String(kind||'')});
      const response=await fetch(`/api/source-file?${query.toString()}`,{cache:'no-store'});
      if(response.status===404)return null;
      if(!response.ok)throw new Error('No se ha podido recuperar el archivo fuente guardado.');
      return response.blob();
    }
    async function saveDocumentAnalysis(id,analysis){const db=await openDocumentStore();return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const tx=db.transaction('analyses','readwrite');tx.objectStore('analyses').put(analysis,id);tx.oncomplete=()=>{db.close();resolve();};tx.onerror=()=>{db.close();reject(tx.error);};tx.onabort=()=>{db.close();reject(tx.error||new Error('La escritura del análisis se ha cancelado.'));};});}
    async function getDocumentAnalysis(id){const db=await openDocumentStore();return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const tx=db.transaction('analyses','readonly');const request=tx.objectStore('analyses').get(id);request.onsuccess=()=>{db.close();resolve(request.result||null);};request.onerror=()=>{db.close();reject(request.error);};});}
    async function deleteDocumentAnalysis(id){const db=await openDocumentStore();return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const tx=db.transaction('analyses','readwrite');tx.objectStore('analyses').delete(id);tx.oncomplete=()=>{db.close();resolve();};tx.onerror=()=>{db.close();reject(tx.error);};});}
    function publishPilotDiagnostic(){
      const item=sourceItems.find(value=>value.id===PILOT_SOURCE_ID),analysis=item?.analysis;
      let output=document.getElementById('pilot-storage-diagnostic');
      if(!output){output=document.createElement('output');output.id='pilot-storage-diagnostic';output.hidden=true;document.body.appendChild(output);}
      output.textContent=JSON.stringify({
        storageAudit:documentStorageAudit.find(record=>record.id===PILOT_SOURCE_ID)||null,
        documentId:item?.id||null,status:item?.status||null,errorMessage:item?.analysisError||item?.analysisFailure?.message||null,
        errorPhase:item?.analysisFailure?.phase||null,stackTrace:item?.analysisStack||item?.analysisFailure?.stack||null,
        hasAnalysis:Boolean(analysis),savedPages:analysis?.extractedPages?.length||analysis?.pages||0,
        savedTextLength:analysis?.extractedText?.length||0,extractedContentLength:analysis?.extraction?.textLength||0,
        savedFindings:analysis?.findings?.length||0,savedRelations:analysis?.relations?.length||0,
        reviewDecisions:analysis?.clusterReview||{},reviewStatus:analysis?.review?.status||null,publicationStatus:analysis?.publication?.status||null,
        hydratedFrom:item?.analysisHydration?.storage||null,hydratedAt:item?.analysisHydration?.timestamp||null,
        analysisSummary:item?.analysisSummary||null,processing:item?.processing||null
      });
    }
    // Read-only storage audit, captured before hydration or any recovery write.
    let documentStorageAudit=[];
    async function auditDocumentStorage(){
      const saved=safeStorageRead();
      documentStorageAudit=await Promise.all(sourceItems.map(async item=>{
        const localDocument=(saved.documents||[]).find(record=>record.id===item.id)||null;
        try{
          const analysis=await getDocumentAnalysis(item.id);
          const fingerprint=analysis?Array.from(new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256',new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(analysis))))).map(byte=>byte.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join(''):null;
          return {id:item.id,statusBeforeHydration:item.status,localDocument,indexedDB:{found:Boolean(analysis),fingerprint,pages:analysis?.pages||0,findings:analysis?.findings?.length||0,relations:analysis?.relations?.length||0,clusterReview:analysis?.clusterReview||{},publication:analysis?.publication||null,metadata:analysis?.metadata||null,extraction:analysis?.extraction||null,review:analysis?.review||null}};
        }catch(error){return {id:item.id,statusBeforeHydration:item.status,localDocument,indexedDB:{found:null,error:String(error?.message||error)}};}
      }));
      let output=document.getElementById('document-storage-audit');
      if(!output){output=document.createElement('output');output.id='document-storage-audit';output.hidden=true;document.body.appendChild(output);}
      output.textContent=JSON.stringify(documentStorageAudit);
    }
    function hasDocumentAnalysis(item){return Boolean(item?.analysis?.findings?.length&&Number(item.analysis.pages)>0&&Number(item.analysis.extraction?.pagesRead)>0)||hasRecoveredDocumentAnalysis(item);}
    function isDocumentPublished(item){return hasDocumentAnalysis(item)&&item.analysis.publication?.status==='published';}
    function canEditDocumentReview(item){return hasDocumentAnalysis(item);}
    function hasDocumentHistory(item){
      return Boolean(['Revisado','Procesado','Publicado','Análisis histórico no disponible en esta copia'].includes(item?.status)||item?.archivedReview?.insights?.length||item?.analysisSummary?.pages>0&&item.analysisSummary.findings>0);
    }
    function documentHistoricalUnavailable(item){return !item.analysis&&(hasDocumentHistory(item)||hasRelatedDocumentKnowledge(item));}
    function resolvedDocumentStatus(item,restoring=false){
      if(!restoring&&item.status==='Analizando')return 'Analizando';
      if(isDocumentPublished(item))return item.status==='Publicado'?'Publicado':'Procesado';
      if(documentHistoricalUnavailable(item)&&(!item.analysisFailure||item.analysisFailure.phase==='analysis_restore')&&!item.analysisError)return 'Procesado';
      if(item.analysisFailure||item.analysisError)return 'Error de análisis';
      if(hasDocumentAnalysis(item))return ['Revisado','Procesado','Publicado'].includes(item.status)?item.status:'Pendiente de revisión';
      if(item.analysis)return 'Error de análisis';
      return 'Pendiente de analizar';
    }
    let documentHydrationComplete=false;
    async function hydrateDocumentAnalyses(){
      for(const item of sourceItems){
        const beforeStatus=item.status;
        try{
          const stored=await getDocumentAnalysis(item.id);
          const fromProject=item.analysisHydration?.storage==='Proyecto/history';
          // Keep a proven project successor active without deleting or rewriting the
          // older browser artifact. Recheck here in case another tab edited it.
          if(fromProject&&stored){
            if(!sameAnalysisValue(stored,item.analysis)&&!projectReviewSubsumesLocal(item.id,item,stored).subsumed)throw Error('La revisión local ha cambiado durante la restauración. No se ha sobrescrito ninguna revisión.');
          }
          else if(stored)item.analysis=stored;
          else if(hasDocumentAnalysis(item))await saveDocumentAnalysis(item.id,item.analysis);
          else item.analysis=null;
          item.analysisHydration={storage:fromProject?'Proyecto/history':'IndexedDB/analyses',timestamp:new Date().toISOString(),found:Boolean(item.analysis),beforeStatus};
          if(!item.analysis&&item.hasAnalysis&&!documentHistoricalUnavailable(item)){
            item.analysisFailure={phase:'analysis_restore',message:'El documento conserva una referencia a su análisis, pero el análisis no está disponible en este almacenamiento.',timestamp:new Date().toISOString()};
          }
          item.status=resolvedDocumentStatus(item,true);
          item.hasAnalysis=Boolean(item.analysis)||Boolean(item.hasAnalysis);
        }catch(error){
          item.analysisHydration={storage:'IndexedDB/analyses',timestamp:new Date().toISOString(),found:false,beforeStatus,error:String(error?.message||error)};
          item.status='Error de análisis';
          console.error(`No se pudo recuperar el análisis de ${item.name}`,error);
        }
      }
      documentHydrationComplete=true;
      // Hydration never saves the analysis back or rewrites the Knowledge Base.
      // Only the document index is reconciled, preserving error/missing-analysis markers.
      try{
        const state=safeStorageRead();
        state.documents=sourceItems.map(item=>{
          const {analysis,analysisHydration,archivedReview,...metadata}=item;
          return {...metadata,hasAnalysis:Boolean(analysis)||Boolean(item.hasAnalysis),analysisSummary:analysis?{pages:analysis.pages||0,findings:analysis.findings?.length||0,relations:analysis.relations?.length||0,pagesRead:analysis.extraction?.pagesRead||0}:item.analysisSummary||null};
        });
        localStorage.setItem(PILOT_STORAGE_KEY,JSON.stringify(state));
      }catch(error){console.error('No se pudo guardar el índice de documentos recuperado',error);}
      publishPilotDiagnostic();
      renderOverview();refreshDocumentDateFilter();renderSources();
      if(document.querySelector('#review.panel.active'))renderReview();
      const openDocumentId=document.querySelector('.drawer.open [data-document-detail]')?.dataset.documentDetail;
      if(openDocumentId)openSource(openDocumentId);
    }

    const patternMeta = {
      "P-01":{product:"Booking",screen:"Home",component:"Buscador",element:"",zone:"Above the fold",device:"Desktop + mobile"},
      "P-02":{product:"Booking",screen:"Home",component:"Header",element:"",device:"Desktop + mobile"},
      "P-03":{product:"Booking",screen:"Home",component:"Buscador",element:"",device:"Desktop + mobile"},
      "P-04":{product:"Booking",screen:"Home",component:"Buscador",element:"Calendario",device:"Desktop + mobile"},

      "P-06":{product:"Booking",screen:"Home",component:"Buscador",element:"Última búsqueda",device:"Desktop + mobile"}
    };
    if(persistedPilot.patternState?.length)patternItems.splice(0,patternItems.length,...persistedPilot.patternState);

    const confidenceClass = value => value==='Alta'?'confidence-high':value==='Media'?'confidence-medium':'confidence-low';
    const impactClass = value => value==='Alto'?'impact-high':value==='Medio'?'impact-medium':value==='Bajo'?'impact-low':'impact-unknown';
    const outcomeClass = value => ({Positivo:'outcome-positive',Mixto:'outcome-mixed',Neutral:'outcome-neutral',Negativo:'outcome-negative'}[value]||'unknown');
    const validityClass = value => ({Vigente:'validity-current',Revisar:'validity-review',Obsoleto:'validity-obsolete'}[value]||'unknown');
    function normalizeText(value){return String(value||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLocaleLowerCase('es');}
    function escapeHtml(value){return String(value??'').replace(/[&<>"]/g,char=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[char]));}
    function compactPath(path){const parts=String(path||'').split(/\s*\/\s*/).map(value=>value.trim()).filter(Boolean),out=[];parts.forEach(value=>{if(!out.length||normalizeText(out[out.length-1])!==normalizeText(value))out.push(value);});return out.join(' / ');} 
    function compactJourneyValues(item){const current=governedTaxonomyRecord(item);return [current.product,current.funnel,current.minifunnel,current.component].map(value=>String(value||'').trim()).filter(Boolean);}
    function sourceHasTransversalScopeMarker(item){
      if(String(item?.documentScopeMode||'').toLowerCase()==='transversal')return true;
      const values=[item?.funnel,item?.minifunnel,item?.screen,item?.component,item?.element].map(value=>normalizeText(value));
      return values.some(value=>value==='multipantalla'||value.includes('multipantalla')||value.includes('transversal'));
    }
    function documentApplicationValues(item){
      const current=governedTaxonomyRecord(item);
      if(sourceHasTransversalScopeMarker(item))return [current.product||String(item?.product||'').trim(),'Transversal'].filter(Boolean);
      return [current.product,current.funnel,current.minifunnel,current.component]
        .map(value=>String(value||'').trim())
        .filter(Boolean);
    }
    function documentApplicationPath(item){return documentApplicationValues(item).join(' › ');}
    function normalizeDocumentApplicationMetadata(item){
      if(!item||typeof item!=='object')return false;
      let changed=false;
      if(!item.documentScopeMode&&sourceHasTransversalScopeMarker(item)){item.documentScopeMode='transversal';changed=true;}
      if(item.documentScopeMode==='transversal'){
        ['funnel','minifunnel','screen','component','element'].forEach(key=>{if(String(item[key]||'').trim()){item[key]='';changed=true;}});
        if(Array.isArray(item.components)&&item.components.length){item.components=[];changed=true;}
      }else if(item.documentScopeMode==='specific'){
        // Specific document scope is intentionally independent from the granular Insight Register rows.
      }
      return changed;
    }
    function pathOf(item){
      if(isLearningCatalogItem(item)){
        // Los learnings consolidados conservan un journey explícito.
        // No inferimos Funnel/Componente desde otro término de la taxonomía,
        // porque puede mezclar contextos que el learning no pretende representar.
        const product=String(item?.product||'').trim();
        const funnel=String(item?.funnel||'').trim();
        const minifunnel=String(item?.minifunnel||'').trim();
        const component=String(item?.component||'').trim();
        return compactPath([product,funnel,minifunnel,component].filter(Boolean).join(' / '));
      }
      const current=governedTaxonomyRecord(item);
      return compactPath([current.product,current.funnel,current.minifunnel,current.component].filter(Boolean).join(' / '));
    }
    const matches = (item,value) => value==='Todos'||value==='Todas'||normalizeText(item).includes(normalizeText(value));
    const empty = label => `<div class="empty-state">${label==='learnings'?'No hay learnings consolidados para este contexto.':'No hay resultados para esta combinación de filtros.'}</div>`;

    const knowledgeTypes=['Hecho','Evidencia interna','Evidencia externa','Hallazgo','Insight del autor','Hipótesis','Inferencia','Oportunidad','Decisión','Experimento','Resultado','Learning consolidado','Patrón'];
    const consolidationOptions=['Crear nuevo learning','Reforzar learning existente','Ampliar learning','Contradice learning','Crear pregunta abierta','Mantener solo como evidencia','Posible patrón','Posible oportunidad','Recomendación de sistema'];
    const relevanceTerms=['buscador','busqueda','buscar','vuelo','origen','destino','fecha','calendario','pasajer','pax','cta','formulario','campo','header','cabecera','banner','above the fold','home','responsive','mobile','movil','desktop','seo','magnolia','accesibilidad','jerarquia','prioridad','friccion','carga cognitiva','error','usuario','conversion','interaccion','navegacion','edicion','autogestion','check-in','reserva','promocion','disponibilidad','booking','negocio','restriccion','amadeus','pss','sesion','suma'];
    const methodTerms=['objetivo del documento','metodologia','indice','agenda','alcance del analisis','plan de trabajo','entregable'];
    function detectKnowledgeType(text){
      const value=normalizeText(text);
      if(/\b(test a\/?b|experimento|variante|control)\b/.test(value))return 'Experimento';
      if(/\b(resultado|uplift|chance to win|significan|aumento|disminucion|incremento|caida|mejoro|empeoro)\b/.test(value)&&(/%|\d/.test(value)))return 'Resultado';
      if(/\b(se decidio|decision|se aprobo|se implementara|se mantiene la decision)\b/.test(value))return 'Decisión';
      if(/\b(hipotesis|podria|cabria esperar|se espera que|es posible que)\b/.test(value))return 'Hipótesis';
      if(/\b(oportunidad|facilitar|simplificar|mejorar la experiencia|reducir friccion|permitir al usuario)\b/.test(value))return 'Oportunidad';
      if(/\b(quantum metric|qm|dato interno|analytics|click|interacciones|usuarios|sesiones|conversion)\b/.test(value)&&(/\d|%/.test(value)))return 'Evidencia interna';
      if(/\b(benchmark|competencia|competidor|desk research|estudio externo|buenas practicas del sector)\b/.test(value))return 'Evidencia externa';
      if(/\b(se infiere|sugiere que|parece|puede generar|podria deberse|heuristic|ley de hick|saliencia)\b/.test(value))return 'Inferencia';
      if(/\b(es|son|incluye|utiliza|dispone|muestra|permite|contiene)\b/.test(value)&&!(/\b(deberia|recomienda|necesita)\b/.test(value)))return 'Hecho';
      return 'Hallazgo';
    }
    function detectContext(text){
      const value=normalizeText(text);
      let component='Buscador',element='';
      if(/\bheader|cabecera|menu|navegacion global|check-in|mi cuenta|gestionar reserva\b/.test(value)){component='Header';element=/check-in/.test(value)?'Acceso Check-in':/mi cuenta|gestionar reserva|autogestion/.test(value)?'Accesos de autogestión':'';}
      else if(/\bbanner|above the fold|aviso|seo|magnolia|promocion\b/.test(value)){component='Above the fold';element=/banner|promocion/.test(value)?'Banners y avisos':/seo/.test(value)?'SEO':/magnolia/.test(value)?'Magnolia':'Jerarquía de componentes';}
      else if(/\bcalendario|fecha de ida|fecha de vuelta|fechas\b/.test(value))element='Calendario';
      else if(/\bcta|buscar vuelos|boton buscar\b/.test(value))element='CTA Buscar vuelos';
      else if(/\bultima busqueda|busqueda reciente|recuperar busqueda\b/.test(value))element='Última búsqueda';
      else if(/\bvuelo \+ hotel|coche|vacaciones|pestanas? de producto\b/.test(value))element='Pestañas de producto';
      else if(/\borigen|destino\b/.test(value))element='Origen y destino';
      else if(/\bpasajer|pax\b/.test(value))element='Pasajeros';
      const hasMobile=/\bmobile|movil|smartphone\b/.test(value),hasDesktop=/\bdesktop|escritorio\b/.test(value);
      const device=hasMobile&&hasDesktop?'Desktop + mobile':hasMobile?'Mobile':hasDesktop?'Desktop':'No especificado';
      const market=/\bes\/?es|mercado espanol|espana\b/.test(value)?'ES/ES':/\binternacional|otros mercados\b/.test(value)?'Internacional':'No especificado';
      return {product:'Booking',screen:'Home',component,element,device,market};
    }
    function confidenceFor(type,text){
      const value=normalizeText(text);
      if(type==='Experimento'||type==='Resultado'){
        if(/muestra|significan|chance to win|probabilidad/.test(value)&&/\d/.test(value))return {level:'Alta',why:'La fuente aporta una medición experimental y datos para valorar su solidez.'};
        return {level:'Media',why:'Hay un resultado medido, pero faltan en este bloque datos suficientes de muestra, significancia o instrumentación.'};
      }
      if(type==='Evidencia interna')return {level:/periodo|sesiones|usuarios/.test(value)?'Alta':'Media',why:'Se apoya en datos internos; la confianza depende del periodo, la instrumentación y el alcance descritos en la fuente.'};
      if(type==='Evidencia externa')return {level:'Media',why:'Sirve para contrastar y generar criterios, pero no valida por sí sola el comportamiento en Air Europa.'};
      if(['Hipótesis','Oportunidad'].includes(type))return {level:'Baja',why:'Es una dirección a investigar o validar, no un resultado demostrado.'};
      if(type==='Inferencia')return {level:'Media',why:'Es una lectura experta trazable a la auditoría, pendiente de contraste con datos o research cuando corresponda.'};
      return {level:'Media',why:'El hallazgo está documentado en la auditoría, pero debe leerse dentro de su contexto y método.'};
    }
    function explicitDate(text){
