      const lines=String(text||'').split(/\n+/).map(line=>line.trim()).filter(Boolean).slice(0,40);
      const explicitLine=lines.find(line=>/^(?:fecha(?:\s+del\s+documento)?\s*[:·-]\s*)?(?:[0-3]?\d[\/.][01]?\d[\/.]20\d{2}|(?:enero|febrero|marzo|abril|mayo|junio|julio|agosto|septiembre|octubre|noviembre|diciembre|ene|feb|mar|abr|may|jun|jul|ago|sep|sept|oct|nov|dic|january|february|march|april|june|july|august|september|october|november|december|jan|feb|mar|apr|may|jun|jul|aug|sep|sept|oct|nov|dec)\s+(?:de\s+)?20\d{2}|20\d{2})$/i.test(line));
      if(!explicitLine)return 'No especificada';
      return explicitLine.replace(/^fecha(?:\s+del\s+documento)?\s*[:·-]\s*/i,'').trim();
    }
    function temporalValue(value,source='metadata'){
      if(value&&typeof value==='object'&&value.value)return value;
      const raw=String(value||'').trim(),normalizedRaw=raw.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLocaleLowerCase('es');if(!normalizedRaw||['no especificado','no especificada','no indicada','no indicado','desconocido','desconocida'].includes(normalizedRaw))return null;
      const months={ene:'01',enero:'01',jan:'01',january:'01',feb:'02',febrero:'02',february:'02',mar:'03',marzo:'03',march:'03',abr:'04',abril:'04',apr:'04',april:'04',may:'05',mayo:'05',jun:'06',junio:'06',june:'06',jul:'07',julio:'07',july:'07',ago:'08',agosto:'08',aug:'08',august:'08',sep:'09',sept:'09',septiembre:'09',september:'09',oct:'10',octubre:'10',october:'10',nov:'11',noviembre:'11',november:'11',dic:'12',diciembre:'12',dec:'12',december:'12'};
      const iso=raw.match(/^(20\d{2})-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])(?:-([0-3]\d))?(?:T.*)?$/);if(iso)return {value:iso[3]?`${iso[1]}-${iso[2]}-${iso[3]}`:`${iso[1]}-${iso[2]}`,precision:iso[3]?'day':'month',source};
      const toFourDigitYear=year=>String(year).length===2?`20${year}`:String(year);
      const numeric=raw.match(/^([0-3]?\d)[\/.]([01]?\d)[\/.](\d{2}|20\d{2})$/);if(numeric){const year=toFourDigitYear(numeric[3]);return {value:`${year}-${String(numeric[2]).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(numeric[1]).padStart(2,'0')}`,precision:'day',source};}
      const numericMonth=raw.match(/^(0?[1-9]|1[0-2])[\/-](\d{2}|20\d{2})$/);if(numericMonth){const year=toFourDigitYear(numericMonth[2]);return {value:`${year}-${String(numericMonth[1]).padStart(2,'0')}`,precision:'month',source};}
      const yearMonth=raw.match(/^(20\d{2})[\/](0?[1-9]|1[0-2])$/);if(yearMonth)return {value:`${yearMonth[1]}-${String(yearMonth[2]).padStart(2,'0')}`,precision:'month',source};
      const month=normalizedRaw.match(/^(?:([0-3]?\d)\s+)?([a-z]+)\.?\s+(\d{2}|20\d{2})$/);if(month&&months[month[2]]){const year=toFourDigitYear(month[3]);return {value:month[1]?`${year}-${months[month[2]]}-${String(month[1]).padStart(2,'0')}`:`${year}-${months[month[2]]}`,precision:month[1]?'day':'month',source};}
      if(/^(\d{2}|20\d{2})$/.test(raw)){const year=toFourDigitYear(raw);return {value:year,precision:'year',source};}
      return null;
    }
    function formatDateEs(value){
      const temporal=temporalValue(typeof value==='string'?value.replace(/\s+de\s+/gi,' '):value);if(!temporal)return 'No especificada';
      const [year,month]=String(temporal.value).split('-');
      const labels={'01':'ene','02':'feb','03':'mar','04':'abr','05':'may','06':'jun','07':'jul','08':'ago','09':'sept','10':'oct','11':'nov','12':'dic'};
      return temporal.precision!=='year'&&labels[month]?`${labels[month]} ${year}`:'No especificada';
    }
    function temporalYear(value){return temporalValue(value)?.value?.slice(0,4)||'';}
    function isSpecifiedMetadata(value){const normalized=normalizeText(value).trim();return Boolean(normalized)&&!['no especificado','no especificada','no indicada','no indicado','desconocido','desconocida'].includes(normalized);}
    function firstSpecifiedMetadata(...values){return values.find(isSpecifiedMetadata)||'No especificada';}
    function documentDate(documentItem){
      const titleDate=String(documentItem?.name||'').match(/\b(?:ene(?:ro)?|feb(?:rero)?|mar(?:zo)?|abr(?:il)?|may(?:o)?|jun(?:io)?|jul(?:io)?|ago(?:sto)?|sep(?:t(?:iembre)?)?|oct(?:ubre)?|nov(?:iembre)?|dic(?:iembre)?)\s+(?:de\s+)?20\d{2}\b/i)?.[0];
      const candidates=[documentItem?.documentDate?.source==='manual'?documentItem.documentDate:null,documentItem?.analysis?.metadata?.date,documentItem?.documentDate,documentItem?.exactDate,documentItem?.catalogDate,documentItem?.date,titleDate];
      return candidates.map(formatDateEs).find(value=>value!=='No especificada')||'No especificada';
    }
    function refreshDocumentDateFilter(){
      const select=document.getElementById('source-date');if(!select)return;
      const current=select.value,dates=[...new Set(sourceItems.map(documentDate))].sort((a,b)=>{
        if(a==='No especificada')return 1;if(b==='No especificada')return -1;
        return (temporalValue(b)?.value||'').localeCompare(temporalValue(a)?.value||'');
      });
      select.innerHTML='<option value="Todos">Todos</option>'+dates.map(value=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(value)}">${escapeHtml(value)}</option>`).join('');
      select.value=dates.includes(current)?current:'Todos';
    }
    function documentYear(documentItem){return temporalYear(documentItem?.documentDate||documentItem?.exactDate||documentItem?.catalogDate);}
    function extractExperimentMetadata(text){
      const raw=String(text),value=normalizeText(raw),percentages=raw.match(/[+−-]?\d+(?:[,.]\d+)?\s*%/g)||[];
      const duration=raw.match(/\b\d+\s*(?:d[ií]as?|semanas?|meses?)\b/i)?.[0]||'No especificado';
      const sample=raw.match(/(?:muestra|n\s*=)\s*(?:de\s*)?[\d.,kKmM]+/i)?.[0]||'No especificada';
      const traffic=raw.match(/\d+(?:[,.]\d+)?\s*%\s*(?:del\s*)?tr[aá]fico/i)?.[0]||'No especificado';
      const significance=raw.match(/(?:chance to win|probabilidad|significancia)[^.;]{0,60}/i)?.[0]||'No especificada';
      const positive=/\b(mejora|aumenta|incrementa|positivo|supera)\b/.test(value),negative=/\b(empeora|cae|reduce|negativo|disminuye)\b/.test(value);
      const outcome=positive&&negative?'Mixto':positive?'Positivo':negative?'Negativo':/\bneutral|sin diferencias\b/.test(value)?'Neutral':'No especificado';
      const implementation=/\bno implementado\b/.test(value)?'No implementado':/\bpendiente|en desarrollo\b/.test(value)?'Pendiente / En desarrollo':/\ben pro|en produccion|implementado en produccion\b/.test(value)?'En PRO':'Desconocido';
      return {duration,sample,traffic,metric:percentages.join(' · ')||'No especificada',significance,outcome,implementation};
    }
    function isHeadingLine(line){const clean=line.trim();return clean.length>2&&clean.length<92&&!/[.!?]$/.test(clean)&&((clean===clean.toUpperCase()&&/[A-ZÁÉÍÓÚÑ]/.test(clean))||/^\d+(?:\.\d+)*\s+/.test(clean)||/^(introducci[oó]n|conclusiones|hallazgos|recomendaciones|auditor[ií]a|desktop|mobile|buscador|header|responsive|seo|magnolia)/i.test(clean));}
    function sentenceChunks(text){
      return String(text).replace(/\s+/g,' ').split(/(?<=[.!?])\s+(?=[A-ZÁÉÍÓÚÑ¿])|\s+[•▪◦]\s+|\s+-\s+(?=[A-ZÁÉÍÓÚÑ])/).flatMap(part=>part.length>700?part.split(/;\s+|:\s+(?=[A-ZÁÉÍÓÚÑ])/):[part]).map(part=>part.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    }
    function collectDocumentBlocks(pages){
      const blocks=[],discarded=[],sections=new Set();
      pages.forEach(page=>{
        let section=`Página ${page.page}`;
        const rawLines=String(page.text||'').split(/\n/).map(line=>line.replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim());
        const candidates=[];let buffer='';
        const flush=()=>{if(buffer.trim())candidates.push(buffer.trim());buffer='';};
        rawLines.forEach(line=>{
          if(!line){flush();return;}
          if(isHeadingLine(line)){flush();section=line.slice(0,120);sections.add(section);return;}
          if(/^[-–—•▪◦]\s*/.test(line))flush();
          buffer=`${buffer} ${line.replace(/^[-–—•▪◦]\s*/,'' )}`.trim();
          if(/[.!?]$/.test(line))flush();
        });
        flush();
        if(candidates.length<=1)candidates.splice(0,candidates.length,...sentenceChunks(page.text));
        candidates.forEach((line,index)=>{
          sentenceChunks(line.replace(/^[-–—•▪◦]\s*/,'' )).forEach((text,subIndex)=>{
            const normalized=normalizeText(text);
            if(text.length<28){discarded.push({page:page.page,text,reason:'Fragmento demasiado corto o etiqueta sin afirmación.'});return;}
            if(methodTerms.some(term=>normalized.includes(term))&&!relevanceTerms.some(term=>normalized.includes(term))){discarded.push({page:page.page,text,reason:'Contexto o metodología sin afirmación de producto.'});return;}
            const relevant=relevanceTerms.some(term=>normalized.includes(term))||/\b(debe|deberia|necesita|dificulta|facilita|problema|recomend|evitar|mantener|priorizar|funciona|no funciona)\b/.test(normalized);
            if(!relevant){discarded.push({page:page.page,text,reason:'No se detecta una afirmación aplicable a producto, UX, negocio o restricciones.'});return;}
            blocks.push({page:page.page,section,text,block:`P${page.page}-${index+1}.${subIndex+1}`});
          });
        });
      });
      const seen=new Set();
      const unique=blocks.filter(block=>{const key=normalizeText(block.text).replace(/\W/g,'');if(seen.has(key)){discarded.push({...block,reason:'Duplicado literal dentro del documento.'});return false;}seen.add(key);return true;});
      return {blocks:unique,discarded,sections:[...sections]};
    }
    function analysisTokens(text){
      const stop=new Set(['para','como','pero','porque','desde','hasta','entre','sobre','esta','este','estos','estas','tiene','tener','debe','deberia','puede','cada','cuando','donde','tambien','solo','muy','mas','menos','una','uno','unos','unas','del','las','los','con','sin','por','que','se','su','sus','al','el','la','en','y','o']);
      const synonymMap={buscador:'search',busqueda:'search',buscar:'search',vuelos:'search',vuelo:'search',cta:'accion',boton:'accion',saliencia:'jerarquia',destacar:'jerarquia',prioridad:'jerarquia',principal:'jerarquia',movil:'mobile',responsive:'device',desktop:'device',dispositivo:'device',dispositivos:'device',calendario:'fechas',fecha:'fechas'};
      return new Set(normalizeText(text).replace(/[^a-z0-9áéíóúñ]+/g,' ').split(/\s+/).filter(token=>token.length>2&&!stop.has(token)).map(token=>synonymMap[token]||token));
    }
    function semanticSimilarity(a,b){const left=analysisTokens(a),right=analysisTokens(b);if(!left.size||!right.size)return 0;let intersection=0;left.forEach(token=>{if(right.has(token))intersection++;});return (2*intersection)/(left.size+right.size);}
    function explicitContradictionSignal(text){
      const normalized=normalizeText(text);
      // Limitaciones metodológicas o reducciones positivas no son contradicciones.
      if(/\b(no demuestra|no implica|no permite atribuir|no permite concluir|pendiente de validar|no esta validada|no valida)\b/.test(normalized))return false;
      if(/\b(reduce|reducir|reduccion)\s+(?:el\s+|la\s+)?(?:esfuerzo|friccion|carga|incertidumbre|errores?)\b/.test(normalized))return false;
      const explicit=/\b(contradice|evidencia contradictoria|no confirma|no respalda|resultado negativo|direccion opuesta)\b/.test(normalized);
      const negativeOutcome=/\b(empeora|cae|disminuye|reduce)\b[^.;]{0,70}\b(conversion|avance|uso|clic|click|ctr|exito|completitud|rendimiento|reservas?)\b/.test(normalized)
        ||/\b(conversion|avance|uso|clic|click|ctr|exito|completitud|rendimiento|reservas?)\b[^.;]{0,70}\b(empeora|cae|disminuye|reduce)\b/.test(normalized);
      return explicit||negativeOutcome;
    }
    function relationForFinding(finding){
      const ranked=learnings.map(learning=>({learning,score:semanticSimilarity(finding.text,`${learning.title} ${learning.know} ${learning.text} ${learning.applications.join(' ')}`)+(finding.component===learning.component?.trim?.()?0.08:0)})).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score);
      const best=ranked[0];
      const contradiction=explicitContradictionSignal(finding.text)&&best?.score>.24;
      let suggestion='Mantener solo como evidencia';
      if(finding.type==='Oportunidad')suggestion='Posible oportunidad';
      else if(finding.type==='Hipótesis'||finding.confidence==='Baja')suggestion='Crear pregunta abierta';
      else if(best?.score>=.22)suggestion=contradiction?'Contradice learning':best.score>=.4?'Reforzar learning existente':'Ampliar learning';
      else if(finding.type==='Hallazgo'&&finding.confidence==='Media')suggestion='Mantener solo como evidencia';
      return {id:`R-${finding.id}`,findingId:finding.id,learningId:best?.score>=.16?best.learning.id:'',score:best?.score||0,suggestion,decision:suggestion,status:'pending'};
    }
    function buildAnalysis(documentItem,pages,{engine='document-first-1'}={}){
      const collected=collectDocumentBlocks(pages);
      const findings=collected.blocks.map((block,index)=>{
        const type=detectKnowledgeType(block.text),context=detectContext(block.text),confidence=confidenceFor(type,block.text);
        return {id:`${documentItem.id}-H-${String(index+1).padStart(3,'0')}`,text:block.text,type,...context,confidence:confidence.level,confidenceWhy:confidence.why,experiment:['Experimento','Resultado'].includes(type)?extractExperimentMetadata(block.text):null,source:{documentId:documentItem.id,name:documentItem.name,page:block.page,section:block.section,block:block.block},status:'pending',discardReason:''};
      });
      const relations=[];
      findings.forEach((finding,index)=>{
        const prior=findings.slice(0,index).map(other=>({other,score:semanticSimilarity(finding.text,other.text)})).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score)[0];
        if(prior?.score>=.66)relations.push({id:`RD-${finding.id}`,findingId:finding.id,learningId:'',relatedFindingId:prior.other.id,score:prior.score,suggestion:'Mantener solo como evidencia',decision:'Mantener solo como evidencia',status:'pending',duplicate:true});
      });
      const typeCounts=findings.reduce((acc,item)=>{acc[item.type]=(acc[item.type]||0)+1;return acc;},{});
      const analysis={
        createdAt:new Date().toISOString(),pages:pages.length,sections:collected.sections,findings,relations,discarded:collected.discarded,typeCounts,
        coverage:{pagesRead:pages.length,blocksReviewed:collected.blocks.length+collected.discarded.length,represented:findings.length,discarded:collected.discarded.length,check:'Todas las páginas y bloques de texto extraíbles han pasado por las reglas de relevancia. La cobertura semántica debe confirmarse durante la revisión humana.'},
        metadata:{detectedType:'Auditoría UX',initiative:'Buscador',date:explicitDate(pages[0]?.text||''),products:['Booking'],screens:[...new Set(findings.map(item=>item.screen))],components:[...new Set(findings.map(item=>item.component))],devices:[...new Set(findings.map(item=>item.device).filter(Boolean))],markets:[...new Set(findings.map(item=>item.market).filter(Boolean))]}
      };
      const prepared=UXDocumentAnalysis.prepare(analysis,documentItem,pages,learnings);
      if(engine!=='document-first-2')return prepared;
      return UXDocumentAnalysisV2.prepareNewIngest({analysis:prepared,document:documentItem,knowledge:{learningState:learnings,evidenceState:evidenceItems,patternState:patternItems,knowledgeUnits,openQuestionItems},canonicalContext:{product:documentItem.product,screen:documentItem.screen,component:documentItem.component}});
    }
    function serializableAnalysisClone(analysis){
      const issues=[],active=new WeakMap(),firstPath=new WeakMap();
      const visit=(value,path)=>{
        const type=typeof value;
        if(type==='undefined')return undefined;
        if(type==='function'||type==='symbol'||type==='bigint'){issues.push(`${path}: ${type}`);return undefined;}
        if(!value||type!=='object')return value;
        if(value instanceof Blob||value instanceof File){issues.push(`${path}: ${value.constructor.name}`);return undefined;}
        if(active.has(value)){issues.push(`${path}: referencia circular con ${active.get(value)}`);return undefined;}
        if(firstPath.has(value)){issues.push(`${path}: referencia compartida con ${firstPath.get(value)}`);return undefined;}
        active.set(value,path);firstPath.set(value,path);
        const prototype=Object.getPrototypeOf(value),plainObject=Object.prototype.toString.call(value)==='[object Object]'&&(prototype===null||value.constructor?.name==='Object');
        if(!Array.isArray(value)&&!plainObject){issues.push(`${path}: ${value.constructor?.name||'objeto no plano'}`);return undefined;}
        if(Array.isArray(value)){
          const clone=value.map((item,index)=>{
          if(typeof item==='undefined'){issues.push(`${path}.${index}: undefined en array`);return null;}
          return visit(item,`${path}.${index}`);
          });
          active.delete(value);return clone;
        }
        const clone=Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(value).flatMap(([key,item])=>{
          if(typeof item==='undefined')return [];
          return [[key,visit(item,`${path}.${key}`)]];
        }));
        active.delete(value);return clone;
      };
      const cleaned=visit(analysis,'analysis');
      if(issues.length)throw new Error(`El análisis contiene valores no serializables: ${issues.slice(0,8).join(' · ')}`);
      const json=JSON.stringify(cleaned);
      return {value:JSON.parse(json),jsonLength:json.length};
    }
    function pipelineCheckpoint(documentItem,phase,details={}){
      const timestamp=new Date().toISOString();
      if(!documentItem.processing)documentItem.processing={runId:`RUN-${Date.now()}`,startedAt:timestamp,phase:'starting',checkpoints:[]};
      documentItem.processing.phase=phase;documentItem.processing.updatedAt=timestamp;
      documentItem.processing.checkpoints.push({phase,timestamp,...details});
      console.info(`[UX Intel pipeline] ${phase}`,JSON.stringify({documentId:documentItem.id,fileName:documentItem.originalName,phase,timestamp,...details}));
      // Checkpoints are diagnostic and may describe an incomplete transaction. Keep
      // them in the browser only; the versioned project is committed once, after
      // the complete analysis has passed its persistence checks.
      safeStorageWrite({project:false});
    }
    function readBlobAsArrayBuffer(blob){
      if(typeof blob?.arrayBuffer==='function')return blob.arrayBuffer();
      return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const reader=new FileReader();reader.onload=()=>resolve(reader.result);reader.onerror=()=>reject(reader.error||new Error('No se pudo leer el archivo seleccionado.'));reader.readAsArrayBuffer(blob);});
    }
    function readBlobAsText(blob){
      if(typeof blob?.text==='function')return blob.text();
      return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const reader=new FileReader();reader.onload=()=>resolve(String(reader.result||''));reader.onerror=()=>reject(reader.error||new Error('No se pudo leer el archivo seleccionado.'));reader.readAsText(blob);});
    }
    function pdfErrorDetails(error){
      return {name:error?.name||'Error',message:error?.message||String(error),stack:error?.stack||'Stack no disponible'};
    }
    function ensureReadableStreamAsyncIterator(){
      const prototype=globalThis.ReadableStream?.prototype,symbol=globalThis.Symbol?.asyncIterator;
      if(!prototype||!symbol)return {supported:false,polyfilled:false,reason:'ReadableStream o Symbol.asyncIterator no está disponible'};
      if(typeof prototype[symbol]==='function')return {supported:true,polyfilled:false};
      Object.defineProperty(prototype,symbol,{configurable:true,writable:true,value:async function*(){
        const reader=this.getReader();
        try{for(;;){const {value,done}=await reader.read();if(done)return;yield value;}}
        finally{try{reader.releaseLock();}catch{}}
      }});
      return {supported:true,polyfilled:true};
    }
    async function readTextContentStream(page,options){
      const stream=page.streamTextContent(options),reader=stream.getReader(),content={items:[],styles:Object.create(null),lang:null};
      try{
        for(;;){
          const {value,done}=await reader.read();
          if(done)break;
          if(!value)continue;
          if(content.lang==null&&value.lang!=null)content.lang=value.lang;
          Object.assign(content.styles,value.styles||{});
          content.items.push(...(value.items||[]));
        }
      }finally{try{reader.releaseLock();}catch{}}
      return content;
    }
    async function extractDocumentPages(file,fileName='',onStage=()=>{}){
      const resolvedName=String(fileName||file?.name||'documento.pdf');
      const extension=(resolvedName.split('.').pop()||'').toLowerCase();
      const diagnostics={fileName:resolvedName,fileSize:file?.size||0,mimeType:file?.type||'No indicado',detectedPages:0,pagesRead:0,pagesWithText:0,textLength:0,failedPages:[],pagesWithoutText:[],pageResults:[],worker:null,browserCompatibility:null};
      console.groupCollapsed?.(`[UX Intel PDF][diagnóstico temporal] ${resolvedName}`);
      console.info('[UX Intel PDF] Archivo',{name:resolvedName,sizeBytes:diagnostics.fileSize,sizeMB:(diagnostics.fileSize/1048576).toFixed(2),type:diagnostics.mimeType});
      try{
        if(['txt','md','csv'].includes(extension)){
          const text=await readBlobAsText(file);
          Object.assign(diagnostics,{detectedPages:1,pagesRead:1,pagesWithText:text.trim()?1:0,textLength:text.length});
          console.info('[UX Intel PDF] Lectura de texto completada',diagnostics);
          return {pages:[{page:1,text}],diagnostics};
        }
        if(extension!=='pdf')throw new Error('Este piloto lee PDF, TXT, MD y CSV. Los demás formatos se conectarán después de validar el flujo.');
        if(location.protocol==='file:')throw new Error('Para analizar PDFs abre UX Intel desde http://127.0.0.1:4175/. La vista file:// permite consultar, pero el lector local de PDF necesita el servidor del proyecto.');
        const moduleUrl=new URL('./vendor/pdfjs/pdf.min.mjs',location.href).href;
        const workerUrl=new URL('./vendor/pdfjs/pdf.worker.min.mjs',location.href).href;
        diagnostics.browserCompatibility={
          readableStreamAsyncIteratorBefore:typeof globalThis.ReadableStream?.prototype?.[globalThis.Symbol?.asyncIterator]==='function',
          promiseWithResolvers:typeof Promise.withResolvers==='function',promiseTry:typeof Promise.try==='function',userAgent:navigator.userAgent
        };
        diagnostics.browserCompatibility.readableStream=ensureReadableStreamAsyncIterator();
        const pdfjs=await import(moduleUrl);
        pdfjs.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc=workerUrl;
        const forceFakeWorker=new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('pdfWorker')==='off';
        if(forceFakeWorker)await import(workerUrl);
        let workerProbe={status:null,contentType:null};
        try{const response=await fetch(workerUrl,{method:'HEAD',cache:'no-store'});workerProbe={status:response.status,contentType:response.headers.get('content-type')};}
        catch(error){workerProbe={status:'fetch-error',contentType:null,error:pdfErrorDetails(error)};}
        diagnostics.worker={requestedMode:forceFakeWorker?'fake-main-thread':'module-worker',workerSrc:workerUrl,coreSrc:moduleUrl,probe:workerProbe};
        console.info('[UX Intel PDF] Lector inicializado',{library:'PDF.js',version:pdfjs.version,moduleUrl,workerUrl,worker:diagnostics.worker,browserCompatibility:diagnostics.browserCompatibility});
        const arrayBuffer=await readBlobAsArrayBuffer(file);
        onStage('array_buffer_read',{byteLength:arrayBuffer.byteLength});
        console.info('[UX Intel PDF] ArrayBuffer leído',{byteLength:arrayBuffer.byteLength});
        const task=pdfjs.getDocument({data:new Uint8Array(arrayBuffer),isEvalSupported:false});
        let pdf;
        try{pdf=await task.promise;}catch(error){throw new Error(`PDF.js no pudo abrir el documento: ${error?.message||error}`,{cause:error});}
        diagnostics.worker.actualPort=task._worker?.port?.constructor?.name||'No disponible';
        diagnostics.worker.actualMode=diagnostics.worker.actualPort==='Worker'?'module-worker':diagnostics.worker.actualPort==='LoopbackPort'?'fake-main-thread':'No determinado';
        onStage('pdf_loaded',{pdfVersion:pdfjs.version,worker:diagnostics.worker,browserCompatibility:diagnostics.browserCompatibility});
        diagnostics.detectedPages=pdf.numPages;
        onStage('pages_detected',{pages:pdf.numPages});
        console.info('[UX Intel PDF] Páginas detectadas',pdf.numPages);
        const pages=[];
        for(let pageNumber=1;pageNumber<=pdf.numPages;pageNumber++){
          const pageResult={pageNumber,getPage:{ok:false},getTextContent:{ok:false},fallback:{used:false,ok:false},characters:0};
          diagnostics.pageResults.push(pageResult);
          let page=null;
          try{
            page=await pdf.getPage(pageNumber);
            pageResult.getPage.ok=true;
            console.info(`[UX Intel PDF] Página ${pageNumber}: getPage() correcto`);
          }catch(error){
            pageResult.getPage.error=pdfErrorDetails(error);
            diagnostics.failedPages.push({page:pageNumber,phase:'getPage',...pageResult.getPage.error});
            console.error(`[UX Intel PDF] Página ${pageNumber}: getPage() falló`,pageResult.getPage.error,error);
            continue;
          }
          try{
            const textOptions={disableNormalization:false,includeMarkedContent:false};
            let content;
            try{
              content=await page.getTextContent(textOptions);
              pageResult.getTextContent.ok=true;
              console.info(`[UX Intel PDF] Página ${pageNumber}: getTextContent() correcto`,{items:content?.items?.length||0});
            }catch(error){
              pageResult.getTextContent.error=pdfErrorDetails(error);
              console.error(`[UX Intel PDF] Página ${pageNumber}: getTextContent() falló`,pageResult.getTextContent.error,error);
              pageResult.fallback.used=true;
              try{
                content=await readTextContentStream(page,textOptions);
                pageResult.fallback.ok=true;
                console.warn(`[UX Intel PDF] Página ${pageNumber}: extracción compatible mediante streamTextContent()`,{items:content?.items?.length||0,originalError:pageResult.getTextContent.error});
              }catch(fallbackError){
                pageResult.fallback.error=pdfErrorDetails(fallbackError);
                throw new Error(`getTextContent() falló: ${pageResult.getTextContent.error.message}. El fallback streamTextContent() también falló: ${pageResult.fallback.error.message}`,{cause:fallbackError});
              }
            }
            let text='';
            for(const item of Array.from(content?.items||[]))text+=`${item?.str||''}${item?.hasEOL?'\n':' '}`;
            text=text.replace(/[ \t]+\n/g,'\n').trim();
            pages.push({page:pageNumber,text});
            pageResult.characters=text.length;
            diagnostics.pagesRead++;
            diagnostics.textLength+=text.length;
            if(text){diagnostics.pagesWithText++;console.info(`[UX Intel PDF] Página ${pageNumber} leída`,{characters:text.length});}
            else{diagnostics.pagesWithoutText.push(pageNumber);console.warn(`[UX Intel PDF] Página ${pageNumber} sin texto extraíble`);}
            try{page.cleanup?.();}catch(cleanupError){console.warn(`[UX Intel PDF] No se pudo liberar la página ${pageNumber}`,cleanupError);}
          }catch(error){
            const pageError={page:pageNumber,phase:pageResult.getTextContent.ok||pageResult.fallback.ok?'text_assembly':'getTextContent',...pdfErrorDetails(error)};
            diagnostics.failedPages.push(pageError);
            console.error(`[UX Intel PDF] Error final en página ${pageNumber}`,pageError,error);
          }
        }
        try{await task.destroy?.();}catch(workerError){console.warn('[UX Intel PDF] El worker terminó con una advertencia después de extraer el texto',workerError);}
        onStage('pages_extracted',{pagesRead:diagnostics.pagesRead,pagesWithText:diagnostics.pagesWithText,textLength:diagnostics.textLength,failedPages:diagnostics.failedPages,pageResults:diagnostics.pageResults});
        console.info('[UX Intel PDF] Extracción completada',{detectedPages:diagnostics.detectedPages,pagesRead:diagnostics.pagesRead,pagesWithText:diagnostics.pagesWithText,textLength:diagnostics.textLength,failedPages:diagnostics.failedPages,pagesWithoutText:diagnostics.pagesWithoutText,pageResults:diagnostics.pageResults,worker:diagnostics.worker});
        if(!pages.length)throw new Error(`No se pudo leer ninguna de las ${diagnostics.detectedPages} páginas del PDF.`);
        if(diagnostics.textLength<120)throw new Error('El PDF parece escaneado o no contiene texto extraíble. Este piloto todavía no incorpora OCR.');
        return {pages,diagnostics};
      }catch(error){
        console.error('[UX Intel PDF] Error exacto',{name:error?.name||'Error',message:error?.message||String(error),stack:error?.stack||'Stack no disponible',cause:error?.cause||null,diagnostics});
        throw error;
      }finally{console.groupEnd?.();}
    }


    // Catalog metadata only. Original PDF findings, review decisions and publication logic stay untouched.
    function normalizeCatalogContext(item){
      if(item.screen==='EXTRAS')item.screen='Extras';
      if(item.component==='Above the fold'){
        item.zone='Above the fold';
        if(item.element==='Jerarquía de componentes'){item.scope='Transversal';item.contextConcepts=[...new Set([...(item.contextConcepts||[]),item.element])];}
        if(item.element==='Banners y avisos')item.components=['Banner promocional','Avisos'];
        item.component='';
        if(item.element&&!['Banners y avisos','Jerarquía de componentes'].includes(item.element))item.contextConcepts=[...new Set([...(item.contextConcepts||[]),item.element])];
        
      }
      if(item.component==='Banners y avisos'){
        item.component='';item.components=['Banner promocional','Avisos'];item.scope='Transversal';
      }
      if(item.component==='Jerarquía de componentes'){item.component='';item.scope='Transversal';}
      if(item.element==='Jerarquía de componentes'){item.scope='Transversal';}
      if(item.market==='ES/ES + benchmark'){
        item.market='ES/ES';
        item.evidenceKinds=[...new Set([...(item.evidenceKinds||[]),'Benchmark'])];
      }
      if(item.market==='Transversal'){item.market='No especificado';item.scope='Transversal';}
      if(!item.market)item.market=/\bES\/ES\b/i.test(item.context||'')?'ES/ES':'No especificado';
      if(item.screen==='Home'&&/above the fold/i.test([item.title,item.know,item.flow,item.context,item.scope].filter(Boolean).join(' ')))item.zone='Above the fold';
      return item;
    }
    function sourceIdentityValues(source){
      return [
        source?.name,
        source?.originalName,
        source?.fileMeta?.name,
        source?.insightRegister?.file?.name
      ].filter(value=>typeof value==='string'&&value.trim());
    }
    function contextInitiatives(item){
      return [...new Set([...(item?.initiatives||[]),item?.initiative].filter(value=>value&&!['Sin clasificar','No especificado','Transversal'].includes(value)).map(normalizeText))];
    }
    function sourceInitiatives(source){
      return [...new Set([...(source?.initiatives||[]),source?.initiative].filter(value=>value&&!['Sin clasificar','No especificado','Transversal','Documentación'].includes(value)).map(normalizeText))];
    }
    function initiativesOverlap(item,source){
      const left=contextInitiatives(item),right=sourceInitiatives(source);
      if(!left.length||!right.length)return false;
      return left.some(value=>right.includes(value));
    }
    function normalizedSourceBase(value){
      return normalizeText(String(value||'').replace(/\.(pdf|xlsx?|csv)$/i,'').replace(/^\d+[._-]*/,'').trim());
    }
    function sourceCandidates(name,item=null){
      const target=normalizeText(name),targetBase=normalizedSourceBase(name);
      if(!targetBase)return [];
      let candidates=sourceItems.filter(source=>sourceIdentityValues(source).some(value=>{
        const identity=normalizeText(value),base=normalizedSourceBase(value);
        return identity===target||base===targetBase;
      }));
      if(candidates.length>1&&item){
        const scoped=candidates.filter(source=>initiativesOverlap(item,source));
        if(scoped.length)candidates=scoped;
      }
      if(candidates.length)return candidates;

      // El fallback difuso solo se permite con nombres suficientemente específicos.
      // Evita que "Benchmark", "Desk research", etc. relacionen iniciativas distintas.
      if(targetBase.length<12)return [];
      candidates=sourceItems.filter(source=>sourceIdentityValues(source).some(value=>{
        const base=normalizedSourceBase(value);
        return base.length>=12&&(base.includes(targetBase)||targetBase.includes(base));
      }));
      if(item){
        const scoped=candidates.filter(source=>initiativesOverlap(item,source));
        if(scoped.length)candidates=scoped;
      }
      return candidates;
    }
    function catalogSources(item){
      const names=[...(item.sourceNames||[]),item.sourceName,item.source,...(item.publicationHistory||[]).map(entry=>entry.sourceName)].filter(value=>typeof value==='string');
      const directIds=[item.documentId,...(item.publicationHistory||[]).map(entry=>entry.documentId)].filter(Boolean);
      // These evidence records are the existing UX readings of the two named CRO reports.
      const croSources={'E-03':['S-07'],'E-04':['S-08'],'E-13':['S-07','S-08'],'AB-01':['S-07'],'AB-02':['S-08']};
      directIds.push(...(croSources[item.id]||[]));
      const catalogAliases={'Header · Síntesis A/B mobile':'S-07','Header · Síntesis A/B desktop':'S-08','Header · Evolución Above the Fold':'S-09'};
      names.forEach(name=>{if(catalogAliases[name])directIds.push(catalogAliases[name]);});
      const matchedIds=new Set(directIds);
      names.forEach(name=>sourceCandidates(name,item).forEach(source=>matchedIds.add(source.id)));
      return sourceItems.filter(source=>matchedIds.has(source.id));
    }
    function initiativesOf(item){
      if(item?.initiativeManual&&item.initiative&&!['Sin clasificar','No especificado','Transversal'].includes(item.initiative))return [item.initiative];
      const existing=[...(item.initiatives||[]),item.initiative].filter(value=>value&&!['Sin clasificar','No especificado','Transversal'].includes(value));

      const namedSources=[...(item.sourceNames||[]),item.sourceName,item.source,...(item.publicationHistory||[]).map(entry=>entry.sourceName)].filter(value=>typeof value==='string');
      const known=[...new Set(sourceItems.flatMap(source=>[...(source.initiatives||[]),source.initiative]).filter(value=>value&&!['Sin clasificar','No especificado'].includes(value)))];
      const fromNames=known.filter(name=>namedSources.some(source=>normalizeText(source).startsWith(normalizeText(name)+' ·')));
      const fromDocuments=catalogSources(item).flatMap(source=>[...(source.initiatives||[]),source.initiative]);
      const linkedLearnings=item.proof?learnings.filter(learning=>item.proof.includes(learning.id)||(learning.product===item.product&&learning.screen===item.screen&&componentsOf(learning).some(component=>componentsOf(item).includes(component)))):[];
      return [...new Set([...existing,...fromNames,...fromDocuments,...linkedLearnings.flatMap(learning=>learning.initiatives||[])].filter(value=>value&&!['Sin clasificar','No especificado','Transversal'].includes(value)))].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,'es'));
    }
    function catalogCollection(group){
      return {learning:learnings,evidence:evidenceItems,experiment:experiments,pattern:patternItems,source:sourceItems,explore:[...learnings,...evidenceItems,...experiments,...patternItems,...knowledgeUnits,...openQuestionItems,...sourceItems]}[group]||[];
    }
    const initiativeExcludedValues=new Set(['sin clasificar','no especificado','transversal','documentacion']);
    const initiativeNameEquals=(value,target)=>Boolean(String(value||'').trim()&&String(target||'').trim()&&normalizeText(value)===normalizeText(target));
    function dedupeInitiativeNames(values){
      const seen=new Set(),out=[];
      (values||[]).map(value=>String(value||'').trim()).filter(Boolean).forEach(value=>{const key=normalizeText(value);if(initiativeExcludedValues.has(key)||seen.has(key))return;seen.add(key);out.push(value);});
      return out;
    }
    function replaceInitiativeList(values,oldName,newName){
      return dedupeInitiativeNames((Array.isArray(values)?values:[]).map(value=>initiativeNameEquals(value,oldName)?newName:value));
    }
    function syncInsightRegisterInitiative(item){
      if(!item?.insightRegister||!Array.isArray(item.insightRegister.rows))return false;
      const initiatives=dedupeInitiativeNames([...(item.initiatives||[]),item.initiative]);
      const primary=String(item.initiative||initiatives[0]||'').trim();
      if(!primary)return false;
      let changed=false;
      item.insightRegister.rows.forEach(row=>{
        if(!row||typeof row!=='object')return;
        if(row.initiative!==primary){row.initiative=primary;changed=true;}
        if(Array.isArray(row.initiatives)){const next=[...initiatives];if(JSON.stringify(row.initiatives)!==JSON.stringify(next)){row.initiatives=next;changed=true;}}
        if(row.extraFields&&typeof row.extraFields==='object'){
          Object.keys(row.extraFields).forEach(key=>{if(['iniciativa','initiative'].includes(normalizeText(key))&&row.extraFields[key]!==primary){row.extraFields[key]=primary;changed=true;}});
        }
      });
      return changed;
    }
    function updateInitiativeMetadata(target,oldName,newName){
      if(!target||typeof target!=='object')return false;
      let changed=false;
      if(initiativeNameEquals(target.initiative,oldName)){target.initiative=newName;changed=true;}
      if(Array.isArray(target.initiatives)){const next=replaceInitiativeList(target.initiatives,oldName,newName);if(JSON.stringify(next)!==JSON.stringify(target.initiatives)){target.initiatives=next;changed=true;}}
      if(target.documentProfile&&initiativeNameEquals(target.documentProfile.initiative,oldName)){target.documentProfile.initiative=newName;changed=true;}
      if(target.metadata&&initiativeNameEquals(target.metadata.initiative,oldName)){target.metadata.initiative=newName;changed=true;}
      if(target.analysis?.metadata&&initiativeNameEquals(target.analysis.metadata.initiative,oldName)){target.analysis.metadata.initiative=newName;changed=true;}
      if(target.insightRegister&&Array.isArray(target.insightRegister.rows)){
        target.insightRegister.rows.forEach(row=>{
          if(!row||typeof row!=='object')return;
          if(initiativeNameEquals(row.initiative,oldName)){row.initiative=newName;changed=true;}
          if(Array.isArray(row.initiatives)){const next=replaceInitiativeList(row.initiatives,oldName,newName);if(JSON.stringify(next)!==JSON.stringify(row.initiatives)){row.initiatives=next;changed=true;}}
          if(row.extraFields&&typeof row.extraFields==='object'){Object.keys(row.extraFields).forEach(key=>{if(['iniciativa','initiative'].includes(normalizeText(key))&&initiativeNameEquals(row.extraFields[key],oldName)){row.extraFields[key]=newName;changed=true;}});}
        });
      }
      return changed;
    }
    function renameInitiativeEverywhere(oldName,newName){
      oldName=String(oldName||'').trim();newName=String(newName||'').trim();
      if(!oldName||!newName||initiativeNameEquals(oldName,newName))return 0;
      let changed=0;
      [sourceItems,learnings,evidenceItems,experiments,patternItems,knowledgeUnits,openQuestionItems].forEach(items=>(items||[]).forEach(item=>{if(updateInitiativeMetadata(item,oldName,newName))changed++;}));
      sourceItems.forEach(item=>syncInsightRegisterInitiative(item));
      return changed;
    }
    function reconcileInitiativesFromSources(){
      const liveSourceInitiatives=dedupeInitiativeNames(sourceItems.flatMap(item=>[...(item.initiatives||[]),item.initiative]));
      const liveKeys=new Set(liveSourceInitiatives.map(normalizeText));
      let changed=false;
      learnings.forEach(item=>{
        const linked=dedupeInitiativeNames(catalogSources(item).flatMap(source=>[...(source.initiatives||[]),source.initiative]));
        if(!linked.length)return;
        const current=dedupeInitiativeNames([...(item.initiatives||[]),item.initiative]);
        const orphaned=current.filter(value=>!liveKeys.has(normalizeText(value)));
        if(!orphaned.length)return;
        const next=dedupeInitiativeNames([...current.filter(value=>liveKeys.has(normalizeText(value))),...linked]);
        if(!next.length)return;
        if(JSON.stringify(item.initiatives||[])!==JSON.stringify(next)){item.initiatives=next;changed=true;}
        if(!next.some(value=>initiativeNameEquals(value,item.initiative))){item.initiative=next[0];changed=true;}
      });
      return changed;
    }
    function refreshCatalogMetadata(){
      let changed=false;
      sourceItems.forEach(item=>{
        normalizeCatalogContext(item);
        if(normalizeDocumentApplicationMetadata(item))changed=true;
        item.initiatives=initiativesOf(item);
        if(syncInsightRegisterInitiative(item))changed=true;
        if(item.supportingDocument)item.status='Disponible';
        if(item.simpleKnowledgeActive&&item.insightRegister)item.status='Procesado';
      });
      [learnings,evidenceItems,experiments,patternItems].forEach(items=>items.forEach(item=>{
        normalizeCatalogContext(item);
        item.initiatives=initiativesOf(item);
      }));
      if(reconcileInitiativesFromSources())changed=true;
      return changed;
    }
    const initiativeMetadataRecovered=refreshCatalogMetadata();
    if(initiativeMetadataRecovered)safeStorageWrite({project:false});

    // Fuente única de vocabularios controlados para toda UX Intelligence.
    const UX_CONTROLLED_VOCABULARIES=Object.freeze({
      device:Object.freeze(['Desktop','Mobile','Desktop + mobile','App','No especificado']),
      market:Object.freeze(['Alemania','Argentina','Bolivia','Brasil','Bélgica','Chile','Colombia','Ecuador','España','Estados Unidos','Francia','Israel','Italia','México','Panamá','Paraguay','Países Bajos','Perú','Portugal','Reino Unido','República Dominicana','Suiza','Turquía','Uruguay','World (EUR)','World (USD)','Multimercado','No especificado']),
      region:Object.freeze(['España','Europa','África','Oriente Medio','Norteamérica','Caribe','América Central','América del Sur','Global','No especificado']),
      origin:Object.freeze(['Auditoría UX','Test con usuarios','Análisis conductual','Análisis de datos','Experimentación / CRO','Benchmark','Desk research','Voz del cliente / CSAT','Síntesis estratégica']),
      recordType:Object.freeze(['Hecho','Evidencia interna','Evidencia externa','Hipótesis','Inferencia','Oportunidad','Restricción','Decisión','Pregunta abierta']),
      impactUx:Object.freeze(['Alto','Medio','Bajo']),
      uxLine:Object.freeze(["Accesibilidad","Arquitectura de información","Claridad y comprensión","Consistencia","Contenido y copy","Control y autonomía","Encontrabilidad","Feedback y estado","Flujo y secuenciación","Fricción y eficiencia","Jerarquía visual","Navegación","Orientación y ayuda","Personalización"])
    });
    globalThis.UXControlledVocabulary=UX_CONTROLLED_VOCABULARIES;

    function optionSet(select,allLabel,values,current='Todos'){
      const unique=[...new Set(values.filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,'es'));
      const allValue=select.options[0]?.value==='Todas'?'Todas':'Todos';
      // UI copy is deliberately uniform across all filters: the field label already provides context.
      const visibleAllLabel='Todos';
      select.innerHTML=`<option value="${allValue}">${escapeHtml(visibleAllLabel)}</option>${unique.map(value=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(value)}">${escapeHtml(value)}</option>`).join('')}`;
      select.value=unique.includes(current)?current:allValue;
}
    function refreshInitiativeFilters(oldName='',newName=''){
      const sharedInitiatives=sourceItems.flatMap(item=>[...(item.initiatives||[]),item.initiative]).filter(value=>value&&!['Sin clasificar','No especificado','Documentación'].includes(value));
      ['learning','evidence','experiment','pattern','source'].forEach(group=>{
        const select=document.getElementById(`${group}-initiative`);if(!select)return;
        const items=catalogCollection(group);
        const values=[...items.flatMap(item=>item.initiatives||[]),...sharedInitiatives];
        const current=initiativeNameEquals(select.value,oldName)?newName:select.value;
        optionSet(select,'Todos',values,current);
      });
      globalThis.__uxSimpleExploreRefresh?.();
    }
    function learningInitiativeScope(){
      const selected=document.getElementById('learning-initiative')?.value||'Todos';
      const exact=(actual,target)=>['Todos','Todas',''].includes(target)||normalizeText(actual||'')===normalizeText(target);
      return learnings.filter(item=>{
        if(['Todos','Todas',''].includes(selected))return true;
        return governedLearningInitiatives(item).some(value=>exact(value,selected));
      });
    }

    function learningProductValues(){
      return [...new Set(learningInitiativeScope()
        .map(item=>governedTaxonomyRecord(item).product)
        .filter(Boolean))]
        .sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,'es'));
    }

    function hierarchyValues(product='Todos',funnel='Todos',minifunnel='Todos',group=''){
      const exact=(actual,selected)=>['Todos','Todas',''].includes(selected)||normalizeText(actual||'')===normalizeText(selected);
      const sort=(values)=>[...new Set(values.filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,'es'));

      // Learnings: la iniciativa seleccionada limita los contextos disponibles.
      // Se usan únicamente valores ya normalizados por la taxonomía gobernada.
      if(group==='learning'){
        const scoped=learningInitiativeScope();
        const contexts=scoped.map(item=>({item,current:governedTaxonomyRecord(item)}));

        const inProduct=contexts.filter(({current})=>exact(current.product,product));
        const funnels=sort(inProduct.map(({current})=>current.funnel));

        const inFunnel=inProduct.filter(({current})=>exact(current.funnel,funnel));
        const minifunnels=sort(inFunnel.map(({current})=>current.minifunnel));

        const hasProduct=!['Todos','Todas',''].includes(product);
        const hasFunnel=!['Todos','Todas',''].includes(funnel);
        const hasMinifunnel=!['Todos','Todas',''].includes(minifunnel);

        let componentItems=[];
        if(hasMinifunnel){
          componentItems=inFunnel.filter(({current})=>exact(current.minifunnel,minifunnel));
        }else if(hasFunnel){
          componentItems=inFunnel.filter(({current})=>!String(current.minifunnel||'').trim());
        }else if(hasProduct&&normalizeText(product)==='home'){
          componentItems=inProduct.filter(({current})=>!String(current.funnel||'').trim()&&!String(current.minifunnel||'').trim());
        }

        const components=sort(componentItems.flatMap(({item})=>governedComponentNames(item)));
        return {funnels,minifunnels,components};
      }

      // Resto de pantallas: comportamiento canónico existente.
      const contexts=taxonomyContexts();
      const inProduct=contexts.filter(ctx=>exact(ctx.product,product));
      const funnels=sort(inProduct.map(ctx=>ctx.funnel));

      const inFunnel=inProduct.filter(ctx=>exact(ctx.funnel,funnel));
      const minifunnels=sort(inFunnel.map(ctx=>ctx.minifunnel));

      const hasProduct=!['Todos','Todas',''].includes(product);
      const hasFunnel=!['Todos','Todas',''].includes(funnel);
      const hasMinifunnel=!['Todos','Todas',''].includes(minifunnel);

      let componentContexts=[];
      if(hasMinifunnel){
        componentContexts=inFunnel.filter(ctx=>exact(ctx.minifunnel,minifunnel));
      }else if(hasFunnel){
        componentContexts=inFunnel.filter(ctx=>!String(ctx.minifunnel||'').trim());
      }else if(hasProduct && normalizeText(product)==='home'){
        componentContexts=inProduct.filter(ctx=>!String(ctx.funnel||'').trim()&&!String(ctx.minifunnel||'').trim());
      }
      const canonical=componentContexts.map(ctx=>ctx.component).filter(Boolean);
      return {funnels,minifunnels,components:sort(canonical)};
    }

    const renderers={explore:renderExplore,learning:renderLearnings,evidence:renderEvidence,experiment:renderExperiments,pattern:renderPatterns,source:renderSources};
    function refreshHierarchy(group,changed){
      const product=document.getElementById(`${group}-product`);
      const funnel=document.getElementById(`${group}-funnel`);
      const minifunnel=document.getElementById(`${group}-minifunnel`);
      const component=document.getElementById(`${group}-component`);
      if(!product||!component)return;

      // En Learnings, Iniciativa limita también Producto.
      // Nunca conservar una selección de producto que ya no pertenezca
      // a la iniciativa seleccionada.
      if(group==='learning'){
        const validProducts=learningProductValues();
        const currentProduct=product.value;
        optionSet(product,'Todos',validProducts,currentProduct);
        if(!validProducts.some(value=>normalizeText(value)===normalizeText(currentProduct))){
          product.value='Todos';
        }
        product.disabled=validProducts.length===0;
      }

      if(changed==='product'){
        if(funnel)funnel.value='Todos';
        if(minifunnel)minifunnel.value='Todos';
        component.value='Todos';
      }else if(changed==='funnel'){
        if(minifunnel)minifunnel.value='Todos';
        component.value='Todos';
      }else if(changed==='minifunnel'){
        component.value='Todos';
      }

      let values=hierarchyValues(product.value,'Todos','Todos',group);
      if(funnel){
        optionSet(funnel,'Todos',values.funnels,funnel.value);
        funnel.disabled=normalizeText(product.value)==='home'||values.funnels.length===0;
      }

      values=hierarchyValues(product.value,funnel?.value||'Todos','Todos',group);
      if(minifunnel){
        optionSet(minifunnel,'Todos',values.minifunnels,minifunnel.value);
        minifunnel.disabled=!funnel||['Todos','Todas'].includes(funnel.value)||values.minifunnels.length===0;
      }

      values=hierarchyValues(product.value,funnel?.value||'Todos',minifunnel?.value||'Todos',group);
      optionSet(component,'Todos',values.components,component.value);
      const productSelected=!['Todos','Todas',''].includes(product.value);
      const funnelSelected=funnel&&!['Todos','Todas',''].includes(funnel.value);
      const homeSelected=normalizeText(product.value)==='home';
      component.disabled=!productSelected||(!homeSelected&&!funnelSelected)||values.components.length===0;

      renderers[group]();
      updateActiveFilters(group);
    }
    document.querySelectorAll('.filter-system').forEach(system=>{
      const group=system.dataset.filterGroup;
      system.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`<div class="active-filter-bar" data-active-filters="${group}" hidden></div>`);
      optionSet(
        system.querySelector('.hierarchy-product'),
        'Todos',
        group==='learning'?learningProductValues():[...new Set(taxonomyContexts().map(ctx=>ctx.product).filter(Boolean))]
      );
      const items=catalogCollection(group);
      const sharedInitiatives=sourceItems.flatMap(item=>[...(item.initiatives||[]),item.initiative]).filter(value=>value&&!['Sin clasificar','No especificado','Documentación'].includes(value));
      const dimensions={initiative:{label:'Todos',values:[...items.flatMap(item=>item.initiatives||[]),...sharedInitiatives]},segment:{label:'Todos',values:items.map(item=>item.segment)},login:{label:'Todos',values:items.map(item=>item.loginStatus||item.login)}};
      Object.entries(dimensions).forEach(([key,{label,values}])=>{const select=document.getElementById(`${group}-${key}`);if(select)optionSet(select,label,values);});

      // Vocabularios controlados: una misma fuente para todas las pantallas.
      const controlledSelects={
        device:UX_CONTROLLED_VOCABULARIES.device,
        market:UX_CONTROLLED_VOCABULARIES.market,
        impact:UX_CONTROLLED_VOCABULARIES.impactUx
      };
      Object.entries(controlledSelects).forEach(([key,values])=>{
        const select=document.getElementById(`${group}-${key}`);
        if(select)optionSet(select,'Todos',values,select.value||'Todos');
      });

      if(group==='explore'){
        optionSet(document.getElementById('explore-evidence-kind'),'Todos',[...new Set(evidenceRecords().map(item=>humanKnowledgeLabel(item.kind||item.type)))],document.getElementById('explore-evidence-kind').value);
        optionSet(document.getElementById('explore-date'),'Todos',[...new Set(sourceItems.map(documentDate).filter(knownMetadata))],document.getElementById('explore-date').value);
      }
      if(group==='source'){
        optionSet(document.getElementById('source-type'),'Todos',UX_CONTROLLED_VOCABULARIES.origin,document.getElementById('source-type')?.value||'Todos');
        optionSet(document.getElementById('source-date'),'Todos',[...new Set(sourceItems.map(documentDate).filter(knownMetadata))],document.getElementById('source-date')?.value||'Todos');
      }

      refreshHierarchy(group);
      system.querySelectorAll('[data-hierarchy-level]').forEach(select=>{
        const level=select.dataset.hierarchyLevel;
        select.addEventListener('change',()=>refreshHierarchy(group,level));
      });
      system.querySelectorAll('select:not([data-hierarchy-level])').forEach(select=>select.addEventListener('change',()=>{
        if(group==='learning'&&select.id==='learning-initiative'){
          const product=document.getElementById('learning-product');
          const funnel=document.getElementById('learning-funnel');
          const minifunnel=document.getElementById('learning-minifunnel');
          const component=document.getElementById('learning-component');
          if(product)optionSet(product,'Todos',learningProductValues(),'Todos');
          if(funnel)funnel.value='Todos';
          if(minifunnel)minifunnel.value='Todos';
          if(component)component.value='Todos';
          refreshHierarchy('learning');
          return;
        }
        renderers[group]();
        updateActiveFilters(group);
      }));
      system.querySelectorAll('input[type="date"]').forEach(input=>input.addEventListener('change',()=>{renderers[group]();updateActiveFilters(group);}));
      // Visible copy is intentionally uniform across Explore, Learnings and Documents.
      system.querySelectorAll('option[value="Todos"],option[value="Todas"]').forEach(option=>{option.textContent='Todos';});
      const more=system.querySelector('.more-filter-button');
      const secondary=system.querySelector('.secondary-filters');
      more.addEventListener('click',()=>{const open=more.getAttribute('aria-expanded')==='true';more.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(!open));secondary.hidden=open;more.textContent=open?'Más filtros':'Menos filtros';});
    });

    function governedLearningInitiatives(item){
      return [...new Set([...(item?.initiatives||[]),item?.initiative].filter(value=>value&&!['Sin clasificar','No especificado','Transversal'].includes(value)))];
    }

    function hierarchyFilter(items,group){
      const product=document.getElementById(`${group}-product`)?.value||'Todos';
      const funnel=document.getElementById(`${group}-funnel`)?.value||'Todos';
      const minifunnel=document.getElementById(`${group}-minifunnel`)?.value||'Todos';
      const component=document.getElementById(`${group}-component`)?.value||'Todos';
      const initiative=document.getElementById(`${group}-initiative`)?.value||'Todos';
      const exact=(actual,selected)=>['Todos','Todas',''].includes(selected)||normalizeText(actual||'')===normalizeText(selected);

      const contextMatches=item=>{
        const current=governedTaxonomyRecord(item);
        return exact(current.product,product)&&
          exact(current.funnel,funnel)&&
          exact(current.minifunnel,minifunnel)&&
          (['Todos','Todas'].includes(component)||componentsOf(item).some(value=>exact(value,component)));
      };

      return items.filter(item=>{
        const itemInitiatives=group==='learning'?governedLearningInitiatives(item):[...(item.initiatives||[]),item.initiative].filter(Boolean);
        const initiativeMatch=['Todos','Todas'].includes(initiative)||itemInitiatives.some(value=>exact(value,initiative));
        if(!initiativeMatch)return false;

        // Un documento puede abarcar varios contextos. Basta con que uno de sus
        // registros estructurados coincida con el filtro seleccionado.
        if(group==='source'){
          const candidates=[item,...(item?.insightRegister?.rows||[])];
          return candidates.some(contextMatches);
        }
        return contextMatches(item);
      });
    }

    function updateActiveFilters(group){
      const system=document.querySelector(`[data-filter-group="${group}"]`);
      const bar=system?.querySelector('[data-active-filters]');if(!bar)return;
      const activeSelects=[...system.querySelectorAll('select:not([data-filter-sort])')].filter(select=>!['Todos','Todas'].includes(select.value)).map(select=>({id:select.id,label:select.options[select.selectedIndex]?.textContent||select.value}));
      const activeDates=[...system.querySelectorAll('input[type="date"]')].filter(input=>input.value).map(input=>{const field=input.closest('label')?.childNodes?.[0]?.textContent?.trim()||'Fecha';return {id:input.id,label:`${field}: ${input.value.split('-').reverse().join('/')}`};});
      const active=[...activeSelects,...activeDates];
      bar.hidden=!active.length;
      bar.innerHTML=active.length?`<span class="active-filter-label">Filtros activos</span>${active.map(item=>`<button class="filter-token" type="button" aria-label="Eliminar filtro ${escapeHtml(item.label)}" data-remove-filter="${item.id}">${escapeHtml(item.label)}<span aria-hidden="true">×</span></button>`).join('')}<button class="clear-filter-button" type="button" data-clear-filters="${group}">Limpiar filtros</button>`:'';
    }
    function resetFilterGroup(group){
      const system=document.querySelector(`[data-filter-group="${group}"]`);if(!system)return;
      system.querySelectorAll('select').forEach(select=>{if(select.hasAttribute('data-filter-sort'))select.value='newest';else select.value=[...select.options].some(option=>option.value==='Todos')?'Todos':'Todas';});
      system.querySelectorAll('input[type="date"]').forEach(input=>{input.value='';input.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));});
      refreshHierarchy(group,'product');
    }
    document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
      const taxonomyToggle=event.target.closest('[data-taxonomy-toggle]');if(taxonomyToggle){taxonomyTreeExpanded=!taxonomyTreeExpanded;renderTaxonomy();return;}
      const taxonomyTermButton=event.target.closest('[data-taxonomy-term]');if(taxonomyTermButton){activeTaxonomyTermId=taxonomyTermButton.dataset.taxonomyTerm;renderTaxonomy();return;}
      const taxonomyEdit=event.target.closest('[data-taxonomy-edit]');
      if(taxonomyEdit){
        // En Learnings la clasificación gobernada termina en Componente.
        // Elemento es metadato auxiliar y no debe bloquear el guardado.
        if(taxonomyEdit.dataset.taxonomyEdit==='learning'){
          openLearningComponentEditor(taxonomyEdit.dataset.taxonomyId);
          return;
        }
        openTaxonomyEditor(taxonomyEdit.dataset.taxonomyEdit,taxonomyEdit.dataset.taxonomyId);
        return;
      }
      const taxonomyProposal=event.target.closest('[data-propose-taxonomy]');if(taxonomyProposal){proposeTaxonomyTerm().then(()=>{showToast('Propuesta guardada','El término queda pendiente de validación y no se usa como canon.');renderTaxonomy();}).catch(error=>showToast('No se ha guardado la propuesta',error.message));return;}
      const remove=event.target.closest('[data-remove-filter]');
      if(remove){const control=document.getElementById(remove.dataset.removeFilter);if(!control)return;const group=control.closest('.filter-system').dataset.filterGroup;if(control.matches('input[type="date"]')){control.value='';control.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));renderers[group]();updateActiveFilters(group);return;}control.value=[...control.options].some(option=>option.value==='Todos')?'Todos':'Todas';const level=control.dataset.hierarchyLevel||null;if(level)refreshHierarchy(group,level);else{renderers[group]();updateActiveFilters(group);}return;}
      const clear=event.target.closest('[data-clear-filters]');
      if(clear)resetFilterGroup(clear.dataset.clearFilters);
    });


    function sameProductContext(item,context){
      const selected=componentsOf(context);
      return item.product===context.product&&(!selected.length||selected.some(component=>componentsOf(item).includes(component)));
    }
    function homeQuestions(){
      // La Home no es un backlog de todas las preguntas históricas.
      // Muestra únicamente preguntas de learnings consolidados, una por learning,
      // priorizadas y con diversidad de iniciativas.
      const rank={Alto:0,Medio:1,Bajo:2};
      const candidates=learnings
        .filter(learning=>Array.isArray(learning.open)&&learning.open.some(value=>String(value||'').trim()))
        .map(learning=>{
          const question=(learning.open||[]).map(value=>String(value||'').trim()).find(Boolean)||'';
          const initiative=(governedLearningInitiatives(learning)[0]||learning.initiative||'Sin iniciativa').trim();
          const updated=Date.parse(learning.updatedAt||learning.createdAt||'')||0;
          return {
            text:question,
            item:learning,
            action:`data-learning="${escapeHtml(learning.id)}"`,
            sources:catalogSources(learning),
            initiative,
            impactRank:rank[String(learning.impactUx||'').trim()]??3,
            updated
          };
        })
        .filter(candidate=>candidate.text)
        .sort((a,b)=>a.impactRank-b.impactRank||b.updated-a.updated||a.text.localeCompare(b.text,'es'));

      const selected=[];
      const perInitiative=new Map();
      for(const candidate of candidates){
        if(selected.some(existing=>semanticSimilarity(existing.text,candidate.text)>=.72))continue;
        const key=normalizeText(candidate.initiative||'Sin iniciativa');
        const used=perInitiative.get(key)||0;
        if(used>=2)continue;
        selected.push(candidate);
        perInitiative.set(key,used+1);
        if(selected.length>=8)break;
      }
      return selected;
    }
    function knowledgeContexts(){
      const keys=new Map();
      learnings.forEach(item=>{const components=componentsOf(item);if(item.product&&components.length){const key=JSON.stringify([item.product,item.funnel||'',item.minifunnel||'',...components]);if(!keys.has(key))keys.set(key,{key,product:item.product,funnel:item.funnel||'',minifunnel:item.minifunnel||'',component:components.length===1?components[0]:'',components});}});
      return [...keys.values()].map(context=>{
        const items=learnings.filter(item=>sameProductContext(item,context));
        return {...context,learnings:items,evidence:evidenceItems.filter(item=>sameProductContext(item,context)),experiments:experiments.filter(item=>sameProductContext(item,context)),patterns:patternItems.filter(item=>sameProductContext(item,context)),questions:homeQuestions().filter(question=>sameProductContext(question.item,context))};
      }).sort((a,b)=>b.learnings.length-a.learnings.length);
    }
    function goToKnowledgeContext(context){
      // Contextos de búsqueda/Home deben abrir el explorador actual y conservar
      // toda la jerarquía seleccionada: Producto → Funnel → Minifunnel → Componente.
      goToSimpleExploreContext(context||{});
    }
    function goToSimpleExploreContext(context){
      showPanel('explore');
      const fields=['initiative','product','funnel','minifunnel','component','device','market','region','origin','recordType','impactUx'];
      fields.forEach(field=>{
        const idMap={recordType:'type',impactUx:'impact'};
        const id=`uxs-${idMap[field]||field}`;
        const select=document.getElementById(id);
        if(!select)return;
        select.value='';
        select.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));
      });
      ['product','funnel','minifunnel','component'].forEach(field=>{
        const value=String(context[field]||'').trim();
        if(!value)return;
        const select=document.getElementById(`uxs-${field}`);
        if(select&&[...select.options].some(option=>normalizeText(option.value)===normalizeText(value))){
          const matched=[...select.options].find(option=>normalizeText(option.value)===normalizeText(value));
          select.value=matched.value;
          select.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));
        }
      });
    }

    function goToSimpleExploreInitiative(initiative){
      showPanel('explore');
      const initiativeSelect=document.getElementById('uxs-initiative');
      if(!initiativeSelect)return;
      const matched=[...initiativeSelect.options].find(option=>normalizeText(option.value)===normalizeText(initiative));
      if(!matched)return;
      initiativeSelect.value=matched.value;
      initiativeSelect.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));
    }

    function openHomeQuestions(){
      const questions=homeQuestions();
      openDrawer('<div class="drawer-kicker">Qué falta saber</div><h2>Preguntas abiertas prioritarias</h2><p class="drawer-lead">Cuestiones aún abiertas dentro de learnings consolidados. Orientan próximos análisis, research o experimentación sin mezclar preguntas históricas de las fuentes.</p>',
        `<div class="relation-list">${questions.map(question=>`<div class="relation-row"><div class="relation-copy"><strong>${escapeHtml(question.text)}</strong><span>${escapeHtml(pathOf(question.item))}</span></div><button class="relation-action" type="button" ${question.action}>Ver contexto</button><span></span></div>`).join('')}</div>`);
    }
    document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
      const coverageSwitch=event.target.closest('[data-home-coverage-view]');
      if(coverageSwitch){
        const coverageTarget=document.getElementById('ux-home-coverage-list');
        const nextView=coverageSwitch.dataset.homeCoverageView;
        if(coverageTarget&&(nextView==='initiatives'||nextView==='products')){
          coverageTarget.dataset.coverageView=nextView;
          renderOverview();
        }
        return;
      }
      const homePager=event.target.closest('[data-home-page]');
      if(homePager){
        const key=String(homePager.dataset.homePage||'');
        const delta=Number(homePager.dataset.homePageDelta||0);
        if(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(homeDashboardState,key)&&delta){homeDashboardState[key]=Math.max(0,Number(homeDashboardState[key]||0)+delta);renderOverview();}
        return;
      }
      if(event.target.closest('[data-home-all-questions]'))openHomeQuestions();
      const homeInitiative=event.target.closest('[data-home-initiative]');
      if(homeInitiative){goToSimpleExploreInitiative(homeInitiative.dataset.homeInitiative);return;}
      const area=event.target.closest('[data-home-area]');
      if(area)goToSimpleExploreContext({
        product:area.dataset.product||'',
        funnel:area.dataset.funnel||'',
        minifunnel:area.dataset.minifunnel||'',
        screen:area.dataset.screen||'',
        component:area.dataset.component||''
      });
      const recent=event.target.closest('[data-home-doc]');
      if(recent){showPanel('sources');openSource(recent.dataset.homeDoc);}
    });
    function realActivity(){
      const events=[];
      sourceItems.forEach(item=>{
        const published=item.analysis?.publication;
        if(published?.status==='published'&&published.publishedAt)events.push({date:published.publishedAt,title:item.name,description:'Conocimiento publicado',action:`data-doc-action="open" data-id="${escapeHtml(item.id)}"`});
        else if(item.ingestedAt)events.push({date:item.ingestedAt,title:item.name,description:'Documento añadido',action:`data-doc-action="open" data-id="${escapeHtml(item.id)}"`});
      });
      learnings.forEach(item=>{
        // Use recorded publication operations, never the report's date as an event.
        (item.publicationHistory||[]).forEach(entry=>{if(entry.publishedAt)events.push({date:entry.publishedAt,title:item.title,description:entry.decision,action:`data-learning="${escapeHtml(item.id)}"`});});
        if(item.createdAt&&!item.publicationHistory?.length)events.push({date:item.createdAt,title:item.title,description:'Learning creado',action:`data-learning="${escapeHtml(item.id)}"`});
      });
      return events.filter(event=>/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T/.test(event.date)&&Number.isFinite(Date.parse(event.date))).sort((a,b)=>Date.parse(b.date)-Date.parse(a.date)).slice(0,4);
    }
    // Presentation-only counts from the current library; no inferred coverage score.
    function homeCoverageMarkup(products,items){
      const counts=products.map(product=>({product,count:new Set(items.filter(item=>item.product===product).map(item=>item.id)).size}));
      const total=new Set(items.map(item=>item.id)).size,max=Math.max(1,...counts.map(item=>item.count));
      const unassigned=new Set(items.filter(item=>!products.includes(item.product)).map(item=>item.id)).size;
      return `<figure class="home-coverage-panel" aria-labelledby="home-coverage-title" aria-describedby="home-coverage-note"><figcaption class="section-head"><span class="home-feature-icon" aria-hidden="true"><span class="home-glyph home-glyph-chart"></span></span><div><h2 id="home-coverage-title">Distribución del conocimiento consolidado por producto</h2><p>Visualiza cómo se reparte el conocimiento actualmente disponible entre productos.</p></div></figcaption><div class="home-coverage-body"><dl class="home-coverage-total"><div><dt>Learnings registrados</dt><dd>${total}</dd></div></dl><dl class="home-coverage-bars" aria-label="Learnings registrados por producto">${counts.map(({product,count})=>`<div class="home-coverage-row"><dt>${escapeHtml(product)}</dt><dd><span class="home-coverage-track" aria-hidden="true"><span style="width:${count/max*100}%"></span></span><strong>${count}</strong></dd></div>`).join('')}</dl></div><p class="home-coverage-note" id="home-coverage-note">El volumen describe presencia de conocimiento en la biblioteca; no equivale a validación ni a un porcentaje de cobertura.${unassigned?` ${unassigned} ${unassigned===1?'learning sin producto representado en el gráfico':'learnings sin producto representado en el gráfico'}.`:''}</p></figure>`;
    }
    const homeDashboardState={recent:0,consolidation:0,gaps:0,areas:0};
    function homePage(items,key,pageSize){
      const pages=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(items.length/pageSize));
      const page=Math.max(0,Math.min(Number(homeDashboardState[key]||0),pages-1));
      homeDashboardState[key]=page;
      return {items:items.slice(page*pageSize,page*pageSize+pageSize),page,pages,total:items.length};
    }
    function homePagerMarkup(key,page,pages,total){
      if(pages<=1)return '';
      return `<div class="ux-home-pager"><span>${total} elementos · página ${page+1} de ${pages}</span><span class="ux-home-pager-controls"><button class="ux-home-page-button" type="button" data-home-page="${key}" data-home-page-delta="-1" aria-label="Página anterior" ${page===0?'disabled':''}>←</button><button class="ux-home-page-button" type="button" data-home-page="${key}" data-home-page-delta="1" aria-label="Página siguiente" ${page===pages-1?'disabled':''}>→</button></span></div>`;
    }

    function renderOverview(){
      try{
        const activeExcel=sourceItems.filter(item=>item?.simpleKnowledgeActive&&item?.insightRegister?.rows);
        const sourceRows=activeExcel.flatMap(doc=>(doc.insightRegister?.rows||[]).map(row=>({...row,__docId:doc.id,__docName:doc.name||doc.originalName||''})));
        const sharedRecords=(()=>{
          try{
            const current=globalThis.UXSimpleKnowledge?.currentNormalizedRecords?.();
            if(Array.isArray(current)&&current.length)return current;
          }catch(_){}
          return sourceRows.map(row=>{
            let normalized=row;
            try{normalized=globalThis.UXGovernedTaxonomy?.normalizeRecord?.(row)||row;}catch(_){}
            let impact=String(normalized?.impactUx||row?.impactUx||'').trim();
            try{impact=globalThis.UXReviewedImpact?.forInsight?.(row?.insight||'')||impact;}catch(_){}
            return {...normalized,impactUx:impact,docId:row.__docId||'',docName:row.__docName||''};
          });
        })();
        const pdfItems=sourceItems.filter(item=>item?.supportingDocument);
        const ignoredInitiatives=new Set(['Sin clasificar','Documentación','No especificado']);
        const initiatives=[...new Set([
          ...sourceItems.flatMap(item=>[item.initiative,...(item.initiatives||[])]),
          ...learnings.flatMap(item=>[item.initiative,...(item.initiatives||[])])
        ].map(value=>String(value||'').trim()).filter(value=>value&&!ignoredInitiatives.has(value)))].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,'es'));
        const products=[...new Set(sharedRecords.map(row=>String(row.product||'').trim()).filter(Boolean))];
        document.getElementById('ux-base-learnings')?.replaceChildren(document.createTextNode(String(learnings.length)));
        document.getElementById('ux-base-insights')?.replaceChildren(document.createTextNode(String(sharedRecords.length)));
        document.getElementById('ux-base-initiatives')?.replaceChildren(document.createTextNode(String(initiatives.length)));
        document.getElementById('ux-base-documents')?.replaceChildren(document.createTextNode(String(sourceItems.length)));
        document.getElementById('ux-base-document-split')?.replaceChildren(document.createTextNode(`${activeExcel.length} Excel revisados · ${pdfItems.length} PDF de apoyo`));

        // Home v6-30: surface the knowledge itself first, then curated gaps, coverage and recent sources.
        const impactRank={Alto:0,Medio:1,Bajo:2};

        // Conocimiento consolidado: all learnings are reachable from Home, balanced by initiative and paginated.
        const learningTarget=document.getElementById('ux-home-learning-list');
        if(learningTarget){
          const learningGroups=new Map();
          learnings.forEach(learning=>{
            const initiative=(governedLearningInitiatives(learning)[0]||learning.initiative||'Sin iniciativa').trim();
            if(!learningGroups.has(initiative))learningGroups.set(initiative,[]);
            learningGroups.get(initiative).push(learning);
          });
          [...learningGroups.values()].forEach(group=>group.sort((a,b)=>(impactRank[a.impactUx]??3)-(impactRank[b.impactUx]??3)||String(a.title||'').localeCompare(String(b.title||''),'es')));
          const groupNames=[...learningGroups.keys()].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,'es'));
          const orderedLearnings=[];let round=0;
          while(groupNames.some(name=>learningGroups.get(name)?.[round])){
            groupNames.forEach(name=>{const candidate=learningGroups.get(name)?.[round];if(candidate)orderedLearnings.push(candidate);});
            round++;
          }
          const learningPage=homePage(orderedLearnings,'consolidation',5);
          learningTarget.innerHTML=learningPage.total?`<div class="ux-home-learning-list">${learningPage.items.map((learning,index)=>{
            // En Resumen mostramos únicamente el journey canónico.
            // La iniciativa sigue disponible en datos y filtros, pero no forma parte de la ruta.
            const meta=pathOf(learning);
            const ordinal=String(learningPage.page*5+index+1).padStart(2,'0');
            const impact=String(learning.impactUx||'').trim();
            const impactClassName=impact==='Alto'?'is-high':impact==='Medio'?'is-medium':impact==='Bajo'?'is-low':'';
            return `<button class="ux-home-learning-row" type="button" data-learning="${escapeHtml(learning.id)}"><span class="ux-home-learning-index" aria-hidden="true">${ordinal}</span><span class="ux-home-learning-copy"><span class="ux-home-learning-title">${escapeHtml(editorialCopy(learning.title))}</span><span class="ux-home-learning-meta">${escapeHtml(meta||'Learning consolidado')}</span></span>${impact?`<span class="ux-home-learning-impact ${impactClassName}"><i aria-hidden="true"></i><span>${escapeHtml(impact)}</span></span>`:''}<span class="ux-home-learning-chevron" aria-hidden="true"></span></button>`;
          }).join('')}</div>${homePagerMarkup('consolidation',learningPage.page,learningPage.pages,learningPage.total)}`:'<p class="ux-home-empty-note">No hay learnings consolidados disponibles en este momento.</p>';
        }

        // Qué nos falta saber: only consolidated/open questions, not every row-level prompt in the source Excel.
        const gapsTarget=document.getElementById('ux-home-gaps-list');
        if(gapsTarget){
          const questions=homeQuestions().sort((a,b)=>(impactRank[a.item?.impactUx]??3)-(impactRank[b.item?.impactUx]??3)||String(a.text||'').localeCompare(String(b.text||''),'es'));
          const visibleQuestions=questions.slice(0,3);
          gapsTarget.innerHTML=visibleQuestions.length?`<div class="ux-home-question-list">${visibleQuestions.map(question=>{
            const item=question.item||{};
            const meta=pathOf(item)||question.sources?.[0]?.name||'Pregunta abierta';
            const isLearning=learnings.some(learning=>learning.id===item.id);
            const action=isLearning?`data-learning="${escapeHtml(item.id)}"`:item.documentId?`data-home-doc="${escapeHtml(item.documentId)}"`:'';
            return `<div class="ux-home-question-row"><strong>${escapeHtml(question.text)}</strong><small>${escapeHtml(meta)}</small>${action?`<button class="view-link" type="button" ${action}>Ver contexto</button>`:''}</div>`;
          }).join('')}</div>`:'<p class="ux-home-empty-note">No hay incertidumbres prioritarias abiertas en los learnings actuales.</p>';
        }

        // Cobertura: product-level summary only. Volume is descriptive, never a score of validation.
        const productKnowledgeMap=new Map();
        const ensureProductKnowledge=product=>{
          const productName=String(product||'No especificado').trim()||'No especificado';
          const key=normalizeText(productName);
          if(!productKnowledgeMap.has(key))productKnowledgeMap.set(key,{product:productName,records:0,sources:new Set(),areas:new Map()});
          return productKnowledgeMap.get(key);
        };
        const firstProductChild=(row,product)=>{
          const levels=[['funnel',row.funnel],['minifunnel',row.minifunnel],['screen',row.screen],['component',row.component]];
          for(const [field,raw] of levels){
            const value=String(raw||'').trim();
            if(!value||normalizeText(value)===normalizeText(product))continue;
            return {field,value};
          }
          return {field:'',value:''};
        };
        sharedRecords.forEach(row=>{
          const productEntry=ensureProductKnowledge(row.product);
          productEntry.records++;
          const sourceKey=String(row.docId||row.documentId||row.__docId||row.sourceFile||row.source||'').trim();
          if(sourceKey)productEntry.sources.add(sourceKey);
          const child=firstProductChild(row,productEntry.product);
