          if(!child.value)return;
          const key=`${child.field}:${normalizeText(child.value)}`;
          if(!productEntry.areas.has(key))productEntry.areas.set(key,{label:child.value,records:0});
          productEntry.areas.get(key).records++;
        });
        const canonicalHomeProducts=(()=>{
          try{
            return new Set((taxonomyProject?.state?.terms||[])
              .filter(term=>{
                const origin=String(term?.origin||'').trim();
                const governed=term?.governed===true||!/^excel revisado\s*·/i.test(origin);
                return term?.level==='product'&&term?.status==='canonical'&&governed;
              })
              .map(term=>normalizeText(term?.canonicalName||term?.name||term?.label||''))
              .filter(Boolean));
          }catch(_){return new Set();}
        })();

        const productKnowledge=[...productKnowledgeMap.values()]
          .filter(item=>canonicalHomeProducts.size?canonicalHomeProducts.has(normalizeText(item.product)):normalizeText(item.product)!=='no especificado')
          .sort((a,b)=>b.records-a.records||a.product.localeCompare(b.product,'es'));

        const coverageTarget=document.getElementById('ux-home-coverage-list');
        if(coverageTarget){
          // Iniciativas es la vista inicial. El usuario puede cambiar a Productos
          // sin alterar registros, taxonomía, filtros ni datos persistidos.
          const coverageView=coverageTarget.dataset.coverageView==='products'?'products':'initiatives';
          document.querySelectorAll('#overview .ux-home-coverage-switch [data-home-coverage-view]').forEach(button=>{
            const selected=button.dataset.homeCoverageView===coverageView;
            button.classList.toggle('is-selected',selected);
            button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(selected));
          });
          const visibleProducts=productKnowledge.slice(0,6);

          // Las iniciativas se contabilizan como iniciativas, nunca como nuevos productos.
          // Cualquier iniciativa con registros aparece automáticamente en Cobertura.
          // Se normaliza el alias histórico Usuarios SUMA sin modificar los datos originales.
          const itemInitiatives=item=>[...(Array.isArray(item?.initiatives)?item.initiatives:[]),item?.initiative]
            .map(value=>String(value||'').trim()).filter(Boolean);
          const canonicalCoverageInitiative=value=>
            normalizeText(value)===normalizeText('Usuarios SUMA')?'SUMA Frequent Flyer':value;
          const initiativeCoverageMap=new Map();
          const ensureInitiativeCoverage=name=>{
            const initiative=canonicalCoverageInitiative(name);
            const key=normalizeText(initiative);
            if(!initiative||ignoredInitiatives.has(initiative)||!key)return null;
            if(!initiativeCoverageMap.has(key))initiativeCoverageMap.set(key,{
              initiative,records:0,sources:new Set(),learnings:0
            });
            return initiativeCoverageMap.get(key);
          };
          sharedRecords.forEach(row=>{
            const uniqueNames=new Set(itemInitiatives(row).map(canonicalCoverageInitiative));
            uniqueNames.forEach(name=>{
              const coverage=ensureInitiativeCoverage(name);
              if(!coverage)return;
              coverage.records+=1;
              const sourceKey=String(row.docId||row.documentId||row.__docId||row.sourceFile||row.source||'').trim();
              if(sourceKey)coverage.sources.add(sourceKey);
            });
          });
          learnings.forEach(learning=>{
            const names=typeof governedLearningInitiatives==='function'
              ?governedLearningInitiatives(learning):itemInitiatives(learning);
            new Set(names.map(canonicalCoverageInitiative)).forEach(name=>{
              const coverage=ensureInitiativeCoverage(name);
              if(coverage)coverage.learnings+=1;
            });
          });
          const initiativeCoverages=[...initiativeCoverageMap.values()]
            .filter(item=>item.records>0)
            .sort((a,b)=>b.records-a.records||a.initiative.localeCompare(b.initiative,'es'));

          const maxCoverageRecords=Math.max(
            1,
            ...(coverageView==='products'?visibleProducts.map(item=>item.records):initiativeCoverages.map(item=>item.records))
          );
          // Iconos de Cobertura: una única familia monocroma, sin logos raster ni dependencias externas.
          // Las nuevas iniciativas utilizan automáticamente el icono genérico.
          const coverageIcon=contextName=>{
            const name=normalizeText(contextName);
            const begin='<svg class="ux-coverage-ui-icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">';
            const finish='</svg>';
            if(name.includes('home'))return begin+
              '<path d="m3 10 9-7 9 7v10a1 1 0 0 1-1 1H4a1 1 0 0 1-1-1V10Z"/>'+
              '<path d="M9 21v-7h6v7"/>'+finish;
            if(name.includes('booking')||name.includes('reserva'))return begin+
              '<rect x="4" y="7" width="16" height="14" rx="2"/>'+
              '<path d="M9 7V5a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h2a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v2"/>'+
              '<path d="M4 13h16"/>'+finish;
            return begin+
              '<rect x="3" y="3" width="7" height="7" rx="1.5"/>'+
              '<rect x="14" y="3" width="7" height="7" rx="1.5"/>'+
              '<rect x="3" y="14" width="7" height="7" rx="1.5"/>'+
              '<rect x="14" y="14" width="7" height="7" rx="1.5"/>'+finish;
          };
          const productCoverageRows=visibleProducts.map(product=>{
            const learningCount=learnings.filter(learning=>normalizeText(learning.product)===normalizeText(product.product)).length;
            const share=Math.max(8,Math.round(product.records/maxCoverageRecords*100));
            const productKey=normalizeText(product.product);
            let sortedAreas=[...product.areas.values()].sort((a,b)=>b.records-a.records).map(area=>area.label);

            // En Booking mostramos el journey canónico, no todos los valores posibles de taxonomía.
            // "Transversal" se conserva como clasificación válida, pero no forma parte de esta lectura de cobertura.
            if(productKey.includes('booking')){
              const bookingOrder=['Availability','Shopping Cart','Passengers','Extras'];
              const present=new Map(sortedAreas.map(label=>[normalizeText(label),label]));
              sortedAreas=bookingOrder.filter(label=>present.has(normalizeText(label)));
            }else{
              sortedAreas=sortedAreas.slice(0,4);
            }

            const areaPrefix=productKey.includes('booking')?'Funnels':productKey.includes('home')?'Componentes':'Áreas';
            const areaLabels=sortedAreas.join(' · ');
            return `<button class="ux-coverage-list-row" type="button" data-home-area data-product="${escapeHtml(product.product)}" data-funnel="" data-minifunnel="" data-screen="" data-component="" aria-label="Explorar ${escapeHtml(product.product)}: ${product.records} registros, ${product.sources.size} fuentes y ${learningCount} learnings"><span class="ux-coverage-list-icon" aria-hidden="true">${coverageIcon(product.product)}</span><span class="ux-coverage-list-product"><strong>${escapeHtml(product.product)}</strong>${areaLabels?`<small><span class="ux-coverage-context-label">${escapeHtml(areaPrefix)}:</span> ${escapeHtml(areaLabels)}</small>`:''}</span><span class="ux-coverage-records"><strong>${product.records}</strong><span class="ux-coverage-bar" aria-hidden="true"><i style="width:${share}%"></i></span></span><span class="ux-coverage-number is-sources">${product.sources.size}</span><span class="ux-coverage-number is-learnings">${learningCount}</span><span class="ux-coverage-arrow" aria-hidden="true">→</span></button>`;
          }).join('');

          const initiativeCoverageRows=initiativeCoverages.map(item=>{
            const share=Math.max(8,Math.round(item.records/maxCoverageRecords*100));
            const icon=coverageIcon('Iniciativa');
            return `<button class="ux-coverage-list-row is-initiative" type="button" data-home-initiative="${escapeHtml(item.initiative)}" aria-label="Explorar iniciativa ${escapeHtml(item.initiative)}: ${item.records} registros, ${item.sources.size} fuentes y ${item.learnings} learnings"><span class="ux-coverage-list-icon" aria-hidden="true">${icon}</span><span class="ux-coverage-list-product"><strong>${escapeHtml(item.initiative)}</strong><small><span class="ux-coverage-context-label">Iniciativa</span></small></span><span class="ux-coverage-records"><strong>${item.records}</strong><span class="ux-coverage-bar" aria-hidden="true"><i style="width:${share}%"></i></span></span><span class="ux-coverage-number is-sources">${item.sources.size}</span><span class="ux-coverage-number is-learnings">${item.learnings}</span><span class="ux-coverage-arrow" aria-hidden="true">→</span></button>`;
          }).join('');

          const selectedRows=coverageView==='products'?productCoverageRows:initiativeCoverageRows;
          const emptyMessage=coverageView==='products'
            ?'Todavía no hay productos con registros estructurados.'
            :'Todavía no hay iniciativas con registros estructurados.';
          coverageTarget.innerHTML=selectedRows?`<div class="ux-coverage-table" aria-label="Cobertura por ${coverageView==='products'?'productos':'iniciativas'}"><div class="ux-coverage-table-head" aria-hidden="true"><span>Contexto</span><span>Registros</span><span>Fuentes</span><span>Learnings</span></div><div class="ux-coverage-table-body">${selectedRows}</div></div>`:`<p class="ux-home-empty-note">${emptyMessage}</p>`;
        }

        const recent=[...sourceItems].sort((a,b)=>Date.parse(b.ingestedAt||0)-Date.parse(a.ingestedAt||0)).slice(0,4);
        const recentTarget=document.getElementById('ux-home-recent-list');
        if(recentTarget)recentTarget.innerHTML=recent.length?`<div class="ux-home-recent-list">${recent.map(item=>{
          const initiative=String(item.initiative||'').trim();
          const product=String(item.product||'').trim();
          const component=String(item.component||'').trim();
          const screen=String(item.screen||'').trim();
          const initiativeKey=normalizeText(initiative);
          const specificContext=[component,screen,product].find(value=>value&&normalizeText(value)!==initiativeKey)||product||initiative||'Sin clasificar';
          const contextParts=[];
          if(initiative)contextParts.push(initiative);
          if(specificContext&&normalizeText(specificContext)!==initiativeKey)contextParts.push(specificContext);
          if(!contextParts.length)contextParts.push('Sin clasificar');
          const contextLabel=contextParts.slice(0,2).join(' · ');
          return `<button class="ux-home-recent-row" type="button" data-home-doc="${escapeHtml(item.id)}"><span class="ux-home-file-kind ${item.supportingDocument?'is-pdf':'is-excel'}">${item.supportingDocument?'<img src="assets/file-icons/file-type-icon.png" alt="" aria-hidden="true">':'<img src="assets/file-icons/excel-file-icon.png" alt="" aria-hidden="true">'}</span><span class="ux-home-recent-copy"><strong>${escapeHtml(item.name)}</strong><small>${escapeHtml(contextLabel)}</small></span><span class="ux-home-recent-arrow" aria-hidden="true">→</span></button>`;
        }).join('')}</div>`:'<p class="ux-home-empty-note">No hay documentos incorporados todavía.</p>';

        const impactValues=sharedRecords.map(item=>String(item.impactUx||'').trim()).filter(value=>['Alto','Medio','Bajo'].includes(value));
        const impactTarget=document.getElementById('ux-home-impact-content');
        if(impactTarget){
          if(impactValues.length){
            const count=value=>impactValues.filter(item=>item===value).length,total=impactValues.length;
            const high=count('Alto'),medium=count('Medio'),low=count('Bajo');
            impactTarget.innerHTML=`<div class="ux-impact-chart-block"><div class="ux-impact-stack" role="img" aria-label="Impacto UX: ${high} alto, ${medium} medio y ${low} bajo"><span class="is-high" style="width:${high/total*100}%"></span><span class="is-medium" style="width:${medium/total*100}%"></span><span class="is-low" style="width:${low/total*100}%"></span></div><p class="ux-home-impact-note">${total} registros tienen Impacto UX informado.</p></div><div class="ux-impact-legend" role="list" aria-label="Detalle por nivel de impacto"><div role="listitem"><i class="is-high" aria-hidden="true"></i><span>Alto</span><strong>${high}</strong></div><div role="listitem"><i class="is-medium" aria-hidden="true"></i><span>Medio</span><strong>${medium}</strong></div><div role="listitem"><i class="is-low" aria-hidden="true"></i><span>Bajo</span><strong>${low}</strong></div></div>`;
          }else{
            impactTarget.innerHTML=`<div class="ux-impact-empty"><div class="ux-impact-empty-track"></div><strong>Impacto UX todavía no informado</strong><p>Aparecerá cuando existan registros estructurados con este dato.</p></div>`;
          }
        }

      }catch(e){
        console.error('Error en resumen simplificado',e);
      }
    }

    function inclusionTimestamp(value){
      const raw=String(value||'').trim();
      if(!raw)return NaN;
      const parsed=Date.parse(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(raw)?`${raw}T12:00:00`:raw);
      return Number.isFinite(parsed)?parsed:NaN;
    }
    function inclusionDateMatch(value,from='',to=''){
      if(!from&&!to)return true;
      const time=inclusionTimestamp(value);
      if(!Number.isFinite(time))return false;
      const fromTime=from?Date.parse(`${from}T00:00:00`):-Infinity;
      const toTime=to?Date.parse(`${to}T23:59:59.999`):Infinity;
      return time>=fromTime&&time<=toTime;
    }
    function sortByInclusion(items,direction='newest',getter=item=>item?.ingestedAt||item?.createdAt||''){
      const sign=direction==='oldest'?1:-1;
      return [...items].sort((a,b)=>{
        const ta=inclusionTimestamp(getter(a)),tb=inclusionTimestamp(getter(b));
        const aKnown=Number.isFinite(ta),bKnown=Number.isFinite(tb);
        if(aKnown&&bKnown&&ta!==tb)return sign*(ta-tb);
        if(aKnown!==bKnown)return aKnown?-1:1;
        return 0;
      });
    }

    function renderLearnings(){
      let items=hierarchyFilter(learnings,'learning');
      const device=document.getElementById('learning-device').value,market=document.getElementById('learning-market').value,impact=document.getElementById('learning-impact').value;
      const includedFrom=document.getElementById('learning-included-from')?.value||'',includedTo=document.getElementById('learning-included-to')?.value||'',sort=document.getElementById('learning-sort')?.value||'newest';
      items=items.filter(item=>matches(item.device,device)&&matches(item.market,market)&&(impact==='Todos'||item.impactUx===impact)&&inclusionDateMatch(item.createdAt,includedFrom,includedTo));
      items=sortByInclusion(items,sort,item=>item.createdAt);
      document.getElementById('learning-result').textContent=`${items.length} ${items.length===1?'learning':'learnings'}`;
      const pageItems=catalogPage(items,'learning','learnings');
      document.getElementById('learning-list').innerHTML=items.length?pageItems.map(item=>`<tr data-learning="${item.id}" tabindex="0"><td data-label="Learning"><strong>${escapeHtml(editorialCopy(item.title))}</strong>${item.know?`<span class="learning-know-snippet">${escapeHtml(item.know)}</span>`:''}<span class="sub learning-context-meta">${escapeHtml((()=>{
  const parts=[
    governedTaxonomyDisplay(item,'product'),
    governedTaxonomyDisplay(item,'funnel'),
    governedTaxonomyDisplay(item,'minifunnel')
  ].map(value=>String(value||'').trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const deduped=[];
  parts.forEach(value=>{
    if(!deduped.length||normalizeText(deduped[deduped.length-1])!==normalizeText(value))deduped.push(value);
  });
  return deduped.join(' › ');
})())}</span></td><td data-label="Componente">${escapeHtml(componentsOf(item).join(' · ')||'No especificado')}</td><td data-label="Impacto UX">${item.impactUx?`<span class="status-badge ${impactClass(item.impactUx)}">${escapeHtml(item.impactUx)}</span>`:'<span class="sub">No informado</span>'}</td><td data-label="Acción" class="row-action"><button class="view-link" type="button" data-learning="${item.id}">Ver learning</button></td></tr>`).join(''):`<tr><td colspan="4">${empty('learnings')}</td></tr>`;
      document.querySelector('.nav-button[data-panel="learnings"] .nav-count').textContent=learnings.length;
    }

    function renderEvidence(){
      let items=displayEvidenceItems(hierarchyFilter(evidenceItems,'evidence'));
      const kind=document.getElementById('evidence-kind').value,device=document.getElementById('evidence-device').value,confidence=document.getElementById('evidence-confidence').value;
      items=items.filter(item=>matches(item.kind,kind)&&matches(item.device,device)&&(confidence==='Todas'||item.confidence===confidence));
      document.getElementById('evidence-result').textContent=`${items.length} ${items.length===1?'evidencia':'evidencias'}`;
      const pageItems=catalogPage(items,'evidence','evidencias');
      document.getElementById('evidence-table').innerHTML=items.length?pageItems.map(item=>`<tr data-detail="evidence" data-id="${item.id}" tabindex="0"><td data-label="Evidencia"><strong>${escapeHtml(editorialCopy(item.name))}</strong><span class="sub">${escapeHtml(humanKnowledgeLabel(item.kind||item.type))}</span></td><td data-label="Componente">${escapeHtml(componentsOf(item).join(', ')||'No especificado')}</td><td data-label="Qué aporta">${escapeHtml(evidenceExplanation(item))}</td><td data-label="Confianza"><span class="status-badge ${confidenceClass(item.confidence)}">${escapeHtml(item.confidence)}</span></td><td data-label="Acción" class="row-action"><button class="view-link" type="button" data-detail="evidence" data-id="${item.id}">Ver evidencia</button></td></tr>`).join(''):`<tr><td colspan="6">${empty('evidencias')}</td></tr>`;
      document.querySelector('.nav-button[data-panel="evidence"] .nav-count')?.replaceChildren(document.createTextNode(String(consolidatedEvidence().length)));
    }

    function renderExperiments(){
      let items=hierarchyFilter(experiments,'experiment');
      const device=document.getElementById('experiment-device').value,outcome=document.getElementById('experiment-outcome').value,pro=document.getElementById('experiment-pro').value;
      items=items.filter(item=>matches(item.device,device)&&matches(item.outcome,outcome)&&matches(item.pro,pro));
      document.getElementById('experiment-result').textContent=`${items.length} ${items.length===1?'test':'tests'}`;
      const pageItems=catalogPage(items,'experiment','tests');
      document.getElementById('experiment-table').innerHTML=items.length?pageItems.map(item=>`<tr data-detail="experiment" data-id="${item.id}" tabindex="0"><td data-label="Test A/B"><strong>${item.title}</strong><span class="sub">${item.result}</span></td><td data-label="Producto">${escapeHtml(taxonomyDisplay(item,'product','experiment'))}</td><td data-label="Componente">${escapeHtml(componentsOf(item).join(' · ')||'No especificado')}</td><td data-label="Dispositivo">${item.device}</td><td data-label="Resultado"><span class="status-badge ${outcomeClass(item.outcome)}">${item.outcome}</span></td><td data-label="Acción" class="row-action"><button class="view-link" type="button" data-detail="experiment" data-id="${item.id}">Ver test</button></td></tr>`).join(''):`<tr><td colspan="7">${empty('tests')}</td></tr>`;
      document.querySelector('.nav-button[data-panel="experiments"] .nav-count')?.replaceChildren(document.createTextNode(String(experiments.length)));
    }

    function renderPatterns(){
      let items=hierarchyFilter(patternItems,'pattern');
      const device=document.getElementById('pattern-device').value,confidence=document.getElementById('pattern-confidence').value,status=document.getElementById('pattern-status').value;
      items=items.filter(item=>matches(item.device,device)&&(confidence==='Todas'||item.confidence===confidence)&&matches(item.status,status));
      document.getElementById('pattern-result').textContent=`${items.length} ${items.length===1?'patrón':'patrones'}`;
      const pageItems=catalogPage(items,'pattern','patrones');
      document.getElementById('pattern-list').innerHTML=items.length?pageItems.map(item=>`<tr data-detail="pattern" data-id="${item.id}" tabindex="0"><td data-label="Patrón"><strong>${escapeHtml(item.title)}</strong><span class="sub">${escapeHtml(item.description)}</span></td><td data-label="Producto">${escapeHtml(taxonomyDisplay(item,'product','pattern'))}</td><td data-label="Componente">${escapeHtml(componentsOf(item).join(' · '))}</td><td data-label="Dispositivo">${escapeHtml(item.device)}</td><td data-label="Confianza"><span class="status-badge ${confidenceClass(item.confidence)}">${escapeHtml(item.confidence)}</span></td><td data-label="Acción" class="row-action"><button class="view-link" type="button" data-detail="pattern" data-id="${item.id}">Ver patrón</button></td></tr>`).join(''):`<tr><td colspan="7">${empty('patrones')}</td></tr>`;
      document.querySelector('.nav-button[data-panel="patterns"] .nav-count')?.replaceChildren(document.createTextNode(String(patternItems.length)));
    }

    function sourceDisplayType(item){
      if(item?.supportingDocument||item?.type==='Documentación adicional')return 'PDF de apoyo';
      if(item?.insightRegister||item?.type==='Insight Register')return 'Excel revisado';
      return item?.type||'Documento';
    }
    function documentStatusLabel(status){if(status==='Pendiente de analizar')return 'Disponible';return status==='Error'?'Error de análisis':status;}
    function documentStatusClass(status){return ['Procesado','Disponible'].includes(status)?'system-success':['Pendiente de revisión','Pendiente de Insight Register'].includes(status)?'system-pending':status==='Analizando'?'system-processing':['Error','Error de análisis'].includes(status)?'system-error':'unknown';}
    function documentCanUpdateSource(item){return Boolean(documentHydrationComplete&&item&&item.status!=='Analizando'&&(documentHistoricalUnavailable(item)||!item.fileMeta));}
    function documentPrimaryAction(item){
      if(!documentHydrationComplete)return null;
      if(item.insightRegister)return {action:'open',label:'Abrir'};
      if(item.supportingDocument)return null;
      if(item.status==='Pendiente de Insight Register')return {action:'add-register',label:'Añadir Excel revisado'};
      if(hasDocumentAnalysis(item)||documentAnalysisRecoverable(item))return {action:'view',label:'Ver ficha'};
      if(hasRelatedDocumentKnowledge(item))return {action:'knowledge',label:'Ver conocimiento relacionado'};
      if(hasDocumentHistory(item))return null;
      if(item.status==='Analizando')return null;
      return {action:'process',label:item.status==='Error de análisis'||item.status==='Error'?'Reintentar análisis':'Seleccionar archivo y analizar'};
    }

    async function insightRegisterFileAvailable(item){
      if(!item?.insightRegister)return false;
      const localBlob=await getInsightRegisterBlob(item.id).catch(()=>null);
      if(localBlob)return true;
      const persistentBlob=await getPersistentSourceBlob(item.id,'insight-register').catch(()=>null);
      return Boolean(persistentBlob);
    }
    async function refreshInsightRegisterRecoveryActions(items){
      const candidates=(items||[]).filter(item=>item?.insightRegister);
      await Promise.all(candidates.map(async item=>{
        const available=await insightRegisterFileAvailable(item);
        const menu=[...document.querySelectorAll('.action-menu[data-menu]')].find(node=>node.dataset.menu===String(item.id));
        if(!menu)return;
        const existing=menu.querySelector('[data-doc-action="relink-register"]');
        if(available){existing?.remove();return;}
        if(existing)return;
        const button=document.createElement('button');
        button.type='button';
        button.dataset.docAction='relink-register';
        button.dataset.id=String(item.id);
        button.textContent='Volver a vincular archivo';
        const edit=menu.querySelector('[data-doc-action="edit"]');
        if(edit)menu.insertBefore(button,edit);else menu.appendChild(button);
      }));
    }
    async function openDocumentReview(id){
      const item=sourceItems.find(record=>record.id===id);if(!item)return;
      if(!documentHydrationComplete){openSource(id);return;}
      if(documentAnalysisRecoverable(item)&&!await recoverDocumentAnalysis(item))return;
      if(!hasDocumentAnalysis(item)){openSource(id);return;}
      activeReviewDocumentId=id;analysisMode='view';closeDrawer();showPanel('review');
    }
    function renderSources(){
      let items=hierarchyFilter(sourceItems,'source');
      const type=document.getElementById('source-type').value,initiative=document.getElementById('source-initiative').value,date=document.getElementById('source-date').value;
      const includedFrom=document.getElementById('source-included-from')?.value||'',includedTo=document.getElementById('source-included-to')?.value||'',sort=document.getElementById('source-sort')?.value||'newest';
      items=items.filter(item=>(type==='Todos'||documentAnalysisType(item)===type)&&(date==='Todos'||documentDate(item)===date)&&inclusionDateMatch(item.ingestedAt,includedFrom,includedTo)&&(['Todas','Todos'].includes(initiative)||[item.initiative,...(item.initiatives||[])].filter(Boolean).some(value=>matches(value,initiative))));
      items=sortByInclusion(items,sort,item=>item.ingestedAt);
      document.getElementById('source-result').textContent=`${items.length} ${items.length===1?'documento':'documentos'}`;
      const summary=document.getElementById('initiative-summary');
      if(!['Todas','Todos'].includes(initiative)){
        const types=[...new Set(items.map(documentAnalysisType).filter(Boolean))];
        summary.hidden=false;summary.innerHTML=`<strong>${escapeHtml(initiative)}</strong> · ${items.length} documentos · ${types.map(escapeHtml).join(', ')||'Sin resultados'}`;
      }else summary.hidden=true;
      const pageItems=catalogPage(items,'source','documentos');
      document.getElementById('source-table').innerHTML=items.length?pageItems.map(item=>{
        const primary=documentPrimaryAction(item),hasRealAnalysis=hasDocumentAnalysis(item);
        const primaryMarkup=primary?`<button data-doc-action="${primary.action}" data-id="${item.id}">${primary.label}</button>`:'';
        const filename=item.originalName||item.fileMeta?.name;
        const relations=documentLearningRelations(item);
        const relatedLearnings=[...relations.used,...relations.proposed].filter((learning,index,array)=>array.findIndex(candidate=>candidate.id===learning.id)===index);
        const recordCount=item.insightRegister?.rows?.length||0;
        const pendingSuggestions=(item.insightRegister?.learningSuggestions?.candidates||[]).filter(candidate=>candidate.status==='pending').length;
        const knowledgeSummary=item.insightRegister
          ? `<span class="document-knowledge-summary">${recordCount} ${recordCount===1?'registro estructurado':'registros estructurados'}${relatedLearnings.length?` · ${relatedLearnings.length} ${relatedLearnings.length===1?'learning relacionado':'learnings relacionados'}`:''}${pendingSuggestions?` · ${pendingSuggestions} ${pendingSuggestions===1?'sugerencia pendiente':'sugerencias pendientes'}`:''}</span>`
          : '';
        const sourceUpdate=documentCanUpdateSource(item)?`<button data-doc-action="link-source" data-id="${item.id}">Actualizar fuente documental</button>`:'';
        const registerAction=item.supportingDocument?'':item.insightRegister?`<button data-doc-action="add-register" data-id="${item.id}">Actualizar Excel</button>`:`<button data-doc-action="add-register" data-id="${item.id}">Añadir Excel revisado</button>`;
        const where=documentApplicationPath(item)||'Pendiente de clasificar';
        const rawDate=documentDate(item);
        const dateLabel=/^No especificad[oa]$/i.test(String(rawDate||'').trim())?'—':rawDate;
        return `<tr data-doc-open="${escapeHtml(item.id)}" tabindex="0"><td data-label="Documento"><div class="document-file-cell"><span class="document-file-icon ${item.supportingDocument?'is-pdf':'is-excel'}">${item.supportingDocument?'<img src="assets/file-icons/file-type-icon.png" alt="" aria-hidden="true">':'<img src="assets/file-icons/excel-file-icon.png" alt="" aria-hidden="true">'}</span><span class="document-file-copy"><strong>${escapeHtml(item.name)}</strong>${filename?`<span class="sub">${escapeHtml(filename)}</span>`:''}${knowledgeSummary}</span></div></td><td data-label="Tipo de análisis">${escapeHtml(documentAnalysisType(item)||'Sin clasificar')}</td><td data-label="Iniciativa">${escapeHtml(item.initiative||((item.initiatives||[]).filter(Boolean).join(' · '))||'Sin clasificar')}</td><td data-label="Dónde aplica">${escapeHtml(where)}</td><td data-label="Fecha"><span class="document-date-value">${escapeHtml(dateLabel)}</span></td><td data-label="Acciones" class="document-actions"><button class="icon-button" type="button" aria-label="Acciones de ${escapeHtml(item.name)}" data-action-menu="${item.id}">⋮</button><div class="action-menu" data-menu="${item.id}"><button data-doc-action="open" data-id="${item.id}">Abrir</button>${registerAction}<button data-doc-action="edit" data-id="${item.id}">Editar datos</button><button class="delete-action" data-doc-action="delete" data-id="${item.id}">Eliminar</button></div></td></tr>`;
      }).join(''):`<tr><td colspan="6">${empty('documentos')}</td></tr>`;
      document.querySelector('.nav-button[data-panel="sources"] .nav-count').textContent=sourceItems.length;
      void refreshInsightRegisterRecoveryActions(pageItems);
    }

    function activeReviewDocument(){return activeReviewDocumentId?sourceItems.find(item=>item.id===activeReviewDocumentId):sourceItems.find(item=>item.status==='Pendiente de revisión')||sourceItems.find(item=>item.id===PILOT_SOURCE_ID);}
    function findingStatusLabel(status){return status==='approved'?'Aprobado':status==='discarded'?'Descartado':status==='published'?'Publicado':'Pendiente';}
    function findingStatusClass(status){return ['approved','published'].includes(status)?'system-success':status==='discarded'?'system-error':'unknown';}

    function hasSemanticPredicate(text){return /\b(es|son|esta|estan|sera|tiene|tienen|incluye|incluyen|utiliza|utilizan|dispone|muestra|permite|permiten|resuelve|resuelven|aporta|aportan|ocupa|ocupan|aparece|aparecen|reduce|reducen|aumenta|aumentan|refuerza|refuerzan|cumple|cumplen|conecta|conectan|puede|pueden|podremos|debe|deben|deberia|deberian|tendria|necesita|necesitan|conviene|interesa|convive|conviven|funciona|funcionan|facilita|facilitan|limita|limitan|evita|evitan|ayuda|ayudan|anade|anaden|suma|resta|retrasa|compite|compiten|explorar|equilibrar|contrastar|formular|testar|testarlas|medir|revisar|mantener|se percibe|se reconoce|se explora|se define|se parte)\b/.test(normalizeText(text));}
    function reviewStructuralFragment(text){
      const clean=String(text||'').trim(),value=normalizeText(clean);
      if(!clean)return 'Registro vacío.';
      if(/:\s*$/.test(clean))return 'Introducción o enumeración incompleta; se usa como contexto del contenido posterior.';
      if(!/[.!?]$/.test(clean)&&clean.split(/\s+/).length<=8&&!hasSemanticPredicate(clean))return 'Título o etiqueta sin una afirmación completa.';
      if(/^(vertical|cualquier elemento)\b/.test(value)&&(clean.match(/,/g)||[]).length>=2)return 'Lista usada como contexto de jerarquía, sin una proposición independiente.';
      if(!hasSemanticPredicate(clean)&&((clean.match(/,/g)||[]).length>=2||/^(pasajeros|claim\s+banners)\b/.test(value)))return 'Lista o fragmento sin una proposición semántica completa.';
      return '';
    }
    function segmentTableColumn(lines,expected){
      const groups=[];let current=[];
      const startsCell=line=>/^["“'¿A-ZÁÉÍÓÚÑ0-9]/.test(line);
      lines.forEach(line=>{
        const previous=current.at(-1)||'',continued=/\b(y|e|o|de|del|la|el|con|para|por|en|aunque|pero|sin)$/i.test(previous.trim());
        if(current.length&&startsCell(line)&&!continued){groups.push(current.join(' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim());current=[];}
        current.push(line);
      });
      if(current.length)groups.push(current.join(' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim());
      while(groups.length>expected){let index=1;for(let i=2;i<groups.length;i++)if(groups[i].length<groups[index].length)index=i;groups[index-1]=`${groups[index-1]} ${groups[index]}`;groups.splice(index,1);}
      if(groups.length!==expected){
        const balanced=[];let cursor=0;
        for(let index=0;index<expected;index++){const remaining=lines.length-cursor,slots=expected-index,size=Math.max(1,Math.round(remaining/slots));balanced.push(lines.slice(cursor,cursor+size).join(' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim());cursor+=size;}
        return balanced;
      }
      return groups;
    }
    function extractStructuredTables(analysis){
      const rows=[],sourceFindingIds=new Set(),pages=analysis?.extractedPages||[];
      pages.forEach(page=>{
        const lines=String(page.text||'').split(/\n/).map(line=>line.replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim()).filter(Boolean),normalized=lines.map(normalizeText);
        const areaIndex=normalized.indexOf('area'),findingIndex=normalized.indexOf('hallazgo',areaIndex+1),impactIndex=normalized.indexOf('impacto potencial',findingIndex+1),opportunityIndex=normalized.indexOf('oportunidad',impactIndex+1);
        if(areaIndex<0||findingIndex<=areaIndex||impactIndex<=findingIndex||opportunityIndex<=impactIndex)return;
        const areas=lines.slice(areaIndex+1,findingIndex).filter(line=>line.length<80),count=areas.length;
        if(count<2||count>30)return;
        const findings=segmentTableColumn(lines.slice(findingIndex+1,impactIndex),count),impacts=segmentTableColumn(lines.slice(impactIndex+1,opportunityIndex),count),opportunities=segmentTableColumn(lines.slice(opportunityIndex+1),count);
        if([findings,impacts,opportunities].some(column=>column.length!==count||column.some(cell=>!cell)))return;
        (analysis.findings||[]).filter(item=>Number(item.source?.page)===Number(page.page)).forEach(item=>sourceFindingIds.add(item.id));
        areas.forEach((area,index)=>{
          const text=findings[index],context=detectContext(`${area} ${text} ${impacts[index]} ${opportunities[index]}`);
          const originalFindingIds=(analysis.findings||[]).filter(item=>Number(item.source?.page)===Number(page.page)).map(item=>item.id);
          rows.push({id:`TABLE-P${page.page}-${String(index+1).padStart(2,'0')}`,text,type:'Hallazgo',...context,confidence:'Media',confidenceWhy:'La fila conserva el hallazgo, su impacto potencial y la oportunidad propuesta por la auditoría.',source:{documentId:analysis.findings?.[0]?.source?.documentId||'',name:analysis.findings?.[0]?.source?.name||'',page:page.page,section:'Hallazgos principales · tabla',block:`Tabla · fila ${index+1}`},status:'pending',semanticRole:'finding',derivedFromTable:true,originalFindingIds,provenance:{kind:'reconstructed-table-row',originalFindingIds},structure:{area,hallazgo:text,impacto:impacts[index],oportunidad:opportunities[index]}});
        });
      });
      return {rows,sourceFindingIds};
    }
    function strictReviewType(finding,pageContext=''){
      const text=normalizeText(finding.text),section=normalizeText(finding.source?.section||''),context=`${text} ${normalizeText(pageContext)}`;
      const authorConclusion=/\b(conclusiones|insights|key findings)\b/.test(section);
      const documentedRestriction=/\b(requisito|condicionante|obligatoriamente|limita las opciones)\b/.test(text)&&/\b(seo|h1|copy|palabras)\b/.test(context);
      const futureValidation=/\b(debera|deberia|tendria que|habra que|siguiente fase|antes de implementar|validarse|testar|testarlas|medirse|contrastar|apoyarse en datos)\b/.test(text)&&/\b(test|cro|research|datos|medir|medirse|validar|validarse|hipotesis|variantes)\b/.test(text);
      const actualExperiment=/\b(test a\/?b|experimento|variante|control)\b/.test(text)&&/\b(resultado|obtuvo|mostro|aumento|disminuyo|uplift|chance to win|significancia|supero)\b/.test(text)&&(/%|\d/.test(text));
      const uxEvaluation=/\b(desde (?:una )?(?:lectura )?ux|se percibe|es correcto desde ux|aspiracional|accionable|carga cognitiva|peso visual|puede (?:reducir|aumentar|aportar|perder|desviar|no activar)|pueden necesitar|son utiles,? pero|conviene|parece)\b/.test(text);
      const opportunity=/\b(oportunidad|tendria sentido explorar|deberia priorizar|deberia trabajarse|deberia documentarse|aplicar enfoque|simplificar|reforzar su protagonismo|coordinar su evolucion|optimizar|se explora otra forma)\b/.test(text);
      const explicitObservation=/^(desktop|mobile):/.test(text)&&/\b(ocupa|aparece|se parte|anade|suma|esta|tiene)\b/.test(text);
      if(documentedRestriction)return 'Hecho';
      if(actualExperiment)return 'Experimento';
      if(futureValidation||/\binteresa saber si\b/.test(text))return 'Hipótesis';
      if(authorConclusion)return 'Insight del autor';
      if(opportunity)return 'Oportunidad';
      if(uxEvaluation)return 'Inferencia';
      if(explicitObservation)return 'Hecho';
      if(finding.type==='Experimento'||finding.type==='Decisión')return 'Hallazgo';
      if(finding.type==='Hecho'&&!/\b(deberia|puede|conviene|se percibe)\b/.test(text))return 'Hecho';
      return finding.type||'Hallazgo';
    }
    function reviewContextTags(finding,pageContext=''){
      const value=normalizeText(`${finding.text} ${pageContext}`),tags=[];
      if(/\b(conclusiones|insights|key findings)\b/.test(normalizeText(finding.source?.section||'')))tags.push('Síntesis del autor');
      if(/\b(seo|h1)\b/.test(value)&&/\b(requisito|condicionante|obligatoriamente|limita)\b/.test(value))tags.push('Restricción SEO');
      if(/\bmagnolia\b/.test(value))tags.push('Capacidad de implementación · Magnolia');
      if(/\b(negocio|estrategia comercial|campanas)\b/.test(value))tags.push('Contexto de negocio');
      if(/\bmobile|movil\b/.test(normalizeText(finding.text)))tags.push('Mobile');
      if(/\bdesktop\b/.test(normalizeText(finding.text)))tags.push('Desktop');
      if(/\b(test a\/?b|cro|research|datos|medir|validar)\b/.test(normalizeText(finding.text)))tags.push('Siguiente paso de validación');
      return [...new Set(tags)];
    }
    function buildReviewInterpretation(analysis){
      if(analysis?.documentEditorial)return UXDocumentAnalysis.interpretation(analysis);
      const pageContexts=new Map((analysis?.extractedPages||[]).map(page=>[Number(page.page),String(page.text||'')]));
      (analysis?.findings||[]).forEach(finding=>{const page=Number(finding.source?.page);if(!pageContexts.has(page))pageContexts.set(page,(analysis.findings||[]).filter(item=>Number(item.source?.page)===page).map(item=>item.text).join(' '));});
      const table=extractStructuredTables(analysis),originalRecords=(analysis?.findings||[]).map((finding,index)=>{
        const pageContext=pageContexts.get(Number(finding.source?.page))||'',tableSource=table.sourceFindingIds.has(finding.id),fragmentReason=tableSource?'Texto de origen de una tabla reconstruida por filas y columnas.':reviewStructuralFragment(finding.text);
        const type=fragmentReason?(tableSource?'Tabla de origen':'Contexto estructural'):strictReviewType(finding,pageContext);
        return {...finding,originalFinding:finding,originalIndex:index+1,type,semanticRole:fragmentReason?'context':'finding',interpretationReason:fragmentReason,contextTags:reviewContextTags(finding,pageContext),authorConclusion:/\b(conclusiones|insights|key findings)\b/.test(normalizeText(finding.source?.section||''))};
      });
      originalRecords.forEach((record,index)=>{
        if(record.semanticRole!=='finding')return;
        const explicit=reviewExplicitTopicId(record);
        if(explicit){record.topicHint=explicit;return;}
        const ambiguous=/^(su |esta |este |estos |estas |en este punto|en mobile|si (?:en )?mobile|si duplican|aun asi)/.test(normalizeText(record.text));
        if(!ambiguous)return;
        const candidates=originalRecords.slice(Math.max(0,index-6),index).filter(item=>item.semanticRole==='finding'&&Number(item.source?.page)===Number(record.source?.page)&&item.topicHint),counts=candidates.reduce((acc,item)=>{acc[item.topicHint]=(acc[item.topicHint]||0)+1;return acc;},{}),preferred=Object.entries(counts).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]||candidates.map(item=>item.topicHint).lastIndexOf(b[0])-candidates.map(item=>item.topicHint).lastIndexOf(a[0]))[0]?.[0];
        if(preferred)record.topicHint=preferred;
      });
      const representedOriginalIds=new Set(table.rows.flatMap(row=>row.originalFindingIds||[])),originalUnits=originalRecords.filter(item=>item.semanticRole==='finding'&&!representedOriginalIds.has(item.id)),units=[...originalUnits,...table.rows],typeCounts={};
      units.forEach(item=>{typeCounts[item.type]=(typeCounts[item.type]||0)+1;});
      const doubleCountedOriginalIds=originalUnits.filter(item=>representedOriginalIds.has(item.id)).map(item=>item.id),traceComplete=table.rows.every(row=>row.originalFindingIds?.length&&row.originalFindingIds.every(id=>originalRecords.some(record=>record.id===id)));
      return {version:REVIEW_INTERPRETATION_VERSION,originalRecords,units,tableRows:table.rows,typeCounts,validOriginals:originalUnits.length,contextRecords:originalRecords.filter(item=>item.semanticRole==='context').length,tableDedup:{representedOriginalIds:[...representedOriginalIds],doubleCountedOriginalIds,traceComplete}};
    }
    function formatPageRanges(values){
      const pages=[...new Set(values.filter(Number.isFinite))].sort((a,b)=>a-b),ranges=[];let start=null,previous=null;
      pages.forEach(page=>{if(start===null){start=previous=page;return;}if(page===previous+1){previous=page;return;}ranges.push(start===previous?`${start}`:`${start}–${previous}`);start=previous=page;});
      if(start!==null)ranges.push(start===previous?`${start}`:`${start}–${previous}`);
      return ranges.join(', ')||'No especificadas';
    }
    function reviewExplicitTopicId(finding){
      const text=normalizeText(finding.text),structure=normalizeText(finding.structure?.area||''),tags=normalizeText((finding.contextTags||[]).join(' '));
      const tableArea={buscador:'search-priority',cta:'cta-form',claim:'claim-seo',header:'header-account',banners:'banners','accesos rapidos':'self-service',mobile:'mobile','ui kit':'design-system'}[structure];
      if(tableArea)return tableArea;
      let anchored='';
      if(/\b(ultima busqueda|busqueda guardada|retomar el proceso)\b/.test(text))anchored='last-search';
      else if(/\b(primer bloque de la home concentra|convive con elementos|ordena mejor la convivencia|muchas funciones necesarias|demasiadas capas)\b/.test(text))anchored='above-fold';
      else if(/\b(check-?\s*in|gestiona (?:tu )?reserva|informacion de vuelos|autoservicio|accesos (?:rapidos|directos)|cards de autoservicio|tareas frecuentes|acciones (?:muy )?pulsadas|resolver una tarea concreta|responden a acciones que los usuarios)\b/.test(text))anchored='self-service';
      else if(/\b(claim|h1|seo)\b/.test(text)||tags.includes('restriccion seo'))anchored='claim-seo';
      else if(/\b(banner|campana|promocion|carrusel|magnolia|ctr|revenue)\b/.test(text))anchored='banners';
      else if(/\b(ui kit|componente robusto|deuda visual|variantes,? estados|accesibilidad bien definida)\b/.test(text))anchored='design-system';
      else if(/\b(mobile-first|escalar(?:lo)? a desktop|escala(?:r)? (?:el buscador )?a desktop)\b/.test(text))anchored='mobile';
      else if(/\b(siguiente fase|contrastar estos hallazgos|apoyandonos en datos|formular hipotesis|testarlas con cro)\b/.test(text))anchored='measurement';
      else if(/\b(cta|buscar vuelos|campos clave|formulario|que debe completar)\b/.test(text))anchored='cta-form';
      else if(/\b(header|cuenta|suma|navegacion global|menu)\b/.test(text)&&!/\babove the fold\b/.test(text))anchored='header-account';
      else if(/\b(above the fold|primer impacto|demasiadas capas|jerarquia (?:comun|visual)|accion principal)\b/.test(text))anchored='above-fold';
      return anchored;
    }
    function reviewTopicForFinding(finding){
      const text=normalizeText(finding.text),anchored=finding.topicHint||reviewExplicitTopicId(finding);
      if(anchored)return reviewTopicDefinitions.find(topic=>topic.id===anchored);
      const fallbackIds=new Set(['search-priority','above-fold','header-account','cta-form']),ranked=reviewTopicDefinitions.filter(topic=>fallbackIds.has(topic.id)).map((topic,index)=>({topic,index,score:semanticSimilarity(`${finding.text} ${finding.source?.section||''}`,`${topic.seed} ${topic.terms.join(' ')}`)+topic.terms.reduce((score,term)=>score+(text.includes(normalizeText(term))?(term.includes(' ')?0.34:0.16):0),0)})).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score||a.index-b.index);
      return ranked[0]?.topic||reviewTopicDefinitions.find(topic=>topic.id==='search-priority');
    }
    function buildReviewClusters(analysis,interpretation=buildReviewInterpretation(analysis)){
      if(analysis?.documentEditorial)return UXDocumentAnalysis.clusters(analysis,learnings);
      const findings=interpretation.units,relations=analysis?.relations||[],clusterReview=analysis?.clusterReview||{};
      const clusters=new Map(reviewTopicDefinitions.map(topic=>[topic.id,{...topic,findings:[],relations:[]} ]));
      findings.forEach(finding=>clusters.get(reviewTopicForFinding(finding).id).findings.push(finding));
      relations.forEach(relation=>{const finding=findings.find(item=>item.id===relation.findingId);if(finding)clusters.get(reviewTopicForFinding(finding).id).relations.push(relation);});
      return [...clusters.values()].filter(cluster=>cluster.findings.length).map(cluster=>{
        const saved=clusterReview[cluster.id]||{},savedIsCurrent=Boolean(saved.decision&&saved.status),decision=savedIsCurrent?saved.decision:cluster.proposal,learning=learnings.find(item=>item.id===cluster.learningId)||null;
        const publishedOperation=analysis?.publication?.operations?.find(item=>item.id===cluster.id),isPublished=analysis?.publication?.status==='published';
        const displayTitle=isPublished?(publishedOperation?.name||saved.editorialTitle||cluster.name):(saved.editorialTitle||cluster.editorialTitle||cluster.name),displaySynthesis=isPublished?(publishedOperation?.synthesis||saved.editorialSynthesis||cluster.synthesis):(saved.editorialSynthesis||cluster.synthesis);
        const pages=cluster.findings.map(item=>Number(item.source?.page)),devices=[...new Set(cluster.findings.map(item=>item.device).filter(value=>value&&value!=='No especificado'))],types=[...new Set(cluster.findings.map(item=>item.type).filter(Boolean))],contextTags=[...new Set(cluster.findings.flatMap(item=>item.contextTags||[]))];
        const hasLowConfidence=cluster.findings.some(item=>item.confidence==='Baja'),hasContradiction=cluster.relations.some(relation=>relation.decision==='Contradice learning'&&/\b(no funciona|empeora|contradice|cae|reduce (?:conversion|avance|uso)|dificulta)\b/.test(normalizeText(cluster.findings.find(item=>item.id===relation.findingId)?.text||'')));
        const highRisk=['Crear nuevo learning','Ampliar learning','Contradice learning','Crear pregunta abierta','Posible patrón','Posible oportunidad','Recomendación de sistema'].includes(decision);
        return {...cluster,displayTitle,displaySynthesis,decision,learning,pages,devices,types,contextTags,hasLowConfidence,hasContradiction,needsDecision:highRisk||hasLowConfidence||hasContradiction,reviewStatus:savedIsCurrent?(saved.status||'pending'):'pending'};
      }).sort((a,b)=>b.findings.length-a.findings.length);
    }
    function reviewInsightsForDocument(documentItem,interpretation=documentInterpretation(documentItem)){
      if(hasRecoveredDocumentAnalysis(documentItem))return applyAnalysisEditorial(documentItem,documentItem.archivedReview.insights);
      const base=isDocumentPublished(documentItem)?publishedAnalysisInsights(documentItem,interpretation):buildReviewClusters(documentItem?.analysis,interpretation);
      return applyAnalysisEditorial(documentItem,base);
    }
    function reviewDecisionCounts(clusters){return clusters.reduce((counts,cluster)=>{counts[cluster.decision]=(counts[cluster.decision]||0)+1;return counts;},{});}
    const publicationImpactDefinitions=[
      {key:'reinforced',label:'Learnings reforzados',decisions:['Reforzar learning','Reforzar learning existente']},
      {key:'expanded',label:'Learnings ampliados',decisions:['Ampliar learning']},
      {key:'newLearnings',label:'Nuevos learnings',decisions:['Crear nuevo learning']},
      {key:'questions',label:'Preguntas abiertas',decisions:['Crear pregunta abierta']},
      {key:'opportunities',label:'Oportunidades',decisions:['Posible oportunidad']},
      {key:'recommendations',label:'Recomendaciones de sistema',decisions:['Recomendación de sistema']},
      {key:'patterns',label:'Patterns',decisions:['Posible patrón']},
      {key:'evidenceOnly',label:'Insights conservados solo como evidencia',decisions:['Mantener solo como evidencia']},
      {key:'contradictions',label:'Contradicciones',decisions:['Contradice learning']},
      {key:'linkedEvidence',label:'Evidencias vinculadas',decisions:['Usar como evidencia']},
      {key:'restrictions',label:'Restricciones nuevas',decisions:['Crear restricción']},
      {key:'updatedRestrictions',label:'Restricciones actualizadas',decisions:['Actualizar restricción existente']},
      {key:'discarded',label:'Insights descartados',decisions:['Descartar']},
      {key:'context',label:'Contexto documental',decisions:['Solo contexto / evidencia documental']}
    ];
    function approvedDocumentFirstV2Plan(documentItem){
      const analysis=documentItem?.analysis,artifact=analysis?.documentFirstV2,stored=artifact?.publicationPlan;
      if(!stored||stored.status!=='approved'||stored.automaticPublication!==false)return null;
      const canonical=stored.canonicalContext||{},canonicalUnits=(analysis.findings||[]).map(unit=>({...cloneAnalysisValue(unit),product:canonical.product||unit.product,screen:canonical.screen||unit.screen,component:canonical.component||unit.component})),byId=new Map(canonicalUnits.map(unit=>[unit.id,unit]));
      const operations=stored.operations.map(operation=>{
        const findings=(operation.evidenceRefs||[]).map(id=>byId.get(id)).filter(Boolean),pages=formatPageRanges(findings.map(item=>Number(item.source?.page))),devices=[...new Set(findings.map(item=>item.device).filter(knownMetadata))],types=[...new Set(findings.map(item=>item.type).filter(Boolean))],scope=[canonical.product,canonical.screen,canonical.component].filter(Boolean).join(' / ');
        const details=operation.decision==='Crear nuevo learning'?{title:operation.name,synthesis:operation.synthesis,scope,confidence:'Media',limitation:(operation.specificLimits||[]).join('\n')}:
          ['Reforzar learning existente','Ampliar learning'].includes(operation.decision)?{targetKind:'learning',targetId:operation.learningId,contribution:operation.nuance||operation.synthesis}:
          operation.decision==='Crear pregunta abierta'?{question:operation.name,scope}:
          operation.decision==='Recomendación de sistema'?{title:operation.name,synthesis:operation.synthesis,scope}:{};
        return {id:operation.id,name:operation.name,synthesis:operation.synthesis,nuance:operation.nuance||'',decision:operation.decision,learningId:operation.learningId||'',learningTitle:learnings.find(item=>item.id===operation.learningId)?.title||'',unitCount:findings.length,pages,devices,types,proposedClassification:'',contextTags:[],findings,reviewStatus:'approved',decisionDetails:details,editorialContext:{product:canonical.product||'',screen:canonical.screen||'',component:canonical.component||'',context:scope,notDemonstrated:(operation.specificLimits||[]).join('\n')}};
      });
      const clusters=operations.map(operation=>({...operation,displayTitle:operation.name,displaySynthesis:operation.synthesis,needsDecision:false,learning:learnings.find(item=>item.id===operation.learningId)||null}));
      const counts=publicationImpactDefinitions.reduce((summary,definition)=>{summary[definition.key]=operations.filter(operation=>definition.decisions.includes(operation.decision)).length;return summary;},{}),decisionErrors=clusters.flatMap(cluster=>insightDecisionErrors(cluster));
      const interpretation={...documentInterpretation(documentItem),units:canonicalUnits};
      return {documentItem,analysis,interpretation,clusters,allClusters:clusters,revision:false,changedIds:[],operations,counts,relevantPending:0,routinePending:0,decisionErrors,ready:operations.length>0&&!decisionErrors.length,trace:{validUnits:canonicalUnits.length,originalRecords:analysis.findings?.length||0,reconstructedRows:0,representedOriginals:0,doubleCounted:0,engine:'document-first-2'}};
    }
    function reviewPublicationPlan(documentItem){
      const approvedV2=approvedDocumentFirstV2Plan(documentItem);if(approvedV2)return approvedV2;
      const analysis=documentItem?.analysis,interpretation=documentInterpretation(documentItem),allClusters=reviewInsightsForDocument(documentItem,interpretation),revision=isDocumentPublished(documentItem),changedIds=revision?pendingAnalysisChanges(documentItem):[],clusters=revision?allClusters.filter(cluster=>changedIds.includes(cluster.id)):allClusters;
      const operations=clusters.map(cluster=>({id:cluster.id,name:cluster.displayTitle,synthesis:cluster.displaySynthesis,nuance:cluster.nuance,decision:cluster.decision,learningId:cluster.learning?.id||cluster.learningId||'',learningTitle:cluster.learning?.title||'',unitCount:cluster.findings.length,pages:formatPageRanges(cluster.pages),devices:cluster.devices,types:cluster.types,proposedClassification:cluster.proposedClassification||'',contextTags:cluster.contextTags,findings:cluster.findings,reviewStatus:cluster.reviewStatus,decisionDetails:cloneAnalysisValue(cluster.decisionDetails||{}),editorialContext:cloneAnalysisValue(cluster.editorialContext||{})}));
      const counts=publicationImpactDefinitions.reduce((summary,definition)=>{summary[definition.key]=operations.filter(operation=>definition.decisions.includes(operation.decision)).length;return summary;},{});
      const relevantPending=clusters.filter(cluster=>cluster.needsDecision&&!insightIsReviewed(cluster)).length,routinePending=clusters.filter(cluster=>!cluster.needsDecision&&!insightIsReviewed(cluster)).length;
      const missingLearning=operations.some(operation=>['Reforzar learning','Reforzar learning existente','Ampliar learning'].includes(operation.decision)&&!learnings.some(learning=>learning.id===operation.learningId));
      const decisionErrors=clusters.flatMap(cluster=>insightDecisionErrors({...cluster,decisionDetails:insightDecisionDetails(cluster)}));
      return {documentItem,analysis,interpretation,clusters,allClusters,revision,changedIds,operations,counts,relevantPending,routinePending,decisionErrors,ready:clusters.length>0&&!missingLearning&&!decisionErrors.length&&!relevantPending&&!routinePending&&clusters.every(cluster=>insightIsReviewed(cluster)),trace:{validUnits:interpretation.units.length,originalRecords:analysis?.findings?.length||0,reconstructedRows:interpretation.tableRows.length,representedOriginals:interpretation.tableDedup?.representedOriginalIds?.length||0,doubleCounted:interpretation.tableDedup?.doubleCountedOriginalIds?.length||0}};
    }
    function publicationSummaryText(counts){
      const labels=[['reinforced','learning reforzado','learnings reforzados'],['expanded','learning ampliado','learnings ampliados'],['newLearnings','nuevo learning','nuevos learnings'],['questions','pregunta abierta','preguntas abiertas'],['opportunities','oportunidad','oportunidades'],['recommendations','recomendación de sistema','recomendaciones de sistema'],['patterns','pattern','patterns'],['evidenceOnly','insight solo como evidencia','insights solo como evidencia'],['contradictions','contradicción','contradicciones'],['linkedEvidence','evidencia vinculada','evidencias vinculadas'],['restrictions','restricción nueva','restricciones nuevas'],['updatedRestrictions','restricción actualizada','restricciones actualizadas'],['discarded','insight descartado','insights descartados'],['context','insight de contexto','insights de contexto']];
      return labels.filter(([key])=>counts[key]).map(([key,singular,plural])=>`${counts[key]} ${counts[key]===1?singular:plural}`).join(' · ')||'Sin operaciones de publicación';
    }
    function publicationOperationNotes(operation,documentItem){
      const source=`Nueva evidencia procedente de ${documentItem.name}.`,trace=`Trazabilidad a ${operation.pages==='—'?'las páginas de origen':`páginas ${operation.pages}`} y ${operation.unitCount} ${operation.unitCount===1?'unidad válida':'unidades válidas'}.`,context=operation.devices.length?`Contexto conservado: ${operation.devices.join(', ')}.`:'El contexto disponible se conserva sin generalizarlo.';
      if(['Reforzar learning','Reforzar learning existente'].includes(operation.decision))return [source,'La evidencia reforzará el learning relacionado sin crear otro learning.',context,trace];
      if(operation.decision==='Ampliar learning')return [source,operation.nuance?`Se añadirá este matiz: ${operation.nuance}`:'Se ampliará el alcance del learning relacionado.',context,trace];
      if(operation.decision==='Contradice learning')return [source,'La contradicción se incorporará separada de la evidencia a favor.',context,trace];
      if(operation.decision==='Crear nuevo learning')return [source,'Se creará un único learning consolidado para este insight.',context,trace];
      if(operation.decision==='Crear pregunta abierta')return [source,'Se conservarán evidencia, inferencias, restricciones y una pregunta para validar.','No se creará un learning.',trace];
      if(operation.decision==='Posible oportunidad')return [source,'Se registrará como oportunidad, separada de learnings y patterns.',context,trace];
      if(operation.decision==='Recomendación de sistema')return [source,'Se registrará como recomendación de sistema, no como pattern validado.',context,trace];
      if(operation.decision==='Descartar')return ['Se conservará el insight y su histórico en el documento.','No se publicará como evidencia ni como conocimiento.',trace];
      if(['Crear restricción','Actualizar restricción existente'].includes(operation.decision))return [source,'Se conservará el estado anterior, la fuente y el impacto de la restricción.',trace];
      if(operation.decision==='Usar como evidencia')return [source,'Se vinculará la aportación al conocimiento seleccionado sin crear un learning.',trace];
      if(operation.decision==='Posible patrón')return [source,'Se creará un único pattern propuesto para este insight.',context,trace];
      return [source,'El insight se conservará como evidencia trazable.','No se creará un learning, pregunta, oportunidad ni pattern.',trace];
    }
    // Presentation only: consume the reviewed publication plan without changing it.
    function publicationPreviewGroups(plan){
      const groups=publicationImpactDefinitions.map(definition=>({...definition,label:definition.key==='patterns'?'Patrones':definition.label,operations:plan.operations.filter(operation=>definition.decisions.includes(operation.decision))})).filter(group=>group.operations.length);
      const remaining=plan.operations.filter(operation=>!groups.some(group=>group.operations.includes(operation)));
      if(remaining.length)groups.push({key:'other',label:'Otras decisiones',operations:remaining});
      return groups;
    }
    function publicationPreviewField(label,value){
      return value&&String(value).trim()?`<div class="publication-field"><h5>${escapeHtml(label)}</h5><p>${escapeHtml(value)}</p></div>`:'';
    }
    function publicationPreviewOperation(operation,documentItem,plan){
      const copy=insightPublicationCopy(operation),context=insightPublicationContext(operation),details=operation.decisionDetails||{},editorial=operation.editorialContext||{};
      const question=operation.decision==='Crear pregunta abierta',newLearning=operation.decision==='Crear nuevo learning';
      const title=question?copy.question:newLearning?copy.title:operation.learningId&&operation.learningTitle?`${operation.learningId} · ${operation.learningTitle}`:details.restriction||copy.title;
      const path=['product','screen','component'].map(key=>context[key]).filter(knownMetadata).join(' / ');
      const metadata=[insightDecisionLabel(operation.decision),newLearning?`Confianza ${String(copy.confidence).toLocaleLowerCase('es')}`:'',path].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
      const field=(label,value)=>value!==title?publicationPreviewField(label,value):'';
      const limits=copy.noProof.join('\n');
      let content;
      if(newLearning)content=field('Qué sabemos',copy.synthesis)+field('Qué no demuestra',limits)+field('Alcance',copy.scope||pathOf(context));
      else if(question)content=field('Contexto de la pregunta',copy.synthesis||editorial.context||copy.scope)+field('Qué necesitamos resolver',details.problem);
      else content=field('Aportación',copy.synthesis)+field('Qué no demuestra',limits)+field('Alcance',copy.scope);
      const shown=new Set(question?['question','contribution','synthesis','problem']:['contribution','synthesis','limitation','scope',...(newLearning?['confidence']:[])]);
      const secondaryProposal=insightProposalPreview({...operation,decisionDetails:Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(details).filter(([key,value])=>!shown.has(key)&&value!==title&&value!==copy.synthesis))});
      const original=plan.clusters.find(cluster=>cluster.id===operation.id);
      const origin=field('Qué dice el documento',operation.synthesis)+field('Matices',operation.nuance)+field('Qué significa para UX',editorial.uxImplication)+field('Contexto del documento',editorial.context)+field('Qué no demuestra la fuente',editorial.notDemonstrated)+field('Límites de la fuente',editorial.limits);
      const unitIds=operation.findings.map(unit=>unit.id).filter(Boolean).join(', ');
      const trace=`<details class="publication-trace"><summary>Trazabilidad</summary><div>${publicationPreviewField('Documento',`${documentItem.name} · ${documentItem.originalName||documentItem.fileMeta?.name||''}`)}<p>${operation.unitCount} ${operation.unitCount===1?'unidad válida':'unidades válidas'} · páginas ${escapeHtml(operation.pages)}</p>${field('Dispositivo',operation.devices.join(', '))}${field('Tipo de conocimiento',operation.types.join(', '))}${secondaryProposal}${analysisRevisionNotes(documentItem,operation)}<details><summary>Ver insight de origen</summary>${operation.name!==title?field('Insight de origen',operation.name):''}${origin}${publicationPreviewField('Referencias de las unidades',unitIds)}${original?insightDecisionHistoryMarkup(original):''}</details></div></details>`;
      return `<details class="publication-operation" data-preview-operation="${escapeHtml(operation.id)}"><summary><span><strong>${escapeHtml(title)}</strong><span class="publication-meta">${escapeHtml(metadata)}</span></span><svg class="publication-chevron" width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"/></svg></summary><div class="publication-operation-body">${content}${trace}</div></details>`;
    }
    function publicationPreviewBody(plan,documentItem){
      const groups=publicationPreviewGroups(plan),selected=selectedEvolutionProposals(documentItem);
      const existingKeys=new Set(['reinforced','expanded','contradictions','linkedEvidence','updatedRestrictions']);
      const existingChanges=plan.operations.filter(operation=>publicationImpactDefinitions.some(definition=>existingKeys.has(definition.key)&&definition.decisions.includes(operation.decision))).length+selected.length;
      const metrics=[...groups.filter(group=>!existingKeys.has(group.key)).map(group=>[group.operations.length,group.label]),[existingChanges,'Cambios sobre conocimiento existente'],[plan.trace.originalRecords,'Registros originales conservados']];
      const summary=`<section class="publication-summary-section"><h3>Resumen de impacto</h3><dl class="publication-summary">${metrics.map(([count,label])=>`<div><dt>${escapeHtml(label)}</dt><dd>${count}</dd></div>`).join('')}</dl></section>`;
      const operations=groups.map(group=>`<section class="publication-group"><header class="section-head"><h4>${escapeHtml(group.label)} <span class="status-badge neutral">${group.operations.length}</span></h4></header>${group.operations.map(operation=>publicationPreviewOperation(operation,documentItem,plan)).join('')}</section>`).join('');
      const evolution=selected.length?`<details class="publication-trace"><summary>Actualizaciones seleccionadas (${selected.length})</summary>${evolutionPreviewMarkup(documentItem)}</details>`:'';
      const legacyDecisions=documentItem.analysis?.documentFirstV2?.publicationPlan?.trace?.legacyHumanDecisionIds?.length||plan.operations.length;
      const trace=`<details class="publication-trace publication-general-trace"><summary>Trazabilidad de la publicación</summary><div>${publicationPreviewField('Documento',`${documentItem.name} · ${documentItem.originalName||documentItem.fileMeta?.name||''}`)}<p><strong>${plan.trace.originalRecords} registros originales conservados</strong> · ${plan.trace.validUnits} unidades válidas vinculadas al documento.</p><p>Las ${plan.trace.reconstructedRows} filas reconstruidas sustituyen semánticamente a sus fragmentos originales. No se duplicará evidencia.</p><p>Las ${legacyDecisions} decisiones humanas previas se conservarán con su fuente y sus referencias. Los registros originales seguirán disponibles para consulta.</p></div></details>`;
      return `<div id="publication-preview"><div class="publication-preview-scroll">${summary}<section aria-labelledby="publication-changes-title"><div class="publication-list-heading"><h3 id="publication-changes-title">Qué va a cambiar</h3><div class="publication-disclosure-actions"><button class="text-button" type="button" data-preview-expand="true" aria-label="Expandir todo el detalle de publicación">Expandir todo</button><button class="text-button" type="button" data-preview-expand="false" aria-label="Contraer todo el detalle de publicación">Contraer todo</button></div></div>${operations}${evolution}</section>${trace}</div><div class="form-actions"><button class="secondary-button" type="button" data-back-to-review>Volver a revisión</button><button class="action-button" type="button" data-confirm-publish>Publicar conocimiento</button></div></div>`;
    }
    function openPublishPreview(){
      const documentItem=activeReviewDocument();if(!reviewCanEdit(documentItem))return;const plan=reviewPublicationPlan(documentItem);if(!plan.ready){showToast('La revisión aún no está cerrada','Revisa todas las decisiones antes de preparar la publicación.');return;}
      const conflicts=[...evolutionConflicts(documentItem,plan),...analysisRevisionConflicts(documentItem,plan)];if(conflicts.length){showToast('Hay decisiones incompatibles',conflicts.join(' '));return;}
      const header=`<div class="drawer-kicker">Preview de publicación</div><h2>Impacto en UX Intel</h2><p class="drawer-lead">Esto es lo que cambiará al publicar ${escapeHtml(documentItem.name)}. ${plan.revision?'La publicación anterior permanece intacta hasta confirmar estos cambios.':'Todavía no se ha incorporado ningún conocimiento.'}</p>`;
      const body=publicationPreviewBody(plan,documentItem);
      openDrawer(header,body);
    }
    async function setClusterReviewDecision(documentItem,clusterId,decision,status='pending'){
      const insight=reviewInsightsForDocument(documentItem).find(item=>item.id===clusterId);
      if(status==='approved'&&insight){const errors=insightDecisionErrors({...insight,decision,decisionDetails:insightDecisionDetails(insight)});if(errors.length){openInsightDecision(clusterId);showToast('Completa la decisión',errors.join(' '));return false;}}
      return saveAnalysisEdit(documentItem,clusterId,{decision,status});
    }
    // Read-only counts for the two review orientation modules, not classification.
    function reviewOrientation(clusters){
      const isQuestion=cluster=>cluster.kind==='question'||(!cluster.kind&&cluster.decision==='Crear pregunta abierta');
      const pending=clusters.filter(cluster=>!insightIsReviewed(cluster));
      const questions=clusters.filter(isQuestion).length,pendingQuestions=pending.filter(isQuestion).length;
      return {total:clusters.length,reviewed:clusters.length-pending.length,insights:clusters.length-questions,questions,pending,pendingInsights:pending.length-pendingQuestions,pendingQuestions};
    }
    function reviewOrientationSummary(orientation,knowledgeChanges){
      return `<div class="cluster-review-summary audit-stats">${[[orientation.reviewed,'Unidades revisadas','system-processing'],[orientation.questions,'Preguntas abiertas','system-pending'],[orientation.pending.length,'Decisiones pendientes',orientation.pending.length?'system-pending':'system-success'],[knowledgeChanges,'Cambios en conocimiento',knowledgeChanges?'system-processing':'neutral']].map(([count,label,tone])=>`<div class="cluster-metric audit-stat"><strong>${count}</strong><span class="audit-stat-label ${tone}">${label}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>`;
    }
    function reviewUnitTypeLabel(cluster){
      if(cluster.kind==='question'||(!cluster.kind&&cluster.decision==='Crear pregunta abierta'))return 'Pregunta abierta';
      if(cluster.decision==='Recomendación de sistema')return 'Recomendación';
      if(['Mantener solo como evidencia','Solo contexto / evidencia documental','Usar como evidencia'].includes(cluster.decision))return 'Evidencia / contexto';
      return 'Insight';
    }
    function reviewDecisionFocus(orientation,editable){
      // Once finished, the existing publication/completion module takes over.
      if(!editable||!orientation.pending.length)return '';
      return `<section class="decision-focus" aria-labelledby="review-decision-focus-title"><div class="decision-focus-copy"><h2 id="review-decision-focus-title">Necesita tu decisión</h2><p class="decision-focus-counts"><span><strong>${orientation.pendingInsights}</strong> ${orientation.pendingInsights===1?'insight pendiente':'insights pendientes'}</span><span><strong>${orientation.pendingQuestions}</strong> ${orientation.pendingQuestions===1?'pregunta abierta':'preguntas abiertas'}</span><span><strong>${orientation.pending.length}</strong> ${orientation.pending.length===1?'decisión por revisar':'decisiones por revisar'}</span></p></div><a class="secondary-button" data-start-review href="#cluster-${escapeHtml(orientation.pending[0].id)}">Empezar revisión</a></section>`;
    }
    function startPendingReview(){
      const item=activeReviewDocument();if(!item)return;
      const first=reviewOrientation(reviewInsightsForDocument(item)).pending[0];if(!first)return;
      const card=document.getElementById(`cluster-${first.id}`);if(!card)return;
card.open=true;
      const summary=card.querySelector('summary');
      summary?.focus({preventScroll:true});
      summary?.scrollIntoView({block:'center',behavior:'auto'});
    }
    function renderReview(){
      const openedInsights=[...(document.querySelectorAll?.('#review-pane-insights .cluster-card[id][open]')||[])].map(node=>node.id);
      const documentItem=activeReviewDocument();
      activeReviewDocumentId=documentItem?.id||PILOT_SOURCE_ID;
      const summary=document.getElementById('review-summary'),metrics=document.getElementById('review-context-metrics'),workspace=document.getElementById('review-workspace'),actions=document.getElementById('review-actions');
      if(!documentItem){summary.innerHTML='';metrics.innerHTML='';actions.innerHTML='';workspace.innerHTML='<div class="analysis-empty"><h2>No hay documento seleccionado</h2><p>Selecciona una fuente en Documentos para consultar su ficha, conocimiento asociado y trazabilidad.</p></div>';return;}
      const analysis=documentItem.analysis,editable=reviewCanEdit(documentItem);
      document.getElementById('review-title').textContent=documentItem.name;
      document.querySelector('#review .eyebrow').textContent='Ficha del documento';
      if(hasRecoveredDocumentAnalysis(documentItem)){
        document.querySelector('#review .eyebrow').textContent='Análisis recuperado';
      }
      const findings=analysis?.findings||[],metadata=analysis?.metadata||{};
      const devices=metadata.devices?.length?metadata.devices:[...new Set(findings.map(item=>item.device).filter(Boolean))];
      const markets=metadata.markets?.length?metadata.markets:[...new Set(findings.map(item=>item.market).filter(Boolean))];
      summary.innerHTML=`<span class="review-context-source"><span>${escapeHtml(documentItem.originalName||documentItem.fileMeta?.name||'No disponible')}</span><span class="review-context-separator" aria-hidden="true">·</span><span>${escapeHtml(documentDate(documentItem))}</span></span><span class="review-context-status"><span class="review-context-separator" aria-hidden="true">·</span><span class="status-badge ${documentStatusClass(documentItem.status)}">${escapeHtml(documentStatusLabel(documentItem.status))}</span></span>`;
      if(!hasDocumentAnalysis(documentItem)||!documentHydrationComplete){
        metrics.innerHTML='';
        actions.innerHTML='';
        workspace.innerHTML=`<div class="analysis-empty"><h2>${documentItem.status==='Analizando'?'Análisis en curso':'Análisis no disponible'}</h2><p>${!documentHydrationComplete?'Recuperando el análisis guardado…':documentItem.status==='Analizando'?'La revisión estará disponible cuando termine el análisis.':'Este documento no tiene un análisis recuperado. Vuelve a su ficha para consultar su estado.'}</p><button class="secondary-button" type="button" data-open-source-detail="${documentItem.id}">Ver documento</button></div>`;
        return;
      }
      const interpretation=documentInterpretation(documentItem),relations=analysis.relations||[],clusters=reviewInsightsForDocument(documentItem,interpretation),decisionCounts=reviewDecisionCounts(clusters),publicationPlan=reviewPublicationPlan(documentItem);
      const approved=findings.filter(item=>item.status==='approved').length,pending=findings.filter(item=>item.status==='pending').length;
      const routineClusters=clusters.filter(cluster=>!cluster.needsDecision&&!insightIsReviewed(cluster));
      actions.innerHTML=!editable||publicationPlan.ready||!routineClusters.length?'':`<button class="secondary-button" type="button" data-approve-routine-clusters>Confirmar ${routineClusters.length} ${routineClusters.length===1?'propuesta rutinaria':'propuestas rutinarias'}</button>`;
      const auditView=buildDocumentAuditView(documentItem,interpretation,clusters);
      const needsDecision=clusters.filter(cluster=>cluster.needsDecision&&!insightIsReviewed(cluster));
      const orientation=reviewOrientation(clusters),decisionFocus=reviewDecisionFocus(orientation,editable);
      const publicationState=analysisEditStatusMarkup(documentItem,publicationPlan)+(analysis.publication?.status==='published'?`<section class="publish-ready"><div class="publish-ready-copy"><span class="publish-ready-label">Conocimiento publicado</span><h2>${clusters.length} unidades de conocimiento incorporadas a UX Intel.</h2><p>${escapeHtml(publicationSummaryText(analysis.publication.counts||{}))}</p><p>Publicado · ${escapeHtml(formatDateEs(analysis.publication.publishedAt))}</p></div><button class="secondary-button" type="button" data-view-published-knowledge>Ver conocimiento publicado</button></section>`:editable&&publicationPlan.ready?`<section class="publish-ready"><div class="publish-ready-copy"><span class="publish-ready-label">Revisión de unidades completada</span><h2>${clusters.length} unidades revisadas</h2><p>El documento sigue pendiente de publicación.</p></div><button class="action-button" type="button" data-open-publish-preview>Ver impacto y publicar</button></section>`:'');
      const clusterCards=clusters.map(cluster=>{
        const device=cluster.devices.join(', ')||'Dispositivo no especificado',status=insightReviewLabel(cluster);
        const findingsList=cluster.findings.map((finding,index)=>{
          const number=finding.derivedFromTable?`T${String(index+1).padStart(2,'0')}`:String(finding.originalIndex||index+1).padStart(2,'0'),structure=finding.structure?`<div class="cluster-finding-structure"><div><small>Impacto potencial</small><span>${escapeHtml(finding.structure.impacto)}</span></div><div><small>Oportunidad</small><span>${escapeHtml(finding.structure.oportunidad)}</span></div><div><small>Origen</small><span>Tabla · Área ${escapeHtml(finding.structure.area)} · ${finding.originalFindingIds?.length||0} registros originales</span></div></div>`:'';
          return `<div class="cluster-finding"><span class="cluster-finding-index">${number}</span><div class="cluster-finding-copy"><strong>${escapeHtml(finding.text)}</strong><span>Pág. ${finding.source?.page||'—'} · ${escapeHtml(finding.source?.section||'Sin sección')} · ${escapeHtml(pathOf(finding))}</span>${structure}</div><span class="status-badge ${confidenceClass(finding.confidence)}">${escapeHtml(humanKnowledgeLabel(finding.type))}</span></div>`;
        }).join('');
        const relationCopy=cluster.learning?`Relacionado con ${escapeHtml(cluster.learning.id)}.`:cluster.id==='design-system'?'Recomendación de sistema sin relación forzada con un learning.':'No se ha encontrado una coincidencia consolidada suficientemente clara.';
        const relationId=cluster.decisionDetails?.targetId||cluster.learningId||'';
        const context=pathOf(cluster.findings[0]||cluster);
        return `<details class="cluster-card" id="cluster-${cluster.id}"><summary><span class="cluster-title cluster-primary"><span class="cluster-unit-label">${escapeHtml(reviewUnitTypeLabel(cluster))}</span><strong>${insightReadCopy(cluster.displayTitle)}</strong><span class="cluster-knowledge-meta">${relationId?`<span>Relacionado con <strong>${escapeHtml(relationId)}</strong></span>`:''}<span>${escapeHtml(context)} · ${escapeHtml(device)}</span><span>${cluster.findings.length} ${cluster.findings.length===1?'evidencia':'evidencias'} · páginas ${formatPageRanges(cluster.pages)}</span></span></span><span class="insight-decision"><strong>${escapeHtml(insightDecisionLabel(cluster.decision))}</strong><span class="${insightIsReviewed(cluster)?'reviewed':''}">${status}</span></span><span class="cluster-disclosure"><span class="insight-disclosure-closed">Ver detalle</span><span class="insight-disclosure-open">Ocultar detalle</span></span></summary><div class="cluster-body">${insightManagementMarkup(cluster,documentItem,findingsList)}</div></details>`;
      }).join('');
      const discardedByReason=(analysis.discarded||[]).reduce((acc,item)=>{acc[item.reason]=(acc[item.reason]||0)+1;return acc;},{});
      const typeBreakdown=Object.entries(interpretation.typeCounts||{}).map(([type,count])=>`<li><strong>${escapeHtml(humanKnowledgeLabel(type))}:</strong> ${count}</li>`).join('');
